
===== ARCHITECTURE.md =====
# PanelSnap: how the app fits together

A SwiftUI iPhone/iPad comic reader (iOS 17+) that snaps the view from panel to panel. Project is generated by XcodeGen
from `project.yml` (`project.lite.yml` is the same without the RAR/7z packages). No Xcode project is committed.

## Where to look
| Area | Files |
| --- | --- |
| App entry, settings | `App/PanelSnapApp.swift`, `Services/AppSettings.swift` |
| Library (shelves, covers, progress) | `Services/LibraryStore.swift`, `Views/LibraryView.swift`, `Models/ComicModels.swift` |
| Opening comics | `Services/ComicSource.swift` (protocol + image decoding), `Zip/Rar/SevenZip/PDFComicSource.swift` |
| Panel detection | `Services/PanelDetector.swift` → `MLPanelDetector.swift` (Core ML YOLO) + `OrderModel.swift` (reading order); results cached by `PanelCache.swift` |
| **Reader** | `Views/ReaderView.swift`, `Views/ZoomLayer.swift`, `Views/PanelCanvas.swift`, `Services/ReaderModel.swift` |
| Companion (who's who) | `Services/Companion/*`, `Views/CompanionView.swift` |
| Browser / Discover / downloads | `Views/BrowserView.swift`, `Services/BrowserModel.swift`, `BrowserShield.swift`, `Views/DiscoverView.swift`, `Services/DiscoverModel.swift`, `SegmentedDownloader.swift` |

## The reader
- `ReaderModel` (main actor) owns the current page image, its panel rectangles (0...1 page coordinates) and `panelIndex`
  (`-1` = whole page). It loads pages off the main thread (decoded once, longest side 2600px), keeps the current page
  and its two neighbours in memory, and prefetches neighbours (de-duplicated, scanning for panels only when snapping is on).
- `PanelCanvas` draws the page and "flies" a camera (scale + offset, spring animation) to the focused panel.
  `PanelCanvas.camera` / `PanelCanvas.panel(at:)` are the pure geometry (also used for tap hit-testing).
- `ZoomState` (in `ZoomLayer.swift`) holds the manual zoom/offset on top of the camera. It is a separate observable so
  zooming redraws only the page. Always change it with `set(zoom:offset:)` (one redraw).

## Reader gestures (who handles what)
| Gesture | Handler |
| --- | --- |
| Tap edges / middle, double-tap zoom | SwiftUI `SpatialTapGesture` in `ReaderView.handleTap` (edges are instant; the middle waits 300 ms for a possible second tap) |
| Swipe left/right (page), up (Companion), down (menu) | SwiftUI `DragGesture` in `ReaderView` → `handleDrag` |
| Pinch (zoom around the fingers; also hands a focused panel over to free zoom) | UIKit `UIPinchGestureRecognizer` on the window, `ZoomGestures.Coordinator.pinched` |
| One-finger pan when zoomed, or nudging a snapped panel | UIKit `UIPanGestureRecognizer` on the window, `Coordinator.panned` / `nudge` |
| Momentum + rubber-band after a drag | `CADisplayLink` in the coordinator (`startGlide` / `glideStep`); moves the real offset so a touch can catch it |
| Drag past the page edge while zoomed → next/previous page | `Coordinator.panned(.ended)` → `onEdgeSwipe` |

The window-level recognisers run alongside SwiftUI's; `ZoomState.lastPinch` keeps the SwiftUI swipe from also firing after
a pinch or nudge. Merging swipe handling into the UIKit pan is the planned next cleanup.

## CI (what Claude without a terminal can read)
- `Build IPA` (`build.yml`): unsigned release IPA + GitHub Release.
- `Check` (`check.yml`): compiles every branch for the iOS Simulator and pushes a report and plain-text source bundles to the
  **`ci-report`** branch: `<branch-name>/status.md` (result, errors, warnings), `build-*.log`, `source/reader.txt`, `source/all.txt`.

===== README.md =====
# PanelSnap

A clean, iOS-style comic reader that **snaps from panel to panel** so comics are easy to read on a phone, with a built-in **Companion** that looks up who's who, the series, creators and story arc for whatever you're reading.

Built for iPhone (including the foldable "Duo": on the wide inner screen the Companion docks beside the comic).

## Get the app on your iPhone (no computer needed)

1. Open this repo on GitHub → **Actions** tab → **Build IPA** → **Run workflow** → green **Run workflow** button.
2. Wait ~6–10 minutes until the run shows a green check.
3. Go to the repo's **Releases** (right side of the main page), open the newest one, and tap **PanelSnap.ipa**.
4. Open the downloaded IPA with **FlareStore** (or your sideloader) to sign and install it.

Shortcut link that always points at the newest build:
`https://github.com/jakebway/PanelSnap/releases/latest/download/PanelSnap.ipa`

> If a build ever fails, open the failed run → the summary at the top shows the first errors. Copy and paste them to Claude.
> If only the optional RAR/7z libraries fail, the workflow automatically builds a **lite** version (CBZ/PDF/EPUB) so you still get an IPA.

## Using it

- **+** adds comics (CBZ, CBR, CB7, PDF, EPUB). Wrong file extension? It checks the real file type.
- **Tap the right/left side** of the screen: next / previous panel. **Swipe** works too. **Tap the middle** to show or hide the controls.
- **Pinch in** to see the whole page, **pinch out** to jump back into the panels.
- **Swipe up** (or tap ✨) to open the **Companion**.
- Manga: long-press a comic in the library → *Read right-to-left*. (Files tagged as manga in ComicInfo.xml switch automatically.)

## Organising your library

- **Shelves**: the chips under the top band. Tap `+` to make one; long-press a chip to rename or delete it (comics stay in your library).
- **Sort**: Recently read, Newest added, Title A–Z, or My order.
- **Organize**: select several comics, then move them to a shelf, mark read/unread or delete. Drag a comic onto another to set My order.
- Long-press any comic to rename it, move it to a shelf, mark it read, flip manga direction or delete it.

## Companion: where the info comes from

| Source | Needs a key? | Gives you |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Built-in guide (`Resources/Characters.json`, works offline) | no | ~1,700 Marvel/DC characters: real name, aliases, first appearance, teams, family. From the Superhero API (MIT) and FiveThirtyEight's comic-characters data (CC BY 4.0). Rebuild with `Scripts/build_glossary.py` |
| The comic file itself (`ComicInfo.xml`) | no | series, issue, summary, creators, characters, teams, arc |
| Grand Comics Database | no | credits, cast and synopses for many issues (CC BY-SA 4.0) |
| Marvel / DC fan wikis | no | issue synopsis (behind a spoiler shield), creators, who appears |
| Wikipedia, Open Library | no | series background, "who is this character?" |
| AniList | no | manga info and characters |

Nothing in the app needs an account or API key.

## How panel snapping works

Everything runs on-device. Panels are found by a neural network and put in reading order; a page with no panels is shown whole. Results are cached per comic. See `PanelSnap/Services/MLPanelDetector.swift`.

## Project layout

- `project.yml` – XcodeGen spec (the Xcode project is generated in CI)
- `project.lite.yml` – same, without RAR/7z (automatic fallback)
- `Scripts/build-ipa.sh` – builds the unsigned IPA
- `.github/workflows/build.yml` – the one-tap workflow

## Browser and ad blocking

The **+** menu has a **Browser**: a small in-app web browser with ads, trackers and pop-ups blocked. Any comic file
you download in it (CBZ, CBR, CB7, PDF, EPUB or a zip/rar/7z of pages) is added to your library automatically.
The filter lists are built from [EasyList](https://github.com/easylist/easylist) (GPLv3 / CC BY-SA 3.0) by
`Scripts/build_adblock.py` into WebKit content-blocker JSON (`PanelSnap/Resources/shield-*.json`). Re-run that
script to refresh them.

## Reading mode

Settings › Reading › *Regular pages* turns off panel detection and zooming and gives a plain page viewer.

## Panel detection

Panels are found by a small neural network and put in reading order by a second small model, both from the [Inkwell](https://huggingface.co/cedarrapidsboy/inkwell-panel-models) project: a YOLOv8-nano panel detector trained on about 3,800 annotated comic pages (AGPL-3.0) and a pairwise reading-order model (MIT). The detector runs on the phone with Apple's Core ML; the order model's decision trees run in the app. There is no rule-based backup for finding panels.

name: PanelSnap
options:
  bundleIdPrefix: com.jakebway
  deploymentTarget:
    iOS: '17.0'
  createIntermediateGroups: true
  generateEmptyDirectories: true
packages:
  ZIPFoundation:
    url: https://github.com/weichsel/ZIPFoundation
    exactVersion: 0.9.20
  Unrar:
    url: https://github.com/mtgto/Unrar.swift
    exactVersion: 0.5.4
  SevenZip:
    url: https://github.com/mtgto/SevenZip.swift
    exactVersion: 0.5.0

  PanelSnap:
    type: application
    platform: iOS
    sources:
    - path: PanelSnap
      excludes:
      - Info.plist
    dependencies:
    - package: ZIPFoundation
      product: ZIPFoundation
    - package: Unrar
      product: Unrar
    - package: SevenZip
      product: SevenZip
    settings:
      base:
        PRODUCT_NAME: PanelSnap
        PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER: com.jakebway.panelsnap
        MARKETING_VERSION: '1.0'
        CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION: '1'

===== .github/workflows/build.yml =====
name: Build IPA

# HOW TO USE (from your phone):
#   GitHub app/website -> this repo -> Actions -> "Build IPA" -> Run workflow.
#   When it finishes (about 6-10 min) open "Releases" and tap PanelSnap.ipa.
on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      build_name:
        description: "Name for this build (optional, e.g. 'test 2')"
        required: false
        default: ""
      publish:
        description: "Publish to Releases so you can download the IPA right on your iPhone"
        type: boolean
        default: true
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths-ignore:
      - "**.md"

permissions:
  contents: write

concurrency:
  group: build-ipa
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build:
    runs-on: macos-26
    timeout-minutes: 45
    steps:
      - name: Check out the code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install XcodeGen
        run: brew install xcodegen

      - name: Build the app
        id: build
        run: bash Scripts/build-ipa.sh

      - name: Check the panel detector
        run: |
          mkdir -p /tmp/pd
          xcrun coremlcompiler compile PanelSnap/Resources/InkwellPanels.mlmodel /tmp/pd
          cp PanelSnap/Resources/InkwellOrder.json /tmp/pd/
          swiftc -O -o /tmp/pd/check PanelSnap/Services/MLPanelDetector.swift PanelSnap/Services/OrderModel.swift Scripts/PanelCheck/*.swift
          /tmp/pd/check Tests/test-page.png Tests/test-page-expected.json

      - name: Save the IPA (Actions artifact)
        if: success()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: PanelSnap-IPA
          path: PanelSnap.ipa
          if-no-files-found: error

      - name: Save the build log
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: build-log
          path: build-*.log
          if-no-files-found: ignore

      - name: Publish to Releases
        if: success() && (github.event_name == 'push' || inputs.publish == true)
        env:
          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          BUILD_NAME: ${{ inputs.build_name }}
          MODE: ${{ steps.build.outputs.mode }}
        run: |
          TAG="build-${{ github.run_number }}"
          TITLE="PanelSnap build ${{ github.run_number }}"
          if [ -n "$BUILD_NAME" ]; then TITLE="$TITLE - $BUILD_NAME"; fi
          NOTES="Unsigned IPA. Open it with FlareStore (or any sideloader) to sign and install."
          if [ "$MODE" = "lite" ]; then
            NOTES="$NOTES  NOTE: this is the LITE build (CBZ/PDF/EPUB only; RAR/7z library failed to compile)."
          fi
          gh release delete "$TAG" --yes --cleanup-tag >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
          ok=0
          for attempt in 1 2 3; do
            if gh release create "$TAG" PanelSnap.ipa \
                 --title "$TITLE" --notes "$NOTES" --target "$GITHUB_SHA" --latest; then ok=1; break; fi
            echo "Release attempt $attempt failed, retrying..."
            gh release delete "$TAG" --yes --cleanup-tag >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
            sleep 10
          done
          [ "$ok" = 1 ] || { echo "Could not publish the release" >&2; exit 1; }
          # keep ONLY the newest release: delete every other release and its tag
          gh release list --limit 200 --json tagName --jq '.[].tagName' | while read -r old; do
            if [ "$old" != "$TAG" ]; then
              echo "Removing old release $old"
              gh release delete "$old" --yes --cleanup-tag || true
            fi
          done
          {
            echo "## PanelSnap is ready"
            echo ""
            echo "Download on your iPhone: https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/releases/tag/$TAG"
            echo ""
            echo "Always-newest direct link: https://github.com/${{ github.repository }}/releases/latest/download/PanelSnap.ipa"
            echo ""
            echo "Build type: **$MODE**"
          } >> "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"

===== .github/workflows/check.yml =====
name: Check (simulator build + report)

# Compiles every pushed branch for the iOS Simulator and publishes a short report and the app's source as plain text
# to the "ci-report" branch, so regular Claude (chat, no terminal) can read the result straight from GitHub:
#   https://github.com/<repo>/tree/ci-report/<branch-name>
# Run it by hand from Actions -> "Check" -> Run workflow, or just push to a branch.
on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches-ignore: [ci-report]

permissions:
  contents: write

concurrency:
  group: check-${{ github.head_ref || github.ref_name }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  check:
    runs-on: macos-26
    timeout-minutes: 40
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install XcodeGen
        run: brew install xcodegen

      - name: Source bundle
        run: bash Scripts/make-source-bundle.sh

      # main is already compiled by "Build IPA", so only the source bundle is refreshed there.
      - name: Build for the simulator
        id: sim
        if: github.ref_name != 'main'
        run: bash Scripts/ci-check.sh

      - name: Publish the report to the ci-report branch
        if: always()
        env:
          TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          REPO: ${{ github.repository }}
        run: |
          set -eu
          BRANCH="${GITHUB_HEAD_REF:-$GITHUB_REF_NAME}"
          SLUG="$(echo "$BRANCH" | tr '/' '-')"
          URL="https://x-access-token:${TOKEN}@github.com/${REPO}.git"
          rm -rf /tmp/pub
          for attempt in 1 2 3 4; do
            if git clone --quiet --depth 1 --branch ci-report "$URL" /tmp/pub 2>/dev/null; then :; else
              mkdir -p /tmp/pub && cd /tmp/pub && git init -q -b ci-report && git remote add origin "$URL" && cd -
            fi
            cd /tmp/pub
            git config user.name "panelsnap-ci"
            git config user.email "ci@users.noreply.github.com"
            rm -rf "$SLUG"
            mkdir -p "$SLUG"
            cp -R "$GITHUB_WORKSPACE"/report/. "$SLUG"/
            {
              echo "# PanelSnap CI reports"
              echo ""
              echo "One folder per branch. Inside: \`status.md\` (build result, errors, warnings), \`build-*.log\` (log tail),"
              echo "and \`source/reader.txt\` + \`source/all.txt\` (the app's source as plain text)."
              echo ""
              for d in */; do echo "- \`${d%/}\`: $(head -1 "$d/status.md" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/^# //')"; done
            } > README.md
            git add -A
            git commit -q -m "Report for $BRANCH ($GITHUB_SHA)" || true
            if git push -q origin ci-report; then exit 0; fi
            echo "push raced, retrying"; cd /; rm -rf /tmp/pub; sleep $((attempt * 3))
          done
          exit 1

===== PanelSnap/App/PanelSnapApp.swift =====
import SwiftUI

@main
struct PanelSnapApp: App {
    @StateObject private var library = LibraryStore()
    @StateObject private var settings = AppSettings()

    init() {
        // Make the "PanelSnap" folder show up in Files > On My iPhone, with a Comics folder ready to drop files in.
        let docs = Paths.documents
        _ = Paths.comics
        Console.log("PanelSnap launched")
        MLPanelDetector.warmUp()
        // An older version left a second note beside this one; one is enough.
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: docs.appendingPathComponent("Put your comics in the Comics folder.txt"))
    }

    var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup {
            LibraryView()
                .environmentObject(library)
                .environmentObject(settings)
                .tint(Theme.accent)
                .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
                .background(Theme.bg.ignoresSafeArea())
                .onOpenURL { url in
                    Task { await library.importFiles([url]) }
                }
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Models/ComicModels.swift =====
import Foundation

enum ComicFormat: String, Codable, Hashable {
    case cbz, cbr, cb7, pdf, epub, other

    var label: String {
        switch self {
        case .cbz: return "CBZ"
        case .cbr: return "CBR"
        case .cb7: return "CB7"
        case .pdf: return "PDF"
        case .epub: return "EPUB"
        case .other: return "COMIC"
        }
    }

    static func from(fileExtension ext: String) -> ComicFormat {
        switch ext.lowercased() {
        case "cbz", "zip": return .cbz
        case "cbr", "rar": return .cbr
        case "cb7", "7z": return .cb7
        case "pdf": return .pdf
        case "epub": return .epub
        default: return .other
        }
    }
}

/// One comic in the library.
struct ComicEntry: Identifiable, Codable, Hashable {
    var id: UUID
    var fileName: String          // name of the stored copy inside the Comics folder
    var originalName: String
    var title: String
    var series: String?
    var issue: String?
    var format: ComicFormat
    var pageCount: Int
    var addedAt: Date
    var lastOpenedAt: Date?
    var lastPage: Int
    var lastPanel: Int            // -1 = whole-page view
    var finished: Bool
    var isManga: Bool
    var shelf: String? = nil      // name of the shelf this comic sits on, if any

    var progress: Double {
        if finished { return 1 }
        if pageCount <= 1 { return 0 }
        return min(1, max(0, Double(lastPage) / Double(pageCount - 1)))
    }

    var hasStarted: Bool { return lastPage > 0 || lastPanel > 0 || finished }
}

/// What we learn about a comic from its own files + file name (works offline).
struct ComicIdentity: Codable, Hashable {
    var series: String?
    var issue: String?
    var title: String?
    var volume: String?
    var year: Int?
    var publisher: String?
    var imprint: String?
    var summary: String?
    var writers: [String] = []
    var pencillers: [String] = []
    var inkers: [String] = []
    var colorists: [String] = []
    var letterers: [String] = []
    var coverArtists: [String] = []
    var editors: [String] = []
    var genres: [String] = []
    var characters: [String] = []
    var teams: [String] = []
    var locations: [String] = []
    var storyArc: String?
    var seriesGroup: String?
    var web: String?
    var isManga: Bool = false
    var rtl: Bool = false
    var ageRating: String?
    var languageISO: String?

    var displayTitle: String {
        var s = series ?? title ?? "Untitled"
        if let n = issue, !n.isEmpty { s += " #" + n }
        return s
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/AppSettings.swift =====
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// Simple, UserDefaults-backed app settings.
final class AppSettings: ObservableObject {

    private let defaults = UserDefaults.standard

    @Published var snapToPanels: Bool { didSet { defaults.set(snapToPanels, forKey: "snapToPanels") } }
    @Published var showFullPageFirst: Bool { didSet { defaults.set(showFullPageFirst, forKey: "wholePageFirst") } }
    @Published var onlineLookup: Bool { didSet { defaults.set(onlineLookup, forKey: "onlineLookup") } }
    @Published var showDiscover: Bool { didSet { defaults.set(showDiscover, forKey: "showDiscover") } }
    @Published var spoilerShield: Bool { didSet { defaults.set(spoilerShield, forKey: "spoilerShield") } }
    @Published var librarySort: LibrarySort { didSet { defaults.set(librarySort.rawValue, forKey: "librarySort") } }
    /// Theme colours, stored as hex ("FFD11A"). Changing them updates `Theme` straight away.
    @Published var accentHex: String { didSet { defaults.set(accentHex, forKey: "accentHex"); Theme.apply(accent: accentHex, highlight: highlightHex) } }
    @Published var highlightHex: String { didSet { defaults.set(highlightHex, forKey: "highlightHex"); Theme.apply(accent: accentHex, highlight: highlightHex) } }

    var themeKey: String { return accentHex + highlightHex }

    init() {
        let d = UserDefaults.standard
        d.register(defaults: [
            "snapToPanels": true,
            "wholePageFirst": true,
            "onlineLookup": true,
            "spoilerShield": true,
            "showDiscover": true
        ])
        snapToPanels = d.bool(forKey: "snapToPanels")
        showFullPageFirst = d.bool(forKey: "wholePageFirst")
        onlineLookup = d.bool(forKey: "onlineLookup")
        spoilerShield = d.bool(forKey: "spoilerShield")
        showDiscover = d.bool(forKey: "showDiscover")
        librarySort = LibrarySort(rawValue: d.string(forKey: "librarySort") ?? "") ?? .recent
        accentHex = d.string(forKey: "accentHex") ?? ThemePreset.comic.accent
        highlightHex = d.string(forKey: "highlightHex") ?? ThemePreset.comic.highlight
        Theme.apply(accent: accentHex, highlight: highlightHex)
    }
}

/// Ready-made colour pairs: a main colour (buttons, highlights) and a second colour (title bar, glow, progress).
struct ThemePreset: Identifiable {
    let name: String
    let accent: String
    let highlight: String
    var id: String { return name }

    static let comic = ThemePreset(name: "Comic", accent: "FFD11A", highlight: "ED1C24")
    static let all: [ThemePreset] = [
        comic,
        ThemePreset(name: "Sakura", accent: "FF7FB6", highlight: "8E44FF"),
        ThemePreset(name: "Ocean", accent: "3DD6FF", highlight: "2F6BFF"),
        ThemePreset(name: "Toxic", accent: "8CFF4F", highlight: "7A2CFF"),
        ThemePreset(name: "Sunset", accent: "FF9F1C", highlight: "E6007A"),
        ThemePreset(name: "Mono", accent: "F2F2F2", highlight: "7A7A7A")
    ]
}

extension Color {
    init(hex: String) {
        var v: UInt64 = 0
        Scanner(string: hex.trimmingCharacters(in: CharacterSet(charactersIn: "# "))).scanHexInt64(&v)
        self.init(red: Double((v >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255, green: Double((v >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255, blue: Double(v & 0xFF) / 255)
    }

    var hexString: String {
        var r: CGFloat = 0, g: CGFloat = 0, b: CGFloat = 0, a: CGFloat = 0
        UIColor(self).getRed(&r, green: &g, blue: &b, alpha: &a)
        return String(format: "%02X%02X%02X", Int((r * 255).rounded()), Int((g * 255).rounded()), Int((b * 255).rounded()))
    }

    /// Black or white, whichever reads better on top of this colour.
    var readableOn: Color {
        var r: CGFloat = 0, g: CGFloat = 0, b: CGFloat = 0, a: CGFloat = 0
        UIColor(self).getRed(&r, green: &g, blue: &b, alpha: &a)
        let luminance = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b
        return luminance > 0.58 ? Color.black : Color.white
    }
}

/// How the library grid is ordered.
enum LibrarySort: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
    case recent, added, title, custom
    var id: String { return rawValue }
    var label: String {
        switch self {
        case .recent: return "Recently Read"
        case .added: return "Recently Added"
        case .title: return "Title A–Z"
        case .custom: return "My Order"
        }
    }
    var icon: String {
        switch self {
        case .recent: return "clock"
        case .added: return "plus.circle"
        case .title: return "textformat.abc"
        case .custom: return "hand.draw"
        }
    }
}

enum Theme {
    /// Main colour (comic yellow by default): confirm buttons, progress and highlights. Changeable in Settings.
    static var accent = Color(hex: ThemePreset.comic.accent)
    /// Text / icons that sit on top of the main colour.
    static var onAccent = Color.black

    static func apply(accent a: String, highlight h: String) {
        accent = Color(hex: a)
        onAccent = accent.readableOn
        red = Color(hex: h)
    }
    /// Manga / comic red: the second brand colour, used for headers, shadows and glow.
    static var red = Color(hex: ThemePreset.comic.highlight)
    static let bg = Color.black                    // true OLED black
    static let card = Color(white: 0.10)
    static let card2 = Color(white: 0.16)
    static let stroke = Color(white: 0.22)
    static let secondary = Color(white: 0.62)
    /// The iPhone Duo's corner camera + status widget: keep controls out of this top-right box (points).
    static let cameraWidth: CGFloat = 112
    static let cameraHeight: CGFloat = 164
}

enum Paths {
    static func ensure(_ url: URL) -> URL {
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: url, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        return url
    }

    static var documents: URL {
        return FileManager.default.urls(for: .documentDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
    }
    static var support: URL {
        let base = FileManager.default.urls(for: .applicationSupportDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
        return ensure(base.appendingPathComponent("PanelSnap", isDirectory: true))
    }
    static var caches: URL {
        let base = FileManager.default.urls(for: .cachesDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
        return ensure(base.appendingPathComponent("PanelSnap", isDirectory: true))
    }
    static var comics: URL {
        // A small note next to the Comics folder: it keeps the app's folder showing up in Files › On My iPhone.
        let note = documents.appendingPathComponent("Put comics in the Comics folder.txt")
        if !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: note.path) {
            try? "Drop CBZ, CBR, CB7, PDF or EPUB files into the Comics folder. PanelSnap adds them to your library by itself."
                .write(to: note, atomically: true, encoding: .utf8)
        }
        return ensure(documents.appendingPathComponent("Comics", isDirectory: true))
    }

    /// Opens the Files app on this app's folder.
    static func showInFiles() {
        _ = comics
        if let url = URL(string: "shareddocuments://" + documents.path) { UIApplication.shared.open(url) }
    }
    static var covers: URL { return ensure(support.appendingPathComponent("Covers", isDirectory: true)) }
    static var panelCache: URL { return ensure(support.appendingPathComponent("Panels", isDirectory: true)) }
    static var companionCache: URL { return ensure(caches.appendingPathComponent("Companion", isDirectory: true)) }
    static var libraryFile: URL { return support.appendingPathComponent("library.json") }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/BrowserModel.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import WebKit

struct BrowserDownload: Identifiable {
    enum State { case downloading, adding, done(String), failed(String) }
    var id = UUID()
    var name: String
    var progress: Double = 0
    var bytes: Int64 = 0
    var total: Int64 = 0
    var speed: Double = 0              // bytes per second
    var fast = false                   // using several connections at once
    var state: State = .downloading
    var retryURL: URL? = nil
}

struct Bookmark: Identifiable, Codable, Hashable {
    var id = UUID()
    var title: String
    var url: String
}

struct BrowserNotice: Identifiable {
    let id = UUID()
    var text: String
    var openURL: URL? = nil
}

/// The in-app browser. Ads are blocked by `BrowserShield`, pop-ups never open on their own, and any comic file
/// that gets downloaded goes straight into the library.
@MainActor
final class BrowserModel: NSObject, ObservableObject, WKNavigationDelegate, WKUIDelegate, WKDownloadDelegate {
    static let shared = BrowserModel()

    @Published var webView: WKWebView?
    @Published var hasPage = false
    @Published var addressText = ""
    @Published var canGoBack = false
    @Published var canGoForward = false
    @Published var isLoading = false
    @Published var progress: Double = 0
    @Published var downloads: [BrowserDownload] = []
    @Published var notice: BrowserNotice?
    @Published var blockedCount = 0
    @Published var bookmarks: [Bookmark] = {
        guard let d = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: "browserBookmarks"),
              let list = try? JSONDecoder().decode([Bookmark].self, from: d) else { return [] }
        return list
    }() {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(try? JSONEncoder().encode(bookmarks), forKey: "browserBookmarks") }
    }
    /// The page the browser opens on and the house button returns to. Empty means none is set.
    @Published var homeURL: String = UserDefaults.standard.string(forKey: "browserHome") ?? "" {
        didSet { UserDefaults.standard.set(homeURL, forKey: "browserHome") }
    }

    private weak var library: LibraryStore?
    private var observers: [NSKeyValueObservation] = []
    private var downloadIDs: [ObjectIdentifier: UUID] = [:]
    private var wkDownloads: [UUID: WKDownload] = [:]
    private var destinations: [UUID: URL] = [:]
    private var progressObservers: [UUID: NSKeyValueObservation] = [:]
    private var samples: [UUID: (time: Date, bytes: Int64)] = [:]
    private var fastDownloads: [UUID: SegmentedDownloader] = [:]
    private var noFast = Set<String>()                  // URLs that must use a single connection
    private(set) var isEditing = false

    private static let comicMIME: Set<String> = [
        "application/x-cbz", "application/x-cbr", "application/x-cb7", "application/vnd.comicbook+zip",
        "application/vnd.comicbook-rar", "application/epub+zip", "application/x-7z-compressed",
        "application/x-rar-compressed", "application/vnd.rar", "application/zip", "application/x-zip-compressed",
    ]
    private static let sniffExtensions: Set<String> = ["", "php", "aspx", "html", "htm", "bin", "download", "tmp", "file"]

    func attach(_ library: LibraryStore) { self.library = library }

    /// Set by Discover: a title to search for on the Home website, or a page to open, once the browser is showing.
    private var pendingSearch: String?
    private var pendingOpen: URL?

    func prepare() async {
        await BrowserShield.shared.prepare()
        if webView == nil {
            build()
            if let u = pendingOpen { pendingOpen = nil; webView?.load(URLRequest(url: u)) }
            else if !homeURL.isEmpty { go(homeURL) }
        }
    }

    /// Opens a page (the browser may not exist yet).
    func open(_ url: URL) {
        pendingSearch = nil
        if let wv = webView { wv.load(URLRequest(url: url)) } else { pendingOpen = url }
    }

    /// Goes to the Home website and types the title into its search box.
    func searchHome(_ query: String) {
        pendingOpen = nil
        guard !homeURL.isEmpty else {
            pendingSearch = nil
            show("Set a Home website first: tap ••• › Set Home Address…")
            return
        }
        pendingSearch = query
        if webView != nil { go(homeURL) }       // otherwise prepare() goes there when the browser appears
    }

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, didFinish navigation: WKNavigation!) {
        guard let query = pendingSearch else { return }
        let home = URL(string: homeURL)?.host?.replacingOccurrences(of: "www.", with: "")
        guard let host = webView.url?.host?.replacingOccurrences(of: "www.", with: ""), host == home else { return }
        pendingSearch = nil
        guard let data = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: [query]),
              let arg = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) else { return }
        // Finds the site's search box, types the title in and submits it, the way a person would.
        let script = """
        (function(q){
          var sel='input[type=search],input[name=s],input[name=q],input[name=search],input[name=query],input[name=keyword],input[name=keywords],input[placeholder*="earch" i],input[id*="earch" i],input[class*="earch" i]';
          var els=[].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(sel)).filter(function(e){return ['hidden','submit','checkbox','radio','button'].indexOf(e.type)<0;});
          var shown=els.filter(function(e){var r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&r.height>0;});
          var el=shown[0]||els[0]; if(!el) return 'none';
          var set=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLInputElement.prototype,'value').set; set.call(el,q);
          el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true})); el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));
          var f=el.form; if(f){ if(f.requestSubmit){f.requestSubmit();}else{f.submit();} return 'form'; }
          ['keydown','keypress','keyup'].forEach(function(t){el.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent(t,{key:'Enter',code:'Enter',keyCode:13,which:13,bubbles:true}));});
          return 'enter';
        })(\(arg)[0]);
        """
        webView.evaluateJavaScript(script) { [weak self] result, _ in
            if (result as? String) == "none" || result == nil {
                UIPasteboard.general.string = query
                self?.show("Couldn't find this site's search box. \"\(query)\" is copied, so you can paste it in.")
            }
        }
    }

    var currentIsBookmarked: Bool {
        guard let u = webView?.url?.absoluteString else { return false }
        return bookmarks.contains { $0.url == u }
    }

    /// Adds the current page, or removes it if it is already saved.
    func toggleBookmark() {
        guard let wv = webView, let url = wv.url, url.scheme?.hasPrefix("http") == true else { return }
        if let i = bookmarks.firstIndex(where: { $0.url == url.absoluteString }) {
            bookmarks.remove(at: i)
        } else {
            let t = (wv.title ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            bookmarks.insert(Bookmark(title: t.isEmpty ? (url.host ?? url.absoluteString) : t, url: url.absoluteString), at: 0)
        }
    }

    func retryDownload(_ id: UUID) {
        guard let url = downloads.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.retryURL else { return }
        downloads.removeAll { $0.id == id }
        webView?.load(URLRequest(url: url))
    }

    func goHome() { if !homeURL.isEmpty { go(homeURL) } }

    func setHome(_ input: String) {
        let text = input.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if text.isEmpty { homeURL = ""; return }
        homeURL = text.contains("://") ? text : "https://" + text
    }

    func setHomeToCurrentPage() {
        if let url = webView?.url, url.scheme?.hasPrefix("http") == true { homeURL = url.absoluteString }
    }

    private func build() {
        let cfg = WKWebViewConfiguration()
        for list in BrowserShield.shared.lists { cfg.userContentController.add(list) }
        cfg.preferences.javaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically = false      // no pop-ups without a real tap
        cfg.allowsInlineMediaPlayback = true
        cfg.mediaTypesRequiringUserActionForPlayback = .all                // no autoplaying video ads
        let wv = WKWebView(frame: .zero, configuration: cfg)
        wv.navigationDelegate = self
        wv.uiDelegate = self
        wv.allowsBackForwardNavigationGestures = true
        wv.isOpaque = false
        wv.backgroundColor = .black
        wv.scrollView.backgroundColor = .black
        observers = [
            wv.observe(\.estimatedProgress) { [weak self] w, _ in DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.progress = w.estimatedProgress } },
            wv.observe(\.isLoading) { [weak self] w, _ in DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.isLoading = w.isLoading } },
            wv.observe(\.canGoBack) { [weak self] w, _ in DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.canGoBack = w.canGoBack } },
            wv.observe(\.canGoForward) { [weak self] w, _ in DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.canGoForward = w.canGoForward } },
            wv.observe(\.url) { [weak self] w, _ in DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.urlChanged(w.url) } },
        ]
        webView = wv
    }

    private func urlChanged(_ url: URL?) {
        guard let url = url else { return }
        hasPage = true
        if !isEditing { addressText = Self.display(url) }
    }

    static func display(_ url: URL) -> String {
        let host = url.host ?? url.absoluteString
        return host.hasPrefix("www.") ? String(host.dropFirst(4)) : host
    }

    func beginEditing() {
        isEditing = true
        if let url = webView?.url { addressText = url.absoluteString }
    }

    func endEditing() {
        isEditing = false
        if let url = webView?.url { addressText = Self.display(url) } else { addressText = "" }
    }

    // MARK: Navigation

    func go(_ input: String) {
        let text = input.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !text.isEmpty, let wv = webView else { return }
        var url: URL?
        if text.contains("://"), let u = URL(string: text) {
            url = u
        } else if !text.contains(" "), text.contains("."), let u = URL(string: "https://" + text) {
            url = u
        }
        if url == nil {
            var c = URLComponents(string: "https://duckduckgo.com/")
            c?.queryItems = [URLQueryItem(name: "q", value: text)]
            url = c?.url
        }
        if let url = url { wv.load(URLRequest(url: url)) }
    }

    func reloadOrStop() {
        guard let wv = webView else { return }
        if wv.isLoading { wv.stopLoading() } else if wv.url != nil { wv.reload() }
    }

    private func show(_ text: String, open: URL? = nil) {
        let n = BrowserNotice(text: text, openURL: open)
        notice = n
        Task {
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 6_000_000_000)
            if notice?.id == n.id { notice = nil }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Which responses are comics

    private static func isComicURL(_ url: URL) -> Bool {
        let ext = url.pathExtension.lowercased()
        return ext != "" && LibraryStore.comicExtensions.contains(ext)
    }

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, decidePolicyFor navigationAction: WKNavigationAction,
                 decisionHandler: @escaping (WKNavigationActionPolicy) -> Void) {
        guard let url = navigationAction.request.url else { decisionHandler(.cancel); return }
        let scheme = url.scheme?.lowercased() ?? ""
        // App-store hops, intent:// links and other non-web jumps are never followed.
        guard ["http", "https", "about", "blob", "data"].contains(scheme) else {
            blockedCount += 1
            decisionHandler(.cancel)
            return
        }
        if navigationAction.shouldPerformDownload || (scheme != "about" && Self.isComicURL(url)) {
            decisionHandler(.download)
            return
        }
        decisionHandler(.allow)
    }

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, decidePolicyFor navigationResponse: WKNavigationResponse,
                 decisionHandler: @escaping (WKNavigationResponsePolicy) -> Void) {
        let response = navigationResponse.response
        let mime = (response.mimeType ?? "").lowercased()
        var attachment = false
        if let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse,
           let cd = http.value(forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Disposition")?.lowercased(), cd.contains("attachment") {
            attachment = true
        }
        let isFile = response.url.map { Self.isComicURL($0) } ?? false
        if navigationResponse.isForMainFrame,
           !navigationResponse.canShowMIMEType || attachment || isFile || Self.comicMIME.contains(mime) {
            decisionHandler(.download)
        } else {
            decisionHandler(.allow)
        }
    }

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, navigationAction: WKNavigationAction, didBecome download: WKDownload) {
        track(download)
    }

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, navigationResponse: WKNavigationResponse, didBecome download: WKDownload) {
        track(download)
    }

    // MARK: Pop-ups: a real tap on a link from the same site opens here; everything else is held back

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, createWebViewWith configuration: WKWebViewConfiguration,
                 for navigationAction: WKNavigationAction, windowFeatures: WKWindowFeatures) -> WKWebView? {
        guard let url = navigationAction.request.url else { return nil }
        if navigationAction.navigationType == .linkActivated {
            let here = webView.url?.host?.replacingOccurrences(of: "www.", with: "")
            let there = url.host?.replacingOccurrences(of: "www.", with: "")
            if here != nil, here == there || (there ?? "").hasSuffix("." + (here ?? "-")) {
                webView.load(URLRequest(url: url))
            } else {
                blockedCount += 1
                show("Held back a new tab to \(Self.display(url)).", open: url)
            }
        } else {
            blockedCount += 1
        }
        return nil
    }

    // MARK: Downloads

    private func track(_ download: WKDownload) {
        download.delegate = self
        let id = UUID()
        downloadIDs[ObjectIdentifier(download)] = id
        wkDownloads[id] = download
        downloads.insert(BrowserDownload(id: id, name: "Starting download…"), at: 0)
        progressObservers[id] = download.progress.observe(\.fractionCompleted) { [weak self] p, _ in
            let done = p.completedUnitCount, all = p.totalUnitCount
            DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.report(id, bytes: done, total: all) }
        }
    }

    /// Records progress and works out the speed (smoothed over the last second or so).
    private func report(_ id: UUID, bytes: Int64, total: Int64) {
        let now = Date()
        var speed: Double? = nil
        if let last = samples[id] {
            let dt = now.timeIntervalSince(last.time)
            if dt >= 0.8 {
                speed = Double(bytes - last.bytes) / dt
                samples[id] = (now, bytes)
            }
        } else {
            samples[id] = (now, bytes)
        }
        update(id) {
            $0.bytes = bytes
            if total > 0 { $0.total = total; $0.progress = min(1, Double(bytes) / Double(total)) }
            if let s = speed { $0.speed = $0.speed == 0 ? s : ($0.speed * 0.5 + s * 0.5) }
        }
    }

    private func update(_ id: UUID, _ change: (inout BrowserDownload) -> Void) {
        if let i = downloads.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) { change(&downloads[i]) }
    }

    /// Removes a download from the list, stopping it first if it is still coming down.
    func dismissDownload(_ id: UUID) {
        fastDownloads[id]?.cancel()
        fastDownloads[id] = nil
        if let key = downloadIDs.first(where: { $0.value == id })?.key {
            wkDownloads[id]?.cancel { _ in }
            downloadIDs[key] = nil
        }
        wkDownloads[id] = nil
        progressObservers[id] = nil
        if let d = destinations[id] { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: d.deletingLastPathComponent()) }
        destinations[id] = nil
        downloads.removeAll { $0.id == id }
    }

    func download(_ download: WKDownload, decideDestinationUsing response: URLResponse, suggestedFilename: String,
                  completionHandler: @escaping (URL?) -> Void) {
        guard let id = downloadIDs[ObjectIdentifier(download)] else { completionHandler(nil); return }
        var name = suggestedFilename.replacingOccurrences(of: "/", with: "_").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        if name.isEmpty { name = "download" }
        let ext = (name as NSString).pathExtension.lowercased()
        if !ext.isEmpty, !LibraryStore.comicExtensions.contains(ext), !Self.sniffExtensions.contains(ext) {
            update(id) { $0.name = name; $0.state = .failed("Not a comic file. Download canceled.") }
            completionHandler(nil)          // passing nil cancels the download
            return
        }
        // The same file already coming down (a page that starts it twice): keep the first, drop this one.
        let twin = downloads.contains { d in
            if d.id != id, d.name == name, case .downloading = d.state { return true }
            return false
        }
        if twin {
            downloads.removeAll { $0.id == id }
            progressObservers[id] = nil
            downloadIDs[ObjectIdentifier(download)] = nil
            completionHandler(nil)
            return
        }
        let dir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("PanelSnapDownload-\(UUID().uuidString)")
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        let dest = dir.appendingPathComponent(name)
        destinations[id] = dest
        update(id) { $0.name = name }
        completionHandler(dest)
        if let url = response.url, response.expectedContentLength >= 3_000_000, !noFast.contains(url.absoluteString) {
            accelerate(id: id, download: download, url: url, length: response.expectedContentLength, name: name)
        }
    }

    // MARK: Fast downloads: the same file over several connections

    private func requestHeaders(for url: URL) async -> [String: String] {
        var h: [String: String] = ["Accept-Encoding": "identity"]
        if let wv = webView {
            if let ua = try? await wv.evaluateJavaScript("navigator.userAgent") as? String { h["User-Agent"] = ua }
            if let page = wv.url { h["Referer"] = page.absoluteString }
            let host = url.host ?? ""
            let cookies = await wv.configuration.websiteDataStore.httpCookieStore.allCookies().filter {
                let d = $0.domain.hasPrefix(".") ? String($0.domain.dropFirst()) : $0.domain
                return host == d || host.hasSuffix("." + d)
            }
            for (k, v) in HTTPCookie.requestHeaderFields(with: cookies) { h[k] = v }
        }
        return h
    }

    /// Asks the server for the first byte only. If it answers with a proper partial response, the file is split up.
    private func probe(_ url: URL, headers: [String: String], length: Int64) async -> Bool {
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.timeoutInterval = 10
        for (k, v) in headers { req.setValue(v, forHTTPHeaderField: k) }
        req.setValue("bytes=0-0", forHTTPHeaderField: "Range")
        guard let (_, resp) = try? await URLSession.shared.data(for: req),
              let http = resp as? HTTPURLResponse, http.statusCode == 206,
              let range = http.value(forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Range"),
              range.hasSuffix("/\(length)") else { return false }
        return true
    }

    private func accelerate(id: UUID, download: WKDownload, url: URL, length: Int64, name: String) {
        Task {
            let headers = await requestHeaders(for: url)
            guard await probe(url, headers: headers, length: length) else {
                Console.log("browser: \(name) stays on one connection")
                return
            }
            // The server supports ranges: stop the single-connection download and fetch it in pieces instead.
            if let old = destinations[id] { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: old.deletingLastPathComponent()) }
            fastDownloads[id] = nil
            progressObservers[id] = nil
            downloadIDs[ObjectIdentifier(download)] = nil
            wkDownloads[id] = nil
            download.cancel { _ in }
            let dir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent("PanelSnapDownload-\(UUID().uuidString)")
            let dest = dir.appendingPathComponent(name)
            destinations[id] = dest
            update(id) { $0.fast = true; $0.total = length }
            Console.log("browser: \(name) downloading over several connections (more are added while it speeds up)")
            let fast = SegmentedDownloader(url: url, headers: headers, total: length, dest: dest, connections: 8)
            fast.onProgress = { [weak self] bytes in
                DispatchQueue.main.async { self?.report(id, bytes: bytes, total: length) }
            }
            fast.onFinish = { [weak self] error in
                DispatchQueue.main.async {
                    guard let self = self else { return }
                    self.fastDownloads[id] = nil
                    if error == nil {
                        self.completed(id: id, dest: dest)
                    } else {
                        Console.log("browser: fast download failed (\(error?.localizedDescription ?? "")), retrying on one connection")
                        self.fallBack(id: id, url: url)
                    }
                }
            }
            fastDownloads[id] = fast
            fast.start()
        }
    }

    /// The pieces would not come through: run the whole download again the ordinary way.
    private func fallBack(id: UUID, url: URL) {
        noFast.insert(url.absoluteString)
        dismissDownload(id)
        guard let wv = webView else { return }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        if let page = wv.url { req.setValue(page.absoluteString, forHTTPHeaderField: "Referer") }
        wv.startDownload(using: req) { [weak self] dl in self?.track(dl) }
    }

    func downloadDidFinish(_ download: WKDownload) {
        let key = ObjectIdentifier(download)
        guard let id = downloadIDs[key], let dest = destinations[id] else { return }
        downloadIDs[key] = nil
        progressObservers[id] = nil
        completed(id: id, dest: dest)
    }

    private func completed(id: UUID, dest destination: URL) {
        var dest = destination
        var ext = dest.pathExtension.lowercased()
        if !LibraryStore.comicExtensions.contains(ext) {
            // No usable extension: look inside the file to see what it is.
            if let sniffed = Self.sniff(dest) {
                let renamed = dest.deletingPathExtension().appendingPathExtension(sniffed)
                try? FileManager.default.moveItem(at: dest, to: renamed)
                dest = renamed
                ext = sniffed
            } else {
                try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: dest.deletingLastPathComponent())
                update(id) { $0.state = .failed("This file isn't a comic.") }
                return
            }
        }
        update(id) { $0.progress = 1; $0.state = .adding }
        guard let library = library else { return }
        let before = library.comics.count
        Task {
            await library.importFiles([dest], shelf: nil)
            let gained = library.comics.count - before
            let name = dest.lastPathComponent
            update(id) {
                if gained > 0 { $0.state = .done("Added to your library") }
                else if library.errorMessage != nil { $0.state = .failed("Downloaded, but couldn't be opened.") }
                else { $0.state = .done("Already in your library") }
            }
            Console.log("browser: downloaded \(name), library +\(gained)")
        }
    }

    func download(_ download: WKDownload, didFailWithError error: Error, resumeData: Data?) {
        let key = ObjectIdentifier(download)
        guard let id = downloadIDs[key] else { return }          // one we replaced with a faster download
        downloadIDs[key] = nil
        progressObservers[id] = nil
        let cancelled = (error as NSError).code == NSURLErrorCancelled
        if cancelled, case .failed = downloads.first(where: { $0.id == id })?.state ?? .downloading { return }
        let source = download.originalRequest?.url
        update(id) { $0.state = .failed("Download failed."); $0.retryURL = source }
        Console.log("browser: download failed: \(error.localizedDescription)")
    }

    private static func sniff(_ url: URL) -> String? {
        guard let h = try? FileHandle(forReadingFrom: url) else { return nil }
        defer { try? h.close() }
        let b = [UInt8]((try? h.read(upToCount: 8)) ?? Data())
        if b.starts(with: [0x50, 0x4B]) { return "cbz" }
        if b.starts(with: [0x52, 0x61, 0x72, 0x21]) { return "cbr" }
        if b.starts(with: [0x37, 0x7A, 0xBC, 0xAF]) { return "cb7" }
        if b.starts(with: [0x25, 0x50, 0x44, 0x46]) { return "pdf" }
        return nil
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/BrowserShield.swift =====
import Foundation
import WebKit

/// The browser's ad blocker. The filter lists (built from EasyList by Scripts/build_adblock.py) are bundled as
/// WebKit content-blocker JSON, compiled once on the device and then cached by WebKit, so later launches are instant.
/// Every list compiles on its own: if one ever fails, the others still protect the page.
@MainActor
final class BrowserShield: ObservableObject {
    static let shared = BrowserShield()

    @Published private(set) var isReady = false
    @Published private(set) var status = ""
    private(set) var lists: [WKContentRuleList] = []
    private var running: Task<Void, Never>?

    func prepare() async {
        if isReady { return }
        if let running = running { await running.value; return }
        let task = Task { await self.compileAll() }
        running = task
        await task.value
    }

    private func listNames() -> [String] {
        var names: [String] = []
        var n = 1
        while Bundle.main.url(forResource: "shield-net-\(n)", withExtension: "json") != nil {
            names.append("shield-net-\(n)")
            n += 1
        }
        if Bundle.main.url(forResource: "shield-hide", withExtension: "json") != nil { names.append("shield-hide") }
        return names
    }

    private func compileAll() async {
        let store: WKContentRuleListStore? = WKContentRuleListStore.default()
        let names = listNames()
        guard let store = store, !names.isEmpty else {
            Console.log("shield: no content rule store or no bundled lists")
            isReady = true
            return
        }
        for (i, name) in names.enumerated() {
            status = "Setting up the ad blocker (\(i + 1) of \(names.count))…"
            guard let url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: name, withExtension: "json"),
                  let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url),
                  let json = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) else { continue }
            // The size is part of the identifier, so a refreshed list is compiled again automatically.
            let id = "panelsnap.shield.\(name).\(data.count)"
            if let cached = await lookUp(store, id) {
                lists.append(cached)
                continue
            }
            if let fresh = await compile(store, id, json) {
                lists.append(fresh)
            }
        }
        Console.log("shield: \(lists.count) of \(names.count) lists active")
        isReady = true
    }

    private func lookUp(_ store: WKContentRuleListStore, _ id: String) async -> WKContentRuleList? {
        await withCheckedContinuation { cont in
            store.lookUpContentRuleList(forIdentifier: id) { list, _ in cont.resume(returning: list) }
        }
    }

    private func compile(_ store: WKContentRuleListStore, _ id: String, _ json: String) async -> WKContentRuleList? {
        await withCheckedContinuation { cont in
            store.compileContentRuleList(forIdentifier: id, encodedContentRuleList: json) { list, error in
                if let error = error { Console.log("shield: \(id) failed to compile: \(error.localizedDescription)") }
                cont.resume(returning: list)
            }
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/ComicInfoParser.swift =====
import Foundation

/// Reads the ComicInfo.xml that most comic tools (ComicTagger, Mylar, Kavita, ...) embed in CBZ/CBR files.
final class ComicInfoParser: NSObject, XMLParserDelegate {

    private var values: [String: String] = [:]
    private var current = ""
    private var buffer = ""

    static func parse(_ data: Data) -> ComicIdentity? {
        let p = ComicInfoParser()
        let xml = XMLParser(data: data)
        xml.delegate = p
        if !xml.parse() && p.values.isEmpty { return nil }
        if p.values.isEmpty { return nil }
        return p.makeIdentity()
    }

    // MARK: XMLParserDelegate

    func parser(_ parser: XMLParser, didStartElement elementName: String, namespaceURI: String?,
                qualifiedName qName: String?, attributes attributeDict: [String: String] = [:]) {
        current = elementName
        buffer = ""
    }

    func parser(_ parser: XMLParser, foundCharacters string: String) {
        buffer += string
    }

    func parser(_ parser: XMLParser, didEndElement elementName: String, namespaceURI: String?,
                qualifiedName qName: String?) {
        let text = buffer.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if !text.isEmpty && elementName != "ComicInfo" && elementName != "Pages" && elementName != "Page" {
            values[elementName.lowercased()] = text
        }
        buffer = ""
    }

    // MARK: Build

    private func list(_ key: String) -> [String] {
        guard let raw = values[key] else { return [] }
        return raw
            .split(whereSeparator: { $0 == "," || $0 == ";" || $0 == "\n" })
            .map { $0.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) }
            .filter { !$0.isEmpty }
    }

    private func makeIdentity() -> ComicIdentity {
        var id = ComicIdentity()
        id.series = values["series"]
        id.issue = values["number"]
        id.title = values["title"]
        id.volume = values["volume"]
        if let y = values["year"] { id.year = Int(y) }
        id.publisher = values["publisher"]
        id.imprint = values["imprint"]
        id.summary = values["summary"]
        id.writers = list("writer")
        id.pencillers = list("penciller")
        id.inkers = list("inker")
        id.colorists = list("colorist")
        id.letterers = list("letterer")
        id.coverArtists = list("coverartist")
        id.editors = list("editor")
        id.genres = list("genre")
        id.characters = list("characters")
        id.teams = list("teams")
        id.locations = list("locations")
        id.storyArc = list("storyarc").first
        id.seriesGroup = values["seriesgroup"]
        id.web = values["web"]
        id.ageRating = values["agerating"]
        id.languageISO = values["languageiso"]
        if let m = values["manga"]?.lowercased() {
            id.isManga = m.hasPrefix("yes")
            id.rtl = m.contains("righttoleft")
        }
        return id
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/ComicSource.swift =====
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics
import ImageIO

/// A comic file we can read pages from (CBZ, CBR, CB7, PDF, EPUB ...).
protocol ComicSource: AnyObject {
    var pageCount: Int { get }
    /// The embedded ComicInfo.xml, if any.
    func metadataXML() -> Data?
    /// Raw bytes of one page image (archives only).
    func pageData(at index: Int) throws -> Data
    /// A decoded page, shrunk so its longest side is at most `maxPixel`.
    func pageImage(at index: Int, maxPixel: CGFloat) -> CGImage?
}

extension ComicSource {
    func pageImage(at index: Int, maxPixel: CGFloat) -> CGImage? {
        guard let data = try? pageData(at: index) else { return nil }
        return ImageDecoder.decode(data, maxPixel: maxPixel)
    }
}

enum ComicError: LocalizedError {
    case unreadable
    case noPages
    case unsupported(String)

    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .unreadable: return "This file couldn't be opened."
        case .noPages: return "No pages were found inside this file."
        case .unsupported(let what): return what
        }
    }
}

enum ImageDecoder {
    static func decode(_ data: Data, maxPixel: CGFloat) -> CGImage? {
        guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithData(data as CFData, nil) else { return nil }
        var longest: CGFloat = maxPixel
        if let props = CGImageSourceCopyPropertiesAtIndex(src, 0, nil) as? [CFString: Any],
           let w = (props[kCGImagePropertyPixelWidth] as? NSNumber)?.doubleValue,
           let h = (props[kCGImagePropertyPixelHeight] as? NSNumber)?.doubleValue {
            longest = min(maxPixel, CGFloat(max(w, h)))
        }
        let options: [CFString: Any] = [
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
            kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
            kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true,
            kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: longest
        ]
        return CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(src, 0, options as CFDictionary)
    }
}

/// Helpers shared by the archive-based sources.
enum ArchiveNames {
    static let imageExtensions: Set<String> = ["jpg", "jpeg", "png", "webp", "gif", "bmp", "tif", "tiff", "heic", "heif", "avif", "jp2"]

    static func isPage(_ path: String) -> Bool {
        let lower = path.lowercased()
        if lower.contains("__macosx/") { return false }
        let last = (lower as NSString).lastPathComponent
        if last.hasPrefix(".") || last.hasPrefix("._") { return false }
        let ext = (last as NSString).pathExtension
        return imageExtensions.contains(ext)
    }

    static func isComicInfo(_ path: String) -> Bool {
        let last = ((path as NSString).lastPathComponent).lowercased()
        return last == "comicinfo.xml"
    }

    /// Natural ordering: page2 comes before page10.
    static func sortKey(_ a: String, _ b: String) -> Bool {
        return a.localizedStandardCompare(b) == .orderedAscending
    }
}

/// Figures out what a file really is by peeking at its first bytes (so a mislabelled .cbz still opens).
enum ComicOpener {

    static func detectFormat(url: URL) -> ComicFormat {
        let ext = ComicFormat.from(fileExtension: url.pathExtension)
        guard let handle = try? FileHandle(forReadingFrom: url) else { return ext }
        defer { try? handle.close() }
        let head = (try? handle.read(upToCount: 8)) ?? Data()
        let bytes = [UInt8](head)
        if bytes.count >= 4 {
            if bytes[0] == 0x50 && bytes[1] == 0x4B {   // "PK"
                return ext == .epub ? .epub : .cbz
            }
            if bytes[0] == 0x52 && bytes[1] == 0x61 && bytes[2] == 0x72 && bytes[3] == 0x21 {   // "Rar!"
                return .cbr
            }
            if bytes[0] == 0x37 && bytes[1] == 0x7A && bytes[2] == 0xBC && bytes[3] == 0xAF {   // "7z"
                return .cb7
            }
            if bytes[0] == 0x25 && bytes[1] == 0x50 && bytes[2] == 0x44 && bytes[3] == 0x46 {   // "%PDF"
                return .pdf
            }
        }
        return ext
    }

    static func open(url: URL) throws -> ComicSource {
        let format = detectFormat(url: url)
        switch format {
        case .cbz, .epub:
            return try ZipComicSource(url: url)
        case .cbr:
            #if canImport(Unrar)
            return try RarComicSource(url: url)
            #else
            throw ComicError.unsupported("CBR/RAR files need the full build of PanelSnap. Convert it to CBZ, or run the build again.")
            #endif
        case .cb7:
            #if canImport(SevenZip)
            return try SevenZipComicSource(url: url)
            #else
            throw ComicError.unsupported("CB7/7z files need the full build of PanelSnap. Convert it to CBZ, or run the build again.")
            #endif
        case .pdf:
            return try PDFComicSource(url: url)
        case .other:
            throw ComicError.unsupported("PanelSnap can't read this kind of file. Try CBZ, CBR, CB7, PDF or EPUB.")
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/Companion/CharacterGlossary.swift =====
import Foundation

/// The built-in "who's who". It ships inside the app (Resources/Characters.json), so character guides work offline
/// and need no account or key. Built from the Superhero API (MIT) and FiveThirtyEight's comic-characters data (CC BY 4.0).
struct GlossaryEntry: Codable {
    var n: String
    var r: String?
    var p: String
    var ap: Int?
    var y: Int?
    var w: String?
    var al: [String]?
    var fa: String?
    var g: String?
    var rel: String?
    var occ: String?
    var img: String?
}

final class CharacterGlossary {
    static let shared = CharacterGlossary()
    static let sourceName = "Built-in guide"

    private(set) var entries: [GlossaryEntry] = []
    private var byName: [String: Int] = [:]          // normalised name / real name / alias -> entry

    private init() {
        guard let url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "Characters", withExtension: "json"),
              let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url),
              let list = try? JSONDecoder().decode([GlossaryEntry].self, from: data) else {
            Console.log("glossary: Characters.json missing from the app")
            return
        }
        entries = list
        for (i, e) in list.enumerated() {
            for key in [e.n, e.r ?? ""] {
                let k = CharacterGlossary.norm(key)
                if k.count >= 3 && byName[k] == nil { byName[k] = i }
            }
        }
        for (i, e) in list.enumerated() {
            for a in e.al ?? [] {
                let k = CharacterGlossary.norm(a)
                if k.count >= 6 && byName[k] == nil { byName[k] = i }
            }
        }
        Console.log("glossary: \(list.count) built-in characters loaded")
    }

    static func norm(_ s: String) -> String {
        var out = ""
        for u in s.lowercased().unicodeScalars where CharacterSet.alphanumerics.contains(u) {
            out.unicodeScalars.append(u)
        }
        return out
    }

    /// Lower-case words separated by single spaces, e.g. "Spider-Man!" -> "spider man".
    private static func words(_ s: String) -> String {
        var out = ""
        var lastSpace = true
        for u in s.lowercased().unicodeScalars {
            if CharacterSet.alphanumerics.contains(u) {
                out.unicodeScalars.append(u)
                lastSpace = false
            } else if !lastSpace {
                out += " "
                lastSpace = true
            }
        }
        return out.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
    }

    func find(name: String) -> GlossaryEntry? {
        var cleaned = name
        if let r = cleaned.range(of: " [") { cleaned = String(cleaned[cleaned.startIndex..<r.lowerBound]) }
        if let i = byName[CharacterGlossary.norm(cleaned)] { return entries[i] }
        return nil
    }

    /// Characters named in the comic's title / series (case-insensitive) first, then names that show up
    /// at least twice (or are a full two-word name) in longer text such as a synopsis.
    func matches(titles: [String], texts: [String], limit: Int) -> [GlossaryEntry] {
        var found: [Int] = []
        var seen = Set<Int>()

        var titleHits: [(Int, Int)] = []          // (position, entry)
        for t in titles {
            let padded = " " + CharacterGlossary.words(t) + " "
            if padded.count < 5 { continue }
            for (i, e) in entries.enumerated() where !seen.contains(i) {
                for key in [e.n, e.r ?? ""] {
                    let w = CharacterGlossary.words(key)
                    if w.count < 4 { continue }
                    if let r = padded.range(of: " " + w + " ") {
                        titleHits.append((padded.distance(from: padded.startIndex, to: r.lowerBound), i))
                        seen.insert(i)
                        break
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        titleHits.sort { $0.0 < $1.0 }
        found += titleHits.map { $0.1 }

        // Longer text: count 1-3 word phrases, keeping their capital letters.
        var counts: [String: Int] = [:]
        for text in texts {
            let tokens = text.split(whereSeparator: { !($0.isLetter || $0.isNumber || $0 == "-" || $0 == "'") }).map(String.init)
            if tokens.isEmpty { continue }
            for n in 1...3 {
                if tokens.count < n { break }
                for i in 0...(tokens.count - n) {
                    let first = tokens[i].first
                    if first == nil || !(first!.isUppercase) { continue }
                    counts[tokens[i..<(i + n)].joined(separator: " "), default: 0] += 1
                }
            }
        }
        var textHits: [(Int, Int)] = []           // (count, entry)
        for (i, e) in entries.enumerated() where !seen.contains(i) {
            var best = 0
            for key in [e.n, e.r ?? ""] where !key.isEmpty {
                let c = counts[key] ?? 0
                let multiword = key.contains(" ")
                if c >= 2 || (multiword && c >= 1) { best = max(best, c) }
            }
            if best > 0 { textHits.append((best, i)) }
        }
        textHits.sort { ($0.0, entries[$0.1].ap ?? 0) > ($1.0, entries[$1.1].ap ?? 0) }
        found += textHits.map { $0.1 }

        return found.prefix(limit).map { entries[$0] }
    }

    func publisherGuess(_ list: [GlossaryEntry]) -> String? {
        var tally: [String: Int] = [:]
        for e in list { tally[e.p, default: 0] += 1 }
        guard let top = tally.max(by: { $0.value < $1.value }), top.value >= 2 || list.count == 1 else { return nil }
        switch top.key {
        case "Marvel": return "Marvel Comics"
        case "DC": return "DC Comics"
        default: return nil
        }
    }

    func card(_ e: GlossaryEntry) -> CharacterCard {
        var real: String?
        if let r = e.r, !r.isEmpty, CharacterGlossary.norm(r) != CharacterGlossary.norm(e.n) { real = r }
        var facts: [Fact] = []
        if let real = real { facts.append(Fact(label: "Real name", value: real)) }
        if let a = e.al, !a.isEmpty { facts.append(Fact(label: "Also called", value: a.prefix(3).joined(separator: ", "))) }
        if let fa = e.fa, !fa.isEmpty {
            facts.append(Fact(label: "First appeared", value: fa))
        } else if let y = e.y, y > 0 {
            facts.append(Fact(label: "First appeared", value: String(y)))
        }
        if let ap = e.ap, ap >= 50 {
            let f = NumberFormatter()
            f.numberStyle = .decimal
            let n = f.string(from: NSNumber(value: ap)) ?? String(ap)
            facts.append(Fact(label: "Comics", value: "In about \(n) issues (2014 count)"))
        }
        if let g = e.g, !g.isEmpty { facts.append(Fact(label: "Teams", value: g)) }
        if let rel = e.rel, !rel.isEmpty { facts.append(Fact(label: "Family & friends", value: rel)) }
        if let o = e.occ, !o.isEmpty { facts.append(Fact(label: "Job", value: o)) }
        facts.append(Fact(label: "Publisher", value: e.p))
        var link = e.w
        if link?.isEmpty == true { link = nil }
        return CharacterCard(name: e.n, role: real, blurb: nil,
                             imageURL: (e.img?.isEmpty == false) ? e.img : nil,
                             url: link, source: CharacterGlossary.sourceName, facts: facts)
    }

    /// Adds the built-in facts to a card that came from somewhere else (e.g. the comic's own metadata).
    func enrich(_ c: CharacterCard, with e: GlossaryEntry) -> CharacterCard {
        let built = card(e)
        var out = c
        if out.facts == nil { out.facts = built.facts }
        if out.imageURL == nil { out.imageURL = built.imageURL }
        if out.url == nil { out.url = built.url }
        if out.role == nil { out.role = built.role }
        return out
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/Companion/CompanionData.swift =====
import Foundation

struct TextBlock: Codable, Hashable {
    var text: String
    var source: String
    var url: String?
}

struct Fact: Codable, Hashable {
    var label: String
    var value: String
}

struct CharacterCard: Codable, Hashable, Identifiable {
    var name: String
    var role: String?
    var blurb: String?
    var imageURL: String?
    var url: String?
    var source: String?
    var facts: [Fact]? = nil

    var id: String { return name }
}

struct Credit: Codable, Hashable, Identifiable {
    var role: String
    var name: String

    var id: String { return role + "|" + name }
}

struct LinkItem: Codable, Hashable, Identifiable {
    var title: String
    var url: String

    var id: String { return url }
}

/// Everything the Companion panel can show about the comic being read.
struct CompanionData: Codable, Hashable {
    var series: String?
    var issue: String?
    var volume: String?
    var year: Int?
    var publisher: String?
    var title: String?
    var coverURL: String?
    var seriesSummary: TextBlock?
    var issueSummary: TextBlock?
    var issueSynopsis: TextBlock?        // may contain spoilers
    var characters: [CharacterCard] = []
    var teams: [String] = []
    var locations: [String] = []
    var creators: [Credit] = []
    var arcs: [String] = []
    var genres: [String] = []
    var links: [LinkItem] = []
    var sources: [String] = []
    var fetchedAt: Date?

    /// Fills in anything we don't know yet from `other`. Existing values always win.
    mutating func fill(from other: CompanionData) {
        if series == nil { series = other.series }
        if issue == nil { issue = other.issue }
        if volume == nil { volume = other.volume }
        if year == nil { year = other.year }
        if publisher == nil { publisher = other.publisher }
        if title == nil { title = other.title }
        if coverURL == nil { coverURL = other.coverURL }
        if seriesSummary == nil { seriesSummary = other.seriesSummary }
        if issueSummary == nil { issueSummary = other.issueSummary }
        if issueSynopsis == nil { issueSynopsis = other.issueSynopsis }

        for c in other.characters {
            if let i = characters.firstIndex(where: { CompanionData.same($0.name, c.name) }) {
                if characters[i].blurb == nil { characters[i].blurb = c.blurb }
                if characters[i].imageURL == nil { characters[i].imageURL = c.imageURL }
                if characters[i].url == nil { characters[i].url = c.url }
                if characters[i].role == nil { characters[i].role = c.role }
                if characters[i].facts == nil { characters[i].facts = c.facts }
            } else {
                characters.append(c)
            }
        }
        teams = CompanionData.union(teams, other.teams)
        locations = CompanionData.union(locations, other.locations)
        arcs = CompanionData.union(arcs, other.arcs)
        genres = CompanionData.union(genres, other.genres)
        for c in other.creators {
            if !creators.contains(where: { CompanionData.same($0.name, c.name) && $0.role == c.role }) {
                creators.append(c)
            }
        }
        for l in other.links where !links.contains(where: { $0.url == l.url }) {
            links.append(l)
        }
        for s in other.sources where !sources.contains(s) {
            sources.append(s)
        }
    }

    static func same(_ a: String, _ b: String) -> Bool {
        return a.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).lowercased() == b.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).lowercased()
    }

    static func union(_ a: [String], _ b: [String]) -> [String] {
        var out = a
        for item in b where !out.contains(where: { same($0, item) }) {
            out.append(item)
        }
        return out
    }
}

enum CompanionCache {
    static func fileURL(for id: UUID) -> URL {
        return Paths.companionCache.appendingPathComponent(id.uuidString + "-v2.json")
    }

    static func read(_ id: UUID, maxAgeDays: Double) -> CompanionData? {
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: fileURL(for: id)) else { return nil }
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        guard let value = try? decoder.decode(CompanionData.self, from: data) else { return nil }
        if let when = value.fetchedAt, Date().timeIntervalSince(when) > maxAgeDays * 86_400 { return nil }
        return value
    }

    static func write(_ id: UUID, _ value: CompanionData) {
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        if let data = try? encoder.encode(value) {
            try? data.write(to: fileURL(for: id), options: .atomic)
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/Companion/CompanionModel.swift =====
import Foundation
import SwiftUI

@MainActor
final class CompanionModel: ObservableObject {

    enum Phase {
        case idle
        case loading
        case done
    }

    @Published private(set) var data: CompanionData
    @Published private(set) var phase: Phase = .idle
    @Published private(set) var tip: String?
    @Published var fixSeries: String
    @Published var fixIssue: String

    let entry: ComicEntry
    let coverURL: URL
    private var identity: ComicIdentity
    private let settings: AppSettings
    private var started = false
    private var running = false

    init(entry: ComicEntry, identity: ComicIdentity, settings: AppSettings, coverURL: URL) {
        self.entry = entry
        self.identity = identity
        self.settings = settings
        self.coverURL = coverURL
        let base = CompanionModel.baseline(identity)
        self.data = base
        self.fixSeries = identity.series ?? ""
        self.fixIssue = identity.issue ?? ""
    }

    /// What we already know from the file itself, with no internet.
    static func baseline(_ id: ComicIdentity) -> CompanionData {
        var d = CompanionData()
        d.series = id.series
        d.issue = id.issue
        d.volume = id.volume
        d.year = id.year
        d.publisher = id.publisher
        d.title = id.title
        if let s = id.summary, !s.isEmpty {
            d.issueSummary = TextBlock(text: TextClean.html(s), source: "Comic file", url: nil)
        }
        d.characters = id.characters.map { CharacterCard(name: $0, role: nil, blurb: nil, imageURL: nil, url: nil, source: nil) }
        d.teams = id.teams
        d.locations = id.locations
        d.genres = id.genres
        if let arc = id.storyArc { d.arcs = [arc] }
        func add(_ role: String, _ names: [String]) {
            for n in names { d.creators.append(Credit(role: role, name: n)) }
        }
        add("Writer", id.writers)
        add("Penciller", id.pencillers)
        add("Inker", id.inkers)
        add("Colorist", id.colorists)
        add("Letterer", id.letterers)
        add("Cover", id.coverArtists)
        add("Editor", id.editors)
        if let web = id.web, let first = web.split(separator: " ").first, first.hasPrefix("http") {
            d.links.append(LinkItem(title: "Link from the comic file", url: String(first)))
        }
        d.sources = ["Comic file"]
        return d
    }

    // MARK: Loading

    func load(force: Bool = false) {
        if started && !force { return }
        started = true

        if !force, let cached = CompanionCache.read(entry.id, maxAgeDays: 21) {
            data = cached
            applyGlossary()
            phase = .done
            updateTip()
            return
        }
        applyGlossary()
        guard settings.onlineLookup else {
            phase = .done
            tip = "Online lookup is off, so this is the built-in guide only. Turn it on in Settings for story summaries and more."
            return
        }
        if running { return }
        phase = .loading
        Task { await runLookups() }
    }

    func relookup(series: String, issue: String) {
