        identity.series = series.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty ? nil : series
        identity.issue = issue.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty ? nil : issue
        var fresh = CompanionModel.baseline(identity)
        fresh.series = identity.series
        fresh.issue = identity.issue
        data = fresh
        applyGlossary()
        started = true
        if settings.onlineLookup {
            phase = .loading
            Task { await runLookups() }
        }
    }

    private func updateTip() {
        let d = data
        let empty = d.issueSynopsis == nil && d.seriesSummary == nil && d.issueSummary == nil && d.characters.isEmpty
        tip = (empty && phase != .loading)
            ? "I couldn't find anything about this one. Tap “Not the right comic?” to type its real name."
            : nil
    }

    /// The built-in guide: no internet, no account. Adds facts to characters we already know about
    /// and picks out famous characters named in the title or any summary text.
    private func applyGlossary() {
        let glossary = CharacterGlossary.shared
        var d = data
        for i in d.characters.indices {
            if let e = glossary.find(name: d.characters[i].name) {
                d.characters[i] = glossary.enrich(d.characters[i], with: e)
            }
        }
        let titles = [d.series, d.title, d.arcs.first].compactMap { $0 }
        let texts = [d.issueSummary?.text, d.issueSynopsis?.text, d.seriesSummary?.text].compactMap { $0 }
        let room = max(0, 12 - d.characters.count)
        if room > 0 {
            let found = glossary.matches(titles: titles, texts: texts, limit: room)
            for e in found where !d.characters.contains(where: { CompanionData.same($0.name, e.n) }) {
                d.characters.append(glossary.card(e))
            }
            if d.publisher == nil, let p = glossary.publisherGuess(found) { d.publisher = p }
        }
        if !d.characters.isEmpty && !d.sources.contains(CharacterGlossary.sourceName) {
            d.sources.append(CharacterGlossary.sourceName)
        }
        data = d
    }

    private func runLookups() async {
        if running { return }
        running = true
        defer { running = false }

        let id = identity
        var d = data
        let seriesName = id.series ?? ""

        if seriesName.isEmpty {
            phase = .done
            tip = "I couldn't work out this comic's name from its file. Tap “Not the right comic?” to type it in."
            return
        }

        // 1. Grand Comics Database: credits, cast and sometimes a synopsis.
        if let gcd = await GCDProvider.lookup(series: seriesName, issue: id.issue, year: id.year, publisher: d.publisher ?? id.publisher) {
            d.fill(from: gcd)
            data = d
        }

        // 2. Marvel / DC fan wikis: issue synopsis, creators and who appears.
        if let number = id.issue ?? d.issue, d.issueSynopsis == nil || d.creators.isEmpty || d.characters.isEmpty {
            if let wiki = await FandomProvider.lookup(publisher: d.publisher ?? id.publisher, series: d.series ?? seriesName, volume: id.volume, issue: number) {
                d.fill(from: wiki)
                data = d
            }
        }

        // 3. Wikipedia series background.
        if d.seriesSummary == nil {
            if let wp = await WikipediaProvider.series(name: d.series ?? seriesName, publisher: d.publisher) {
                d.fill(from: wp)
                data = d
            }
        }

        // 4. Open Library, for graphic novels and collected editions.
        if d.seriesSummary == nil {
            if let ol = await OpenLibraryProvider.lookup(series: d.series ?? seriesName, publisher: d.publisher) {
                d.fill(from: ol)
                data = d
            }
        }

        // 5. AniList for manga (or as a last resort with a strict name match).
        if id.isManga || d.seriesSummary == nil {
            if let al = await AniListProvider.lookup(series: seriesName, strict: !id.isManga) {
                d.fill(from: al)
                data = d
            }
        }

        // 6. Built-in guide again, now that there's more text to read names from.
        applyGlossary()
        d = data
        d.fetchedAt = Date()
        data = d
        phase = .done
        updateTip()
        CompanionCache.write(entry.id, d)

        // 7. Character blurbs (quietly, in the background).
        await enrichCharacters()
        var finalData = data
        finalData.fetchedAt = Date()
        CompanionCache.write(entry.id, finalData)
    }

    private func enrichCharacters() async {
        let publisher = data.publisher
        let names = data.characters.prefix(8).filter { $0.blurb == nil }.map { $0.name }
        if names.isEmpty { return }
        await withTaskGroup(of: CharacterCard?.self) { group in
            for name in names {
                group.addTask {
                    return await WikipediaProvider.character(name: name, publisher: publisher)
                }
            }
            for await card in group {
                guard let card = card else { continue }
                if let i = data.characters.firstIndex(where: { CompanionData.same($0.name, card.name) }) {
                    data.characters[i].blurb = card.blurb
                    if data.characters[i].imageURL == nil { data.characters[i].imageURL = card.imageURL }
                    if data.characters[i].url == nil { data.characters[i].url = card.url }
                    data.characters[i].source = card.source
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Called when someone opens a character that has no description yet.
    func loadCharacter(_ name: String) async {
        if let i = data.characters.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == name }), data.characters[i].facts == nil,
           let e = CharacterGlossary.shared.find(name: name) {
            data.characters[i] = CharacterGlossary.shared.enrich(data.characters[i], with: e)
        }
        guard settings.onlineLookup else { return }
        guard let i = data.characters.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == name }), data.characters[i].blurb == nil else { return }
        if let card = await WikipediaProvider.character(name: name, publisher: data.publisher) {
            if let j = data.characters.firstIndex(where: { $0.name == name }) {
                data.characters[j].blurb = card.blurb
                if data.characters[j].imageURL == nil { data.characters[j].imageURL = card.imageURL }
                if data.characters[j].url == nil { data.characters[j].url = card.url }
                data.characters[j].source = card.source
            }
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/Companion/Net.swift =====
import Foundation

enum HTTP {
    static let userAgent = "PanelSnap/1.0 (iOS comic reader; https://github.com/jakebway/PanelSnap)"

    private static let allowed: CharacterSet = {
        var set = CharacterSet.alphanumerics
        set.insert(charactersIn: "-._~")
        return set
    }()

    static func encode(_ s: String) -> String {
        return s.addingPercentEncoding(withAllowedCharacters: allowed) ?? s
    }

    static func url(_ base: String, _ items: [(String, String)]) -> URL? {
        let query = items.map { $0.0 + "=" + encode($0.1) }.joined(separator: "&")
        return URL(string: base + (base.contains("?") ? "&" : "?") + query)
    }

    static func json(_ url: URL, method: String = "GET", body: Data? = nil, headers: [String: String] = [:]) async throws -> Any {
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.timeoutInterval = 15
        req.httpMethod = method
        req.httpBody = body
        req.setValue(userAgent, forHTTPHeaderField: "User-Agent")
        req.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Accept")
        for (k, v) in headers { req.setValue(v, forHTTPHeaderField: k) }
        let (data, response) = try await URLSession.shared.data(for: req)
        if let http = response as? HTTPURLResponse, !(200..<300).contains(http.statusCode) {
            throw URLError(.badServerResponse)
        }
        return try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data)
    }
}

/// Turns HTML / wiki markup into readable plain text.
enum TextClean {

    static func html(_ input: String) -> String {
        var s = input
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<br\\s*/?>", with: "\n", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "</p>", with: "\n\n")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<li>", with: "• ")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "</li>", with: "\n")
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<[^>]+>", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        s = decodeEntities(s)
        return tidy(s)
    }

    static func decodeEntities(_ input: String) -> String {
        var s = input
        let map: [(String, String)] = [
            ("&nbsp;", " "), ("&amp;", "&"), ("&quot;", "\""), ("&#39;", "'"), ("&#039;", "'"),
            ("&apos;", "'"), ("&lt;", "<"), ("&gt;", ">"), ("&rsquo;", "’"), ("&lsquo;", "‘"),
            ("&ldquo;", "“"), ("&rdquo;", "”"), ("&ndash;", "–"), ("&mdash;", "—"), ("&hellip;", "…")
        ]
        for (k, v) in map { s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: k, with: v) }
        return s
    }

    static func tidy(_ input: String) -> String {
        var s = input.replacingOccurrences(of: "[ \\t]+", with: " ", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\n{3,}", with: "\n\n", options: .regularExpression)
        return s.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
    }

    /// Cuts text to roughly `limit` characters at a sentence end.
    static func shorten(_ input: String, limit: Int) -> String {
        if input.count <= limit { return input }
        let head = String(input.prefix(limit))
        if let r = head.range(of: ". ", options: .backwards), head.distance(from: head.startIndex, to: r.lowerBound) > limit / 2 {
            return String(head[head.startIndex..<r.lowerBound]) + "."
        }
        return head.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines) + "…"
    }

    static func wiki(_ input: String) -> String {
        var s = input
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<ref[^>]*/>", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "(?s)<ref[^>]*>.*?</ref>", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<!--.*?-->", with: "", options: [.regularExpression])
        for _ in 0..<5 {
            s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\{\\{[^{}]*\\}\\}", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        }
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\[\\[(?:File|Image|Category):[^\\]]*\\]\\]", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\[\\[[^\\]|]*\\|([^\\]]*)\\]\\]", with: "$1", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\[\\[([^\\]]*)\\]\\]", with: "$1", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "'{2,5}", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<br\\s*/?>", with: "\n", options: .regularExpression)
        s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "<[^>]+>", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        s = decodeEntities(s)
        return tidy(s)
    }
}

enum Similarity {
    static func normalize(_ s: String) -> String {
        let lowered = s.lowercased()
        let kept = lowered.unicodeScalars.filter { CharacterSet.alphanumerics.contains($0) }
        var out = ""
        out.unicodeScalars.append(contentsOf: kept)
        return out
    }

    /// Roughly "do these two series names mean the same thing?"
    static func score(_ a: String, _ b: String) -> Int {
        let x = normalize(a)
        let y = normalize(b)
        if x.isEmpty || y.isEmpty { return 0 }
        if x == y { return 100 }
        let stripped = { (s: String) -> String in
            return s.hasPrefix("the") ? String(s.dropFirst(3)) : s
        }
        if stripped(x) == stripped(y) { return 95 }
        if x.contains(y) || y.contains(x) { return 55 }
        return 0
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/Companion/Providers.swift =====
import Foundation

// MARK: - Grand Comics Database (no key needed)

/// The GCD is a volunteer-built catalogue of comics. Its public API works without an account.
/// Data is shared under CC BY-SA 4.0 (https://www.comics.org).
enum GCDProvider {
    static let base = "https://www.comics.org/api"

    private static func clean(_ s: Any?) -> String? {
        guard var t = s as? String else { return nil }
        t = t.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\(signed\\)|\\(credited\\)|\\(uncredited\\)|\\[[^\\]]*\\]", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
        t = TextClean.tidy(t)
        if t.isEmpty || t == "?" || t.lowercased() == "none" || t.lowercased() == "n/a" { return nil }
        return t
    }

    private static func names(_ s: Any?) -> [String] {
        guard let raw = s as? String else { return [] }
        return raw.split(separator: ";").compactMap { part in
            var t = String(part)
            t = t.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\([^)]*\\)|\\[[^\\]]*\\]", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
            t = TextClean.tidy(t)
            return (t.isEmpty || t == "?" || t.lowercased() == "none") ? nil : t
        }
    }

    /// "Batman [Bruce Wayne]; Joker (flashback)" -> [("Batman", "Bruce Wayne"), ("Joker", nil)]
    private static func characters(_ raw: String) -> [(String, String?)] {
        let parts: [String]
        if raw.contains(";") {
            parts = raw.split(separator: ";").map(String.init)
        } else {
            // A plain comma list, but keep commas that sit inside brackets/parentheses.
            var out: [String] = []
            var depth = 0
            var cur = ""
            for ch in raw {
                if ch == "(" || ch == "[" { depth += 1 }
                if ch == ")" || ch == "]" { depth = max(0, depth - 1) }
                if ch == "," && depth == 0 { out.append(cur); cur = "" } else { cur.append(ch) }
            }
            out.append(cur)
            parts = out
        }
        var result: [(String, String?)] = []
        for p in parts {
            var name = p
            var real: String?
            if let r = p.range(of: "\\[([^\\]]+)\\]", options: .regularExpression) {
                real = String(p[r]).trimmingCharacters(in: CharacterSet(charactersIn: "[] "))
            }
            name = name.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\([^)]*\\)|\\[[^\\]]*\\]", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
            name = TextClean.tidy(name)
            if name.count < 2 || name.lowercased().hasPrefix("plus ") || name.lowercased() == "none" { continue }
            result.append((name, real))
        }
        return result
    }

    private static func seriesBaseName(_ s: String) -> String {
        return s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\s*\\([^)]*\\)\\s*$", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
    }

    static func lookup(series: String, issue: String?, year: Int?, publisher: String?) async -> CompanionData? {
        guard let number = issue, !number.isEmpty else { return nil }
        var path = "/series/name/\(HTTP.encode(series))/issue/\(HTTP.encode(number))"
        if let y = year { path += "/year/\(y)" }
        guard let listURL = URL(string: base + path + "/?format=json"),
              let root = try? await HTTP.json(listURL) as? [String: Any],
              let results = root["results"] as? [[String: Any]], !results.isEmpty else {
            Console.log("gcd: nothing for \(series) #\(number)")
            return nil
        }

        // The name search matches anywhere in a title, so keep only exact series names, originals first.
        let want = Similarity.normalize(series)
        let exact = results.filter {
            let n = Similarity.normalize(seriesBaseName(($0["series_name"] as? String) ?? ""))
            return n == want && ($0["variant_of"] == nil || $0["variant_of"] is NSNull)
        }
        guard let pick = exact.first(where: { (($0["series_name"] as? String) ?? "").contains("(\(year ?? 0) series)") }) ?? exact.first,
              let urlString = pick["api_url"] as? String, let url = URL(string: urlString),
              let d = try? await HTTP.json(url) as? [String: Any] else { return nil }

        var out = CompanionData()
        out.sources.append("Grand Comics Database")
        if let c = d["cover"] as? String, !c.isEmpty { out.coverURL = c }
        if let n = d["number"] as? Int { out.issue = String(n) }
        if let t = clean(d["title"]) { out.title = t }
        if let p = clean(d["indicia_publisher"]) { out.publisher = out.publisher ?? p }
        let siteID = urlString.split(separator: "/").dropLast().last.map(String.init) ?? ""
        let siteURL = "https://www.comics.org/issue/\(siteID)/"
        out.links.append(LinkItem(title: "This issue on the Grand Comics Database", url: siteURL))

        for n in names(d["editing"]) { out.creators.append(Credit(role: "Editor", name: n)) }

        var synopses: [String] = []
        var cast: [(String, String?)] = []
        if let stories = d["story_set"] as? [[String: Any]] {
            for st in stories {
                let type = (st["type"] as? String) ?? ""
                if type == "cover" {
                    for n in names(st["pencils"]) { out.creators.append(Credit(role: "Cover", name: n)) }
                    continue
                }
                guard type == "comic story" else { continue }
                let roles: [(String, String)] = [("script", "Writer"), ("pencils", "Penciller"), ("inks", "Inker"),
                                                 ("colors", "Colorist"), ("letters", "Letterer")]
                for (key, role) in roles {
                    for n in names(st[key]) { out.creators.append(Credit(role: role, name: n)) }
                }
                if let g = clean(st["genre"]) {
                    for part in g.split(separator: ";") {
                        let t = part.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).capitalized
                        if !t.isEmpty && !out.genres.contains(t) { out.genres.append(t) }
                    }
                }
                if let ch = st["characters"] as? String { cast += characters(ch) }
                if let syn = clean(st["synopsis"]), syn.count > 30 { synopses.append(syn) }
            }
        }
        if !synopses.isEmpty {
            out.issueSynopsis = TextBlock(text: TextClean.shorten(synopses.joined(separator: "\n\n"), limit: 1800),
                                          source: "Grand Comics Database", url: siteURL)
        }
        var seen = Set<String>()
        for (name, real) in cast where seen.insert(Similarity.normalize(name)).inserted {
            out.characters.append(CharacterCard(name: name, role: real, blurb: nil, imageURL: nil, url: nil, source: nil))
            if out.characters.count >= 24 { break }
        }
        return out
    }
}

// MARK: - Open Library (books and graphic novels, no key needed)

enum OpenLibraryProvider {
    /// A description for collected editions / graphic novels that Wikipedia may not cover.
    static func lookup(series: String, publisher: String?) async -> CompanionData? {
        guard let url = HTTP.url("https://openlibrary.org/search.json", [
            ("q", series + " graphic novel"), ("limit", "5"), ("fields", "key,title,first_publish_year")
        ]), let root = try? await HTTP.json(url) as? [String: Any],
              let docs = root["docs"] as? [[String: Any]] else { return nil }
        let want = Similarity.normalize(series)
        for doc in docs {
            guard let key = doc["key"] as? String, let title = doc["title"] as? String,
                  Similarity.score(title, series) >= 55, !want.isEmpty,
                  let workURL = URL(string: "https://openlibrary.org\(key).json"),
                  let work = try? await HTTP.json(workURL) as? [String: Any] else { continue }
            var text: String?
            if let d = work["description"] as? String { text = d }
            else if let o = work["description"] as? [String: Any] { text = o["value"] as? String }
            guard var t = text else { continue }
            t = t.components(separatedBy: "\n----").first ?? t
            t = TextClean.shorten(TextClean.html(t), limit: 900)
            if t.count < 60 { continue }
            var out = CompanionData()
            let page = "https://openlibrary.org\(key)"
            out.seriesSummary = TextBlock(text: t, source: "Open Library", url: page)
            out.links.append(LinkItem(title: "Open Library: " + title, url: page))
            out.sources.append("Open Library")
            return out
        }
        return nil
    }
}

// MARK: - Wikipedia (no key needed)

enum WikipediaProvider {

    struct Page {
        var title: String
        var extract: String
        var url: String?
        var imageURL: String?
    }

    static func lookup(query: String, mustMention: [String]) async -> Page? {
        guard let searchURL = HTTP.url("https://en.wikipedia.org/w/api.php", [
            ("action", "query"), ("list", "search"), ("srsearch", query), ("srlimit", "4"), ("format", "json")
        ]) else { return nil }
        guard let root = try? await HTTP.json(searchURL) as? [String: Any],
              let q = root["query"] as? [String: Any],
              let hits = q["search"] as? [[String: Any]] else { return nil }

        for hit in hits {
            guard let title = hit["title"] as? String else { continue }
            let slug = title.replacingOccurrences(of: " ", with: "_")
            let encoded = slug.addingPercentEncoding(withAllowedCharacters: .urlPathAllowed) ?? slug
            guard let url = URL(string: "https://en.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/" + encoded),
                  let obj = try? await HTTP.json(url) as? [String: Any] else { continue }
            if (obj["type"] as? String) == "disambiguation" { continue }
            guard let extract = obj["extract"] as? String, extract.count > 40 else { continue }
            let lower = extract.lowercased()
            if !mustMention.contains(where: { lower.contains($0) }) { continue }

            var page = Page(title: title, extract: extract, url: nil, imageURL: nil)
            if let urls = obj["content_urls"] as? [String: Any],
               let desktop = urls["desktop"] as? [String: Any] {
                page.url = desktop["page"] as? String
            }
            if let thumb = obj["thumbnail"] as? [String: Any] {
                page.imageURL = thumb["source"] as? String
            }
            return page
        }
        return nil
    }

    static func series(name: String, publisher: String?) async -> CompanionData? {
        var query = name + " comic series"
        if let p = publisher, !p.isEmpty { query = name + " " + p + " comic series" }
        guard let page = await lookup(query: query, mustMention: ["comic", "manga", "graphic novel", "series"]) else { return nil }
        var out = CompanionData()
        out.seriesSummary = TextBlock(text: TextClean.shorten(page.extract, limit: 900), source: "Wikipedia", url: page.url)
        if let u = page.url { out.links.append(LinkItem(title: "Wikipedia: " + page.title, url: u)) }
        out.sources.append("Wikipedia")
        return out
    }

    static func character(name: String, publisher: String?) async -> CharacterCard? {
        var query = name + " comics character"
        if let p = publisher, !p.isEmpty { query = name + " " + p + " character" }
        guard let page = await lookup(query: query, mustMention: ["fictional", "character", "superhero", "villain", "comic", "manga"]) else { return nil }
        return CharacterCard(
            name: name,
            role: nil,
            blurb: TextClean.shorten(page.extract, limit: 520),
            imageURL: page.imageURL,
            url: page.url,
            source: "Wikipedia"
        )
    }
}

// MARK: - Marvel / DC fan wikis (no key needed, best effort)

enum FandomProvider {

    static func host(for publisher: String?) -> (host: String, name: String)? {
        let p = (publisher ?? "").lowercased()
        if p.contains("marvel") { return ("marvel.fandom.com", "Marvel Database") }
        if p == "dc" || p.contains("dc comics") || p.contains("vertigo") || p.contains("dc black label") {
            return ("dc.fandom.com", "DC Database")
        }
        return nil
    }

    static func lookup(publisher: String?, series: String, volume: String?, issue: String) async -> CompanionData? {
        guard let site = host(for: publisher) else { return nil }
        let vol = (volume?.isEmpty == false) ? volume! : "1"

        var candidates: [String] = ["\(series) Vol \(vol) \(issue)"]
        if vol != "1" { candidates.append("\(series) Vol 1 \(issue)") }

        // Fall back to the wiki's search if the guessed page name is wrong.
        if let searchURL = HTTP.url("https://\(site.host)/api.php", [
            ("action", "query"), ("list", "search"), ("srsearch", "\(series) \(issue)"), ("srlimit", "5"), ("format", "json")
        ]), let root = try? await HTTP.json(searchURL) as? [String: Any],
           let q = root["query"] as? [String: Any], let hits = q["search"] as? [[String: Any]] {
            for h in hits {
                guard let t = h["title"] as? String else { continue }
                let lower = t.lowercased()
                if lower.hasSuffix(" " + issue) && lower.contains(series.lowercased()) && !candidates.contains(t) {
                    candidates.append(t)
                }
            }
        }

        for title in candidates {
            guard let parseURL = HTTP.url("https://\(site.host)/api.php", [
                ("action", "parse"), ("page", title), ("prop", "wikitext"), ("redirects", "1"),
                ("format", "json"), ("formatversion", "2")
            ]), let root = try? await HTTP.json(parseURL) as? [String: Any],
               let parse = root["parse"] as? [String: Any] else { continue }

            var wikitext: String?
            if let s = parse["wikitext"] as? String {
                wikitext = s
            } else if let o = parse["wikitext"] as? [String: Any] {
                wikitext = o["*"] as? String
            }
            guard let text = wikitext else { continue }
            let synopsis = extractSynopsis(text)
            let pageTitle = (parse["title"] as? String) ?? title
            let slug = pageTitle.replacingOccurrences(of: " ", with: "_")
            let encoded = slug.addingPercentEncoding(withAllowedCharacters: .urlPathAllowed) ?? slug
            let pageURL = "https://\(site.host)/wiki/\(encoded)"

            var out = CompanionData()
            out.links.append(LinkItem(title: "\(site.name): \(pageTitle)", url: pageURL))
            if let s = synopsis {
                out.issueSynopsis = TextBlock(text: TextClean.shorten(s, limit: 2200), source: site.name, url: pageURL)
            }
            out.sources.append(site.name)
            out.creators = credits(in: text)
            out.characters = appearances(in: text, host: site.host)
            return out
        }
        return nil
    }

    private static func linkNames(_ raw: String) -> [String] {
        var names: [String] = []
        let ns = raw as NSString
        if let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: "\\[\\[([^\\]|#]+)(?:\\|([^\\]]*))?\\]\\]") {
            for m in re.matches(in: raw, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length)) {
                var shown = m.range(at: 2).location != NSNotFound ? ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 2)) : ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 1))
                shown = shown.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\s*\\(.*\\)$", with: "", options: .regularExpression)
                shown = TextClean.tidy(shown)
                if !shown.isEmpty { names.append(shown) }
            }
        }
        return names
    }

    /// | Writer1_1 = [[Name]] style infobox fields.
    static func credits(in wikitext: String) -> [Credit] {
        let fields: [(String, String)] = [("Writer", "Writer"), ("Penciler", "Penciller"), ("Inker", "Inker"),
                                          ("Colourist", "Colorist"), ("Letterer", "Letterer"), ("Editor", "Editor"),
                                          ("CoverArtist", "Cover")]
        var out: [Credit] = []
        for (field, role) in fields {
            let pattern = "\\|\\s*" + field + "\\d*(?:_\\d+)?\\s*=\\s*([^\\n]*)"
            guard let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) else { continue }
            let ns = wikitext as NSString
            for m in re.matches(in: wikitext, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length)) where m.numberOfRanges > 1 {
                let raw = ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 1))
                var found = linkNames(raw)
                if found.isEmpty {
                    let plain = TextClean.wiki(raw)
                    if plain.count > 1 && plain.count < 40 && plain != "?" { found = [plain] }
                }
                for n in found where !out.contains(where: { $0.name == n && $0.role == role }) {
                    out.append(Credit(role: role, name: n))
                }
            }
        }
        return out
    }

    /// The "Appearing in" lists: featured characters first, then supporting, then villains.
    static func appearances(in wikitext: String, host: String) -> [CharacterCard] {
        var out: [CharacterCard] = []
        let pattern = "(?:Featured|Supporting|Antagonists|Villains)\\s*Characters?:?\\s*\\n(.*?)(?=\\n\\s*(?:Featured|Supporting|Antagonists|Villains|Other|Races|Locations|Items|Vehicles|Synopsis|Solicit|Notes|Trivia|\\|)|\\n==|\\z)"
        guard let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern, options: [.dotMatchesLineSeparators, .caseInsensitive]) else { return [] }
        let ns = wikitext as NSString
        for m in re.matches(in: wikitext, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length)) where m.numberOfRanges > 1 {
            for name in linkNames(ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 1))) where !out.contains(where: { CompanionData.same($0.name, name) }) {
                out.append(CharacterCard(name: name, role: nil, blurb: nil, imageURL: nil, url: nil, source: nil))
            }
        }
        return Array(out.prefix(20))
    }

    /// Pulls the "Synopsis" parts out of the wiki page's source.
    static func extractSynopsis(_ wikitext: String) -> String? {
        var parts: [String] = []

        // Template style: | Synopsis1 = text...
        let templatePattern = "\\|\\s*Synopsis\\d*\\s*=\\s*(.*?)(?=\\n\\s*\\|\\s*[A-Za-z0-9_]+\\s*=|\\n\\s*\\}\\})"
        if let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: templatePattern, options: [.dotMatchesLineSeparators]) {
            let ns = wikitext as NSString
            let matches = re.matches(in: wikitext, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length))
            for m in matches where m.numberOfRanges > 1 {
                let raw = ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 1))
                let cleaned = TextClean.wiki(raw)
                if cleaned.count > 40 && !cleaned.lowercased().contains("not yet written") {
                    parts.append(cleaned)
                }
            }
        }

        // Heading style: == Synopsis for "Title" == ...
        if parts.isEmpty {
            let headingPattern = "==+\\s*Synopsis[^=]*==+\\s*\\n(.*?)(?=\\n==|\\z)"
            if let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: headingPattern, options: [.dotMatchesLineSeparators]) {
                let ns = wikitext as NSString
                let matches = re.matches(in: wikitext, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length))
                for m in matches where m.numberOfRanges > 1 {
                    let cleaned = TextClean.wiki(ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 1)))
                    if cleaned.count > 40 && !cleaned.lowercased().contains("not yet written") {
                        parts.append(cleaned)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if parts.isEmpty { return nil }
        return parts.joined(separator: "\n\n")
    }
}

// MARK: - AniList (manga, no key needed)

enum AniListProvider {

    static func lookup(series: String, strict: Bool) async -> CompanionData? {
        guard let url = URL(string: "https://graphql.anilist.co") else { return nil }
        let query = """
        query ($s: String) {
          Media(search: $s, type: MANGA) {
            siteUrl
            title { romaji english }
            description(asHtml: false)
            genres
            coverImage { large }
            staff(perPage: 6) { edges { role node { name { full } } } }
            characters(perPage: 12, sort: ROLE) { nodes { name { full } image { medium } siteUrl } }
          }
        }
        """
        let payload: [String: Any] = ["query": query, "variables": ["s": series]]
        guard let body = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: payload) else { return nil }
        guard let root = try? await HTTP.json(url, method: "POST", body: body, headers: ["Content-Type": "application/json"]) as? [String: Any],
              let data = root["data"] as? [String: Any],
              let media = data["Media"] as? [String: Any] else { return nil }

        if strict {
            var names: [String] = []
            if let t = media["title"] as? [String: Any] {
                if let r = t["romaji"] as? String { names.append(r) }
                if let e = t["english"] as? String { names.append(e) }
            }
            let ok = names.contains(where: { Similarity.score($0, series) >= 55 })
            if !ok { return nil }
        }

        var out = CompanionData()
        if let desc = media["description"] as? String, !desc.isEmpty {
            out.seriesSummary = TextBlock(text: TextClean.shorten(TextClean.html(desc), limit: 900), source: "AniList", url: media["siteUrl"] as? String)
        }
        if let g = media["genres"] as? [String] { out.genres = g }
        if let cover = media["coverImage"] as? [String: Any] { out.coverURL = cover["large"] as? String }
        if let staff = media["staff"] as? [String: Any], let edges = staff["edges"] as? [[String: Any]] {
            for e in edges {
                guard let node = e["node"] as? [String: Any], let name = (node["name"] as? [String: Any])?["full"] as? String else { continue }
                out.creators.append(Credit(role: (e["role"] as? String) ?? "Staff", name: name))
            }
        }
        if let chars = media["characters"] as? [String: Any], let nodes = chars["nodes"] as? [[String: Any]] {
            for n in nodes {
                guard let name = (n["name"] as? [String: Any])?["full"] as? String else { continue }
                let img = (n["image"] as? [String: Any])?["medium"] as? String
                out.characters.append(CharacterCard(name: name, role: nil, blurb: nil, imageURL: img, url: n["siteUrl"] as? String, source: "AniList"))
            }
        }
        if let site = media["siteUrl"] as? String { out.links.append(LinkItem(title: "Series on AniList", url: site)) }
        out.sources.append("AniList")
        return out
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/Console.swift =====
import Foundation

/// A tiny logger for development builds. It prints to the Xcode console and keeps nothing.
enum Console {
    static func log(_ message: String) {
        #if DEBUG
        print("[PanelSnap] \(message)")
        #endif
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/DiscoverModel.swift =====
import Foundation
import WebKit

/// One comic on the weekly release list.
struct DiscoverComic: Identifiable, Hashable {
    let id: String             // the League of Comic Geeks page address
    var title: String          // "Batman #105"
    var publisher: String
    var price: String
    var summary: String
    var cover: URL?
    var pulls: Int             // how many readers have it on their pull list: a popularity measure
    var rating: Int?

    var listingURL: URL? { return URL(string: id) }

    /// What to type into a download site's search box: the series name and issue number, without variant wording.
    var searchTerm: String {
        var t = title.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\(.*?\\)", with: " ", options: .regularExpression)
        for marker in [" Cover ", " Variant", " Virgin", " Foil", " 1:", " Facsimile", " Direct Market"] {
            if let r = t.range(of: marker, options: .caseInsensitive) { t = String(t[..<r.lowerBound]) }
        }
        t = t.replacingOccurrences(of: "#", with: " ").replacingOccurrences(of: "\\s+", with: " ", options: .regularExpression)
        return t.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
    }
}

enum ReleaseWeek: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
    case previous, current, next
    var id: String { return rawValue }
    var label: String {
        switch self {
        case .previous: return "Last Week"
        case .current: return "This Week"
        case .next: return "Next Week"
        }
    }
    var dayOffset: Int {
        switch self {
        case .previous: return -7
        case .current: return 0
        case .next: return 7
        }
    }
}

enum DiscoverSort: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
    case popular, title
    var id: String { return rawValue }
    var label: String { return self == .popular ? "Popular" : "A–Z" }
}

/// Weekly comic-shop release lists from League of Comic Geeks, which lists every week's new comics with covers
/// and how many readers pull each one. It has no official public API, so this reads the same list its own website
/// shows (the way the open-source comicgeeks module does), and asks politely: one request per week, kept for an hour.
@MainActor
final class DiscoverModel: ObservableObject {
    /// One shared copy, so the lists are already loaded by the time Discover is opened.
    static let shared = DiscoverModel()
    @Published var weeks: [ReleaseWeek: [DiscoverComic]] = [:]
    @Published var loadingWeeks = Set<ReleaseWeek>()
    @Published var failedWeeks = Set<ReleaseWeek>()
    @Published var forYou: [DiscoverComic] = []
    @Published var searchResults: [DiscoverComic] = []
    @Published var searching = false

    private var loadedAt: [ReleaseWeek: Date] = [:]
    private let browser = WebJSONFetcher()
    private var recommendedFor = ""

    func load(_ week: ReleaseWeek, force: Bool = false) async {
        if !force, weeks[week] != nil, let t = loadedAt[week], Date().timeIntervalSince(t) < 3600 { return }
        loadingWeeks.insert(week)
        failedWeeks.remove(week)
        let list = await fetch(Self.url(["list": "releases", "list_option": "thumbs", "view": "list",
                                         "date": Self.releaseDay(week), "date_type": "week"]))
        if let list = list, !list.isEmpty {
            weeks[week] = list
            loadedAt[week] = Date()
        } else if weeks[week] == nil {
            failedWeeks.insert(week)
        }
        loadingWeeks.remove(week)
    }

    /// Loads what the For You page shows. Called when the app opens, so Discover is ready when it is.
    func prefetch(libraryTitles: [String]) async {
        async let a: Void = load(.current)
        async let b: Void = load(.next)
        _ = await (a, b)
        await recommend(libraryTitles: libraryTitles)
    }

    /// Searches the whole catalogue, old and new.
    func search(_ query: String) async {
        let q = query.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        guard q.count >= 2 else { searchResults = []; return }
        searching = true
        let list = await fetch(Self.url(["list": "search", "title": q, "list_option": "series"]))
        if q == query.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) { searchResults = list ?? [] }
        searching = false
    }

    /// "You may also like": comics in the same series families as the ones you read, that are not already in your
    /// library, plus new releases that share words with your titles.
    func recommend(libraryTitles: [String]) async {
        let names = Self.seriesNames(from: libraryTitles)
        let key = names.joined(separator: "|")
        guard !names.isEmpty, key != recommendedFor else { return }
        recommendedFor = key
        var picks: [DiscoverComic] = []
        let owned = Set(names.map { Self.normal($0) })
        for name in names.prefix(4) {
            for c in (await fetch(Self.url(["list": "search", "title": name, "list_option": "series"])) ?? []).prefix(8) {
                let n = Self.normal(c.title)
                if owned.contains(n) || picks.contains(where: { $0.id == c.id }) { continue }
                picks.append(c)
            }
        }
        // New releases that mention the same words (a character or a team) as the library.
        await load(.current)
        await load(.next)
        let words = Set(names.flatMap { Self.significantWords($0) })
        let fresh = ((weeks[.current] ?? []) + (weeks[.next] ?? []))
            .map { c -> (DiscoverComic, Int) in (c, Self.significantWords(c.title).filter { words.contains($0) }.count) }
            .filter { $0.1 > 0 && !owned.contains(Self.normal($0.0.title)) }
            .sorted { $0.1 != $1.1 ? $0.1 > $1.1 : $0.0.pulls > $1.0.pulls }
            .map { $0.0 }
        var out: [DiscoverComic] = []
        for c in fresh.prefix(6) + picks where !out.contains(where: { $0.id == c.id }) { out.append(c) }
        forYou = Array(out.prefix(24))
    }

    // MARK: Library titles

    /// "Avengers Disassembled #3 (2006) (digital)" -> "Avengers Disassembled".
    static func seriesNames(from titles: [String]) -> [String] {
        var counts: [String: Int] = [:]
        var order: [String] = []
        for t in titles {
            var s = t.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\(.*?\\)|\\[.*?\\]", with: " ", options: .regularExpression)
            if let r = s.range(of: "#\\s*\\d", options: .regularExpression) { s = String(s[..<r.lowerBound]) }
            s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\b(vol(ume)?\\.?|v)\\s*\\d+.*$", with: " ", options: [.regularExpression, .caseInsensitive])
            s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "[_\\.]", with: " ", options: .regularExpression)
            s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\s+", with: " ", options: .regularExpression).trimmingCharacters(in: CharacterSet(charactersIn: " -–"))
            if s.count < 3 { continue }
            if counts[s] == nil { order.append(s) }
            counts[s, default: 0] += 1
        }
        return order
    }

    static func normal(_ title: String) -> String {
        return title.lowercased().replacingOccurrences(of: "[^a-z0-9]+", with: " ", options: .regularExpression).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
    }

    private static let filler: Set<String> = ["the", "and", "of", "a", "an", "to", "in", "vol", "tp", "hc", "comic", "comics", "issue", "all", "one", "new", "marvel", "dc", "image"]
    static func significantWords(_ title: String) -> [String] {
        return normal(title).split(separator: " ").map(String.init).filter { $0.count > 2 && !filler.contains($0) && Int($0) == nil }
    }

    // MARK: Requests

    private static func url(_ params: [String: String]) -> URL? {
        var c = URLComponents(string: "https://leagueofcomicgeeks.com/comic/get_comics")
        c?.queryItems = params.sorted { $0.key < $1.key }.map { URLQueryItem(name: $0.key, value: $0.value) }
        return c?.url
    }

    /// The Wednesday (new-comic day) of the chosen week, as YYYY-MM-DD.
    private static func releaseDay(_ week: ReleaseWeek) -> String {
        let cal = Calendar(identifier: .gregorian)
        let day = cal.date(byAdding: .day, value: week.dayOffset, to: Date()) ?? Date()
        let weekday = cal.component(.weekday, from: day)            // Sunday = 1 ... Wednesday = 4
        let wednesday = cal.date(byAdding: .day, value: 4 - weekday, to: day) ?? day
        let f = DateFormatter()
        f.calendar = cal
        f.locale = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")
        f.dateFormat = "yyyy-MM-dd"
        return f.string(from: wednesday)
    }

    private func fetch(_ url: URL?) async -> [DiscoverComic]? {
        guard let url = url else { return nil }
        // First a plain request; if the site wants a real browser, use one (a hidden web page) instead.
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.timeoutInterval = 25
        req.setValue("Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 18_0 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 (KHTML, like Gecko) Version/18.0 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1", forHTTPHeaderField: "User-Agent")
        if let (data, response) = try? await URLSession.shared.data(for: req),
           (response as? HTTPURLResponse).map({ (200..<300).contains($0.statusCode) }) ?? false {
            let list = Self.parse(data)
            if !list.isEmpty { return list }
        }
        if let text = await browser.fetch(url), let data = text.data(using: .utf8) {
            let list = Self.parse(data)
            if !list.isEmpty { return list }
        }
        return nil
    }

    // MARK: Reading the list

    /// The answer is JSON holding one block of HTML, a list item per comic.
    static func parse(_ data: Data) -> [DiscoverComic] {
        guard let root = try? JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data) as? [String: Any],
              let html = root["list"] as? String else { return [] }
        var out: [DiscoverComic] = []
        var seen = Set<String>()
        for chunk in html.components(separatedBy: "<li").dropFirst() {
            let item = chunk
            let head = String(item.prefix(upTo: item.firstIndex(of: ">") ?? item.endIndex))
            guard let title = text(of: ["title", "color-primary"], in: item) ?? text(of: ["title"], in: item) ?? item.firstMatch(#"<a[^>]*href="/comics?/[^"]*"[^>]*>([^<]{2,})</a>"#).map(clean), !title.isEmpty else { continue }
            let description = element(["comic-description", "col-feed-max"], in: item)
            var link = ""
            if let d = description, let m = d.firstMatch(#"href="([^"]+)""#) { link = m }
            if link.isEmpty, let m = item.firstMatch(#"href="(/comics?/[^"]+)""#) { link = m }
            let id = link.hasPrefix("http") ? link : (link.isEmpty ? title : "https://leagueofcomicgeeks.com" + link)
            if !seen.insert(id).inserted { continue }
            var summary = ""
            if let d = description {
                summary = d.replacingOccurrences(of: "<a\\b[^>]*>.*?</a>", with: " ", options: [.regularExpression, .caseInsensitive])
                summary = clean(summary)
            }
            var cover: URL?
            let artZone = item.range(of: "comic-cover-art").map { String(item[$0.upperBound...]) } ?? item
            if let src = artZone.firstMatch(#"data-src="([^"]+)""#) ?? artZone.firstMatch(#"<img[^>]+src="([^"]+)""#) {
                var s = src.replacingOccurrences(of: "medium", with: "large")
                if s.hasPrefix("/") { s = "https://leagueofcomicgeeks.com" + s.replacingOccurrences(of: "-med", with: "-lg") }
                cover = URL(string: s)
            }
            let details = text(of: ["comic-details"], in: item) ?? ""
            let publisher = details.components(separatedBy: "·").first?.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines) ?? ""
            let price = text(of: ["price"], in: item) ?? ""
            let pulls = Int(head.firstMatch(#"data-pulls="([^"]*)""#) ?? "") ?? 0
            let rating = Int(head.firstMatch(#"data-community="([^"]*)""#) ?? "")
            out.append(DiscoverComic(id: id, title: title, publisher: publisher, price: price, summary: summary,
                                     cover: cover, pulls: pulls, rating: rating))
        }
        return out
    }

    /// The inside of the first tag whose class list has all of these classes.
    private static func element(_ classes: [String], in html: String) -> String? {
        guard let tags = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: #"<(\w+)\b[^>]*class="([^"]*)"[^>]*>"#, options: [.caseInsensitive]) else { return nil }
        let ns = html as NSString
        for m in tags.matches(in: html, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length)) {
            let have = Set(ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 2)).split(separator: " ").map(String.init))
            guard classes.allSatisfy({ have.contains($0) }) else { continue }
            let name = ns.substring(with: m.range(at: 1))
            let start = m.range.location + m.range.length
            let rest = ns.substring(from: start)
            let end = rest.range(of: "</" + name + ">", options: .caseInsensitive)?.lowerBound ?? rest.endIndex
            return String(rest[..<end])
        }
        return nil
    }

    private static func text(of classes: [String], in html: String) -> String? {
        guard let inner = element(classes, in: html) else { return nil }
        return clean(inner)
    }

    private static func clean(_ html: String) -> String {
        var s = html.replacingOccurrences(of: "<[^>]+>", with: " ", options: .regularExpression)
        for (a, b) in [("&amp;", "&"), ("&#039;", "'"), ("&#39;", "'"), ("&quot;", "\""), ("&nbsp;", " "), ("&lt;", "<"), ("&gt;", ">"), ("&rsquo;", "’"), ("&ndash;", "–")] {
            s = s.replacingOccurrences(of: a, with: b)
        }
        return s.replacingOccurrences(of: "\\s+", with: " ", options: .regularExpression).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
    }
}

private extension String {
    /// The first capture group of the first match.
    func firstMatch(_ pattern: String) -> String? {
        guard let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern),
              let m = re.firstMatch(in: self, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: (self as NSString).length)),
              m.numberOfRanges > 1 else { return nil }
        return (self as NSString).substring(with: m.range(at: 1))
    }
}

/// Reads a JSON address through a hidden web page, for sites that only talk to a real browser.
@MainActor
final class WebJSONFetcher: NSObject, WKNavigationDelegate {
    private var webView: WKWebView?
    private var continuation: CheckedContinuation<String?, Never>?

    func fetch(_ url: URL) async -> String? {
        return await withCheckedContinuation { (c: CheckedContinuation<String?, Never>) in
            finish(nil)
            continuation = c
            let wv = WKWebView(frame: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 320, height: 480))
            wv.navigationDelegate = self
            webView = wv
            wv.load(URLRequest(url: url))
            Task { [weak self] in
                try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 25_000_000_000)
                self?.finish(nil)
            }
        }
    }

    func webView(_ webView: WKWebView, didFinish navigation: WKNavigation!) {
        // A "checking your browser" page may come first; only the JSON answer is accepted.
        webView.evaluateJavaScript("document.body ? document.body.innerText : ''") { [weak self] result, _ in
            let text = (result as? String) ?? ""
            if text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).hasPrefix("{") { self?.finish(text) }
        }
    }

    private func finish(_ text: String?) {
        guard let c = continuation else { return }
        continuation = nil
        webView?.stopLoading()
        webView = nil
        c.resume(returning: text)
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/FilenameParser.swift =====
import Foundation

/// Turns file names like "Batman 012 (2016) (Digital).cbz" into series / issue / year.
enum FilenameParser {

    static func parse(_ fileName: String) -> ComicIdentity {
        var name = fileName
        if let dot = name.lastIndex(of: "."), name.distance(from: dot, to: name.endIndex) <= 6 {
            name = String(name[name.startIndex..<dot])
        }
        name = name.replacingOccurrences(of: "_", with: " ")

        var identity = ComicIdentity()

        // Year in parentheses, e.g. (2016)
        if let m = firstMatch("\\((19\\d{2}|20\\d{2})\\)", in: name) {
            identity.year = Int(m)
        }

        // Remove (...) and [...] groups.
        var cleaned = replace("\\([^)]*\\)", in: name, with: " ")
        cleaned = replace("\\[[^\\]]*\\]", in: cleaned, with: " ")
        cleaned = cleaned.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)

        // Volume: v2, vol 2, volume 2
        if let vol = firstMatch("(?i)\\bv(?:ol(?:ume)?)?\\.?\\s*(\\d{1,3})\\b", in: cleaned, group: 1) {
            identity.volume = vol
            cleaned = replace("(?i)\\bv(?:ol(?:ume)?)?\\.?\\s*\\d{1,3}\\b", in: cleaned, with: " ")
        }

        // Issue: "#12", "No. 12", "Issue 12", or a trailing number.
        var issue: String?
        var seriesPart = cleaned
        if let m = firstMatchRange("(?i)(?:#|\\bno\\.?\\s*|\\bissue\\s*)(\\d{1,4}(?:\\.\\d)?)", in: cleaned) {
            issue = m.number
            seriesPart = String(cleaned[cleaned.startIndex..<m.start])
        } else if let m = firstMatchRange("(?:^|[\\s-])(\\d{1,4}(?:\\.\\d)?)\\s*$", in: cleaned) {
            // A trailing 4-digit number that looks like a year ("Batman 2016") is the year, not issue #2016.
            if let n = Int(m.number), m.number.count == 4, (1900...2099).contains(n) {
                if identity.year == nil { identity.year = n }
                seriesPart = String(cleaned[cleaned.startIndex..<m.start])
            } else {
                issue = m.number
                seriesPart = String(cleaned[cleaned.startIndex..<m.start])
            }
        }

        if let i = issue {
            // Normalise "012" -> "12"
            if let n = Int(i) { identity.issue = String(n) } else { identity.issue = i }
        }

        seriesPart = replace("[\\s\\-:–—]+$", in: seriesPart, with: "")
        seriesPart = replace("\\s{2,}", in: seriesPart, with: " ")
        seriesPart = seriesPart.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        identity.series = seriesPart.isEmpty ? nil : seriesPart
        if identity.series == nil {
            let t = cleaned.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            identity.series = t.isEmpty ? nil : t
        }
        return identity
    }

    // MARK: - regex helpers

    private struct Found {
        var number: String
        var start: String.Index
    }

    private static func firstMatch(_ pattern: String, in text: String, group: Int = 1) -> String? {
        guard let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) else { return nil }
        let ns = text as NSString
        guard let m = re.firstMatch(in: text, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length)) else { return nil }
        let idx = m.numberOfRanges > group ? group : 0
        let r = m.range(at: idx)
        if r.location == NSNotFound { return nil }
        return ns.substring(with: r)
    }

    private static func firstMatchRange(_ pattern: String, in text: String) -> Found? {
        guard let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) else { return nil }
        let ns = text as NSString
        guard let m = re.firstMatch(in: text, range: NSRange(location: 0, length: ns.length)) else { return nil }
        let whole = m.range(at: 0)
        let num = m.range(at: 1)
        if whole.location == NSNotFound || num.location == NSNotFound { return nil }
        guard let startIdx = Range(whole, in: text)?.lowerBound else { return nil }
        return Found(number: ns.substring(with: num), start: startIdx)
    }

    private static func replace(_ pattern: String, in text: String, with template: String) -> String {
        guard let re = try? NSRegularExpression(pattern: pattern) else { return text }
        let range = NSRange(location: 0, length: (text as NSString).length)
        return re.stringByReplacingMatches(in: text, range: range, withTemplate: template)
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/IdentityBuilder.swift =====
import Foundation

enum IdentityBuilder {
    /// Combines what the file name says with what the file's own ComicInfo.xml says (the latter wins).
    static func make(originalName: String, source: ComicSource?) -> ComicIdentity {
        var id = FilenameParser.parse(originalName)
        guard let xml = source?.metadataXML(), let info = ComicInfoParser.parse(xml) else {
            return id
        }
        if let v = info.series, !v.isEmpty { id.series = v }
        if let v = info.issue, !v.isEmpty { id.issue = v }
        if let v = info.title, !v.isEmpty { id.title = v }
        if let v = info.volume, !v.isEmpty { id.volume = v }
        if let v = info.year { id.year = v }
        id.publisher = info.publisher ?? id.publisher
        id.imprint = info.imprint
        id.summary = info.summary
        id.writers = info.writers
        id.pencillers = info.pencillers
        id.inkers = info.inkers
        id.colorists = info.colorists
        id.letterers = info.letterers
        id.coverArtists = info.coverArtists
        id.editors = info.editors
        id.genres = info.genres
        id.characters = info.characters
        id.teams = info.teams
        id.locations = info.locations
        id.storyArc = info.storyArc
        id.seriesGroup = info.seriesGroup
        id.web = info.web
        id.isManga = info.isManga
        id.rtl = info.rtl
        id.ageRating = info.ageRating
        id.languageISO = info.languageISO
        return id
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/LibraryStore.swift =====
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import ImageIO

@MainActor
final class LibraryStore: ObservableObject {

    @Published private(set) var comics: [ComicEntry] = []
    /// Shelf names, in the order they are shown. Comics point at a shelf by name.
    @Published private(set) var shelves: [String] = UserDefaults.standard.stringArray(forKey: "shelves") ?? []
    @Published var isImporting = false
    @Published var importStatus: String?
    @Published var errorMessage: String?

    private var saveWork: Task<Void, Never>?

    init() {
        load()
    }

    // MARK: persistence

    private func load() {
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: Paths.libraryFile) else { return }
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        if let list = try? decoder.decode([ComicEntry].self, from: data) {
            // Drop entries whose file vanished.
            comics = list.filter { FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: fileURL(for: $0).path) }
            // A shelf that comics point at must exist, even if the list of shelves was lost.
            for name in comics.compactMap({ $0.shelf }) where !shelves.contains(name) { shelves.append(name) }
        }
    }

    private func saveShelves() {
        UserDefaults.standard.set(shelves, forKey: "shelves")
    }

    /// Writes the library right now (used when the app goes to the background).
    func flush() {
        saveWork?.cancel()
        saveWork = nil
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        if let data = try? encoder.encode(comics) {
            try? data.write(to: Paths.libraryFile, options: .atomic)
        }
    }

    private func save() {
        let snapshot = comics
        saveWork?.cancel()
        saveWork = Task {
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 300_000_000)
            if Task.isCancelled { return }
            let encoder = JSONEncoder()
            encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
            if let data = try? encoder.encode(snapshot) {
                try? data.write(to: Paths.libraryFile, options: .atomic)
            }
        }
    }

    func fileURL(for entry: ComicEntry) -> URL {
        return Paths.comics.appendingPathComponent(entry.fileName)
    }

    func coverURL(for entry: ComicEntry) -> URL {
        return Paths.covers.appendingPathComponent(entry.id.uuidString + ".jpg")
    }

    func entry(id: UUID) -> ComicEntry? {
        return comics.first(where: { $0.id == id })
    }

    // MARK: import

    nonisolated static let comicExtensions: Set<String> = ["cbz", "cbr", "cb7", "cbt", "zip", "rar", "7z", "pdf", "epub"]

    /// The path of a file relative to the Comics folder, or nil if the file lives somewhere else.
    nonisolated static func relativeName(_ url: URL) -> String? {
        let base = Paths.comics.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path + "/"
        let p = url.resolvingSymlinksInPath().path
        return p.hasPrefix(base) ? String(p.dropFirst(base.count)) : nil
    }

    private var failedScan = Set<String>()

    /// Forgets comics whose file was deleted from Files behind our back.
    func pruneMissing() {
        let before = comics.count
        comics.removeAll { !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: fileURL(for: $0).path) }
        if comics.count != before {
            Console.log("library: removed \(before - comics.count) comic(s) whose file is gone")
            save()
        }
    }

    /// Finds comics that were dropped straight into Files > PanelSnap > Comics and adds them in place (no copy).
    func scanFolder() async {
        if isImporting { return }
        pruneMissing()
        let known = Set(comics.map { $0.fileName })
        let skip = failedScan
        let folder = Paths.comics
        let found: [URL] = await Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
            var out: [URL] = []
            let fm = FileManager.default
            guard let en = fm.enumerator(at: folder, includingPropertiesForKeys: [.isRegularFileKey],
                                         options: [.skipsHiddenFiles]) else { return out }
            while let u = en.nextObject() as? URL {
                guard LibraryStore.comicExtensions.contains(u.pathExtension.lowercased()),
                      let rel = LibraryStore.relativeName(u),
                      !known.contains(rel), !skip.contains(rel) else { continue }
                out.append(u)
            }
            return out
        }.value
        Console.log("scan Comics folder: \(found.count) new file(s)")
        if found.isEmpty { return }
        await importFiles(found)
    }

    /// Adds every comic found inside a folder the person picked (including sub-folders).
    func importFolder(_ folder: URL, shelf: String? = nil) async {
        let scoped = folder.startAccessingSecurityScopedResource()
        Console.log("import: folder \(folder.lastPathComponent) scoped=\(scoped)")
        var found: [URL] = []
        if let walker = FileManager.default.enumerator(at: folder, includingPropertiesForKeys: [.isRegularFileKey],
                                                       options: [.skipsHiddenFiles, .skipsPackageDescendants]) {
            for case let u as URL in walker where LibraryStore.comicExtensions.contains(u.pathExtension.lowercased()) {
                found.append(u)
            }
        }
        found.sort { $0.lastPathComponent.localizedStandardCompare($1.lastPathComponent) == .orderedAscending }
        Console.log("import: found \(found.count) comic(s) in the folder")
        if found.isEmpty {
            errorMessage = "No comics (CBZ, CBR, CB7, PDF or EPUB) were found in that folder."
        } else {
            await importFiles(found, shelf: shelf)
        }
        if scoped { folder.stopAccessingSecurityScopedResource() }
    }

    func importFiles(_ urls: [URL], shelf: String? = nil) async {
        if urls.isEmpty { return }
        isImporting = true
        var added = 0
        var failed: [String] = []
        Console.log("import: \(urls.count) file(s) requested")
        for (i, url) in urls.enumerated() {
            importStatus = "Adding \(i + 1) of \(urls.count)…"
            let rel = LibraryStore.relativeName(url)
            if let rel = rel, comics.contains(where: { $0.fileName == rel }) {
                Console.log("import: \(url.lastPathComponent) is already in the library")
                continue
            }
            let scoped = url.startAccessingSecurityScopedResource()
            // Same name and same size as something already in the library: it is the same comic, skip it.
            if rel == nil, isDuplicate(url) {
                Console.log("import: \(url.lastPathComponent) is already in the library (same name and size)")
                if scoped { url.stopAccessingSecurityScopedResource() }
                continue
            }
            Console.log("import: \(url.lastPathComponent) scoped=\(scoped) inPlace=\(rel != nil)")
            let result = await Task.detached(priority: .userInitiated) {
                return LibraryStore.importOne(url: url, inPlaceName: rel)
            }.value
            if scoped { url.stopAccessingSecurityScopedResource() }
            switch result {
            case .success(var entry):
                entry.shelf = shelf
                comics.insert(entry, at: 0)
                added += 1
                Console.log("import: added \(entry.title) (\(entry.pageCount) pages)")
            case .failure(let err):
                failed.append("\(url.lastPathComponent): \(err.localizedDescription)")
                if let rel = rel { failedScan.insert(rel) }
                Console.log("import: ERROR \(url.lastPathComponent): \(err)")
            }
        }
        save()
        isImporting = false
        importStatus = nil
        Console.log("import: done, added \(added), failed \(failed.count)")
        if !failed.isEmpty {
            errorMessage = failed.joined(separator: "\n")
        }
    }

    private func fileSize(_ url: URL) -> Int64? {
        let v = try? url.resourceValues(forKeys: [.fileSizeKey])
        return v?.fileSize.map { Int64($0) }
    }

    private func isDuplicate(_ url: URL) -> Bool {
        guard let size = fileSize(url) else { return false }
        for c in comics where c.originalName == url.lastPathComponent {
            if fileSize(fileURL(for: c)) == size { return true }
        }
        return false
    }

    nonisolated private static func uniqueDestination(for name: String) -> URL {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let base = (name as NSString).deletingPathExtension
        let ext = (name as NSString).pathExtension
        var candidate = Paths.comics.appendingPathComponent(name)
        var n = 2
        while fm.fileExists(atPath: candidate.path) {
            let next = ext.isEmpty ? "\(base) \(n)" : "\(base) \(n).\(ext)"
            candidate = Paths.comics.appendingPathComponent(next)
            n += 1
        }
        return candidate
    }

    /// Runs off the main thread: copies the file (unless it is already in Comics), reads page count, makes a cover, reads the ComicInfo.
    nonisolated private static func importOne(url: URL, inPlaceName: String?) -> Result<ComicEntry, Error> {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        let t0 = Date()
        let id = UUID()
        let storedName: String
        let dest: URL
        var copied = false
        if let name = inPlaceName {
            storedName = name
            dest = url
        } else {
            dest = uniqueDestination(for: url.lastPathComponent)
            storedName = dest.lastPathComponent
            do {
                // The picker hands us a private temporary copy, so just move it into place (instant, no double copy).
                if url.path.hasPrefix(NSTemporaryDirectory()) || url.path.contains("/tmp/") || url.path.contains("/Inbox/") {
                    do { try fm.moveItem(at: url, to: dest) } catch { try fm.copyItem(at: url, to: dest) }
                } else {
                    try fm.copyItem(at: url, to: dest)
                }
                copied = true
                Console.log("import: copied \(url.lastPathComponent) to Comics in \(String(format: "%.1f", Date().timeIntervalSince(t0)))s")
            } catch {
                return .failure(error)
            }
        }

        let source: ComicSource
        do {
            source = try ComicOpener.open(url: dest)
        } catch {
            if copied { try? fm.removeItem(at: dest) }
            return .failure(error)
        }

        // Identity: embedded info first, then the file name.
        var identity = FilenameParser.parse(url.lastPathComponent)
        if let xml = source.metadataXML(), let info = ComicInfoParser.parse(xml) {
            if let s = info.series, !s.isEmpty { identity.series = s }
            if let n = info.issue, !n.isEmpty { identity.issue = n }
            if let t = info.title, !t.isEmpty { identity.title = t }
            identity.isManga = info.isManga
        }

        // Cover
        if let cover = source.pageImage(at: 0, maxPixel: 560) {
            let ui = UIImage(cgImage: cover)
            if let jpeg = ui.jpegData(compressionQuality: 0.82) {
                let coverURL = Paths.covers.appendingPathComponent(id.uuidString + ".jpg")
                try? jpeg.write(to: coverURL, options: .atomic)
            }
        }

        var title = identity.series ?? (url.deletingPathExtension().lastPathComponent)
        if let n = identity.issue, !n.isEmpty { title += " #" + n }

        let entry = ComicEntry(
            id: id,
            fileName: storedName,
            originalName: url.lastPathComponent,
            title: title,
            series: identity.series,
            issue: identity.issue,
            format: ComicOpener.detectFormat(url: dest),
            pageCount: source.pageCount,
            addedAt: Date(),
            lastOpenedAt: nil,
            lastPage: 0,
            lastPanel: 0,
            finished: false,
            isManga: identity.isManga
        )
        return .success(entry)
    }

    // MARK: edits

    func recordProgress(id: UUID, page: Int, panel: Int, finished: Bool) {
        guard let i = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
        comics[i].lastPage = page
        comics[i].lastPanel = panel
        comics[i].finished = finished
        comics[i].lastOpenedAt = Date()
        save()
    }

    func setManga(id: UUID, _ value: Bool) {
        guard let i = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
        comics[i].isManga = value
        save()
    }

    func markRead(id: UUID, _ read: Bool) {
        guard let i = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
        comics[i].finished = read
        if !read {
            comics[i].lastPage = 0
            comics[i].lastPanel = 0
        }
        save()
    }

    func markRead(ids: Set<UUID>, _ read: Bool) {
        for i in comics.indices where ids.contains(comics[i].id) {
            comics[i].finished = read
            if !read {
                comics[i].lastPage = 0
                comics[i].lastPanel = 0
            }
        }
        save()
    }

    func delete(_ entry: ComicEntry) {
        delete(ids: [entry.id])
    }

    func delete(ids: Set<UUID>) {
        let fm = FileManager.default
        for entry in comics where ids.contains(entry.id) {
            try? fm.removeItem(at: fileURL(for: entry))
            try? fm.removeItem(at: coverURL(for: entry))
            try? fm.removeItem(at: PanelCache.fileURL(for: entry.id))
            try? fm.removeItem(at: CompanionCache.fileURL(for: entry.id))
        }
        comics.removeAll(where: { ids.contains($0.id) })
        save()
    }

    // MARK: organising

    func rename(id: UUID, to title: String) {
        let t = title.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !t.isEmpty, let i = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }) else { return }
        comics[i].title = t
        save()
    }

    /// Creates a shelf (names are unique, ignoring case). Returns the name actually used.
    @discardableResult
    func addShelf(_ name: String) -> String? {
        let n = name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        if n.isEmpty { return nil }
        if let existing = shelves.first(where: { $0.caseInsensitiveCompare(n) == .orderedSame }) { return existing }
        shelves.append(n)
        saveShelves()
        return n
    }

    func renameShelf(_ old: String, to new: String) {
        let n = new.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard !n.isEmpty, let i = shelves.firstIndex(of: old) else { return }
        if n != old, shelves.contains(where: { $0.caseInsensitiveCompare(n) == .orderedSame }) { return }
        shelves[i] = n
        for j in comics.indices where comics[j].shelf == old { comics[j].shelf = n }
        saveShelves()
        save()
    }

    /// Removes the shelf only; its comics stay in the library, just off any shelf.
    func deleteShelf(_ name: String) {
        shelves.removeAll { $0 == name }
        for j in comics.indices where comics[j].shelf == name { comics[j].shelf = nil }
        saveShelves()
        save()
    }

    func move(_ ids: Set<UUID>, toShelf name: String?) {
        for i in comics.indices where ids.contains(comics[i].id) { comics[i].shelf = name }
        save()
    }

    /// Makes the stored order match `ids` (used the first time someone drags a comic while another sort is active).
    func applyOrder(_ ids: [UUID]) {
        let byID = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: comics.map { ($0.id, $0) })
        let ordered = ids.compactMap { byID[$0] }
        if ordered.count == comics.count {
            comics = ordered
            save()
        }
    }

    /// Drag and drop: put `id` where `target` is (the "My order" sort).
    func moveComic(_ id: UUID, onto target: UUID) {
        guard id != target,
              let from = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == id }),
              let to = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == target }) else { return }
        let item = comics.remove(at: from)
        let dest = comics.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == target }) ?? to
        comics.insert(item, at: from < to ? dest + 1 : dest)
        save()
    }
}

/// Cover thumbnails: decoded off the main thread, shrunk, and kept in a size-limited memory cache.
final class CoverCache {
    static let shared = CoverCache()
    private let cache: NSCache<NSString, UIImage> = {
        let c = NSCache<NSString, UIImage>()
        c.totalCostLimit = 64 * 1024 * 1024
        return c
    }()

    /// Instant lookup: only what is already in memory.
    func cached(_ url: URL) -> UIImage? {
        return cache.object(forKey: url.path as NSString)
    }

    func load(_ url: URL) async -> UIImage? {
        if let hit = cached(url) { return hit }
        let img: UIImage? = await Task.detached(priority: .utility) {
            let opts: [CFString: Any] = [
                kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailFromImageAlways: true,
                kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailWithTransform: true,
                kCGImageSourceShouldCacheImmediately: true,
                kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize: 520
            ]
            guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(url as CFURL, nil),
                  let cg = CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex(src, 0, opts as CFDictionary) else { return nil }
            return UIImage(cgImage: cg)
        }.value
        if let img = img {
            cache.setObject(img, forKey: url.path as NSString, cost: Int(img.size.width * img.size.height * 4))
        }
        return img
    }

    /// Synchronous version for one-off places (the Companion header).
    func image(for url: URL) -> UIImage? {
        if let hit = cached(url) { return hit }
        guard let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url), let img = UIImage(data: data) else { return nil }
        cache.setObject(img, forKey: url.path as NSString, cost: Int(img.size.width * img.size.height * 4))
        return img
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/MLPanelDetector.swift =====
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics
import CoreML

/// Finds comic panels with a small neural network, and puts them in reading order with a second small model.
/// Both come from the Inkwell project (huggingface.co/cedarrapidsboy/inkwell-panel-models): a YOLOv8-nano panel
/// detector trained on about 3,800 annotated comic pages (AGPL-3.0), and a pairwise reading-order model (MIT).
/// The detector runs on Apple's own Core ML, which is part of iOS; the ordering model is a few hundred decision
/// nodes evaluated right here, so nothing outside the app is needed.
enum MLPanelDetector {
    private static let side = 640
    private static let confidence: Float = 0.25
    private static let nmsOverlap: CGFloat = 0.45
    private static let lock = NSLock()
    private static var model: MLModel?
    private static var loadFailed = false

    private static func loadModel() -> MLModel? {
        lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
        if let m = model { return m }
        if loadFailed { return nil }
        let config = MLModelConfiguration()
        // The CPU gives exactly the answers the model was checked against; it is quick enough at this size.
        config.computeUnits = .cpuOnly
        do {
            var url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "InkwellPanels", withExtension: "mlmodelc")
            if url == nil, let raw = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "InkwellPanels", withExtension: "mlmodel") {
                // Not compiled at build time: compile it once on the phone and keep the result.
                let kept = Paths.caches.appendingPathComponent("InkwellPanels.mlmodelc")
                if !FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: kept.path) {
                    let tmp = try MLModel.compileModel(at: raw)
                    try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: kept)
                    try FileManager.default.moveItem(at: tmp, to: kept)
                }
                url = kept
            }
            guard let found = url else {
                Console.log("Panel model is missing from the app")
                loadFailed = true
                return nil
            }
            let m = try MLModel(contentsOf: found, configuration: config)
            model = m
            return m
        } catch {
            Console.log("Panel model failed to load: \(error)")
            loadFailed = true
            return nil
        }
    }

    /// Loads the models ahead of time so the first page does not wait for them.
    static func warmUp() {
        DispatchQueue.global(qos: .utility).async { _ = loadModel(); _ = OrderModel.shared }
    }

    /// Panels in reading order as 0...1 rectangles (top-left origin); empty when the page has none; nil if the model could not run.
    static func detect(image: CGImage, rtl: Bool) -> [CGRect]? {
        guard let model = loadModel() else { return nil }
        guard image.width > 8, image.height > 8 else { return nil }

        // The whole page, squeezed to 640 x 640 (the detector was trained on pages stretched this way).
        let count = side * side
        var bytes = [UInt8](repeating: 0, count: count * 4)
        let drawn: Bool = bytes.withUnsafeMutableBytes { buf in
            guard let ctx = CGContext(data: buf.baseAddress, width: side, height: side, bitsPerComponent: 8,
                                      bytesPerRow: side * 4, space: CGColorSpaceCreateDeviceRGB(),
                                      bitmapInfo: CGImageAlphaInfo.noneSkipLast.rawValue) else { return false }
            ctx.interpolationQuality = .high
            ctx.draw(image, in: CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: side, height: side))
            return true
        }
        if !drawn { return nil }

        var rows: [[Float]] = []
        do {
            let input = try MLMultiArray(shape: [1, 3, NSNumber(value: side), NSNumber(value: side)], dataType: .float32)
            let ptr = input.dataPointer.bindMemory(to: Float.self, capacity: count * 3)
            for i in 0..<count {
                ptr[i] = Float(bytes[i * 4]) / 255
                ptr[count + i] = Float(bytes[i * 4 + 1]) / 255
