                ptr[2 * count + i] = Float(bytes[i * 4 + 2]) / 255
            }
            let features = try MLDictionaryFeatureProvider(dictionary: ["images": MLFeatureValue(multiArray: input)])
            let result = try model.prediction(from: features)
            guard let out = result.featureValue(for: "out")?.multiArrayValue else { return nil }
            let dims = out.shape.map { $0.intValue }, strides = out.strides.map { $0.intValue }
            // One row each for centre x, centre y, width, height and confidence, for every candidate box.
            guard dims.count >= 2, dims[dims.count - 2] == 5 else { return nil }
            let n = dims[dims.count - 1]
            let rs = strides[strides.count - 2], cs = strides[strides.count - 1]
            rows = (0..<5).map { r in (0..<n).map { i in readValue(out, r * rs + i * cs) } }
        } catch {
            Console.log("Panel model failed to run: \(error)")
            return nil
        }
        guard rows.count == 5, let n = rows.first?.count, n > 0 else { return nil }
        func value(_ row: Int, _ i: Int) -> Float { rows[row][i] }

        var candidates: [(rect: CGRect, score: Float)] = []
        for i in 0..<n where value(4, i) >= confidence {
            let cx = CGFloat(value(0, i)), cy = CGFloat(value(1, i)), w = CGFloat(value(2, i)), h = CGFloat(value(3, i))
            let s = CGFloat(side)
            let x0 = max(0, (cx - w / 2) / s), y0 = max(0, (cy - h / 2) / s)
            let x1 = min(1, (cx + w / 2) / s), y1 = min(1, (cy + h / 2) / s)
            if x1 - x0 > 0.04 && y1 - y0 > 0.03 {
                candidates.append((CGRect(x: x0, y: y0, width: x1 - x0, height: y1 - y0), value(4, i)))
            }
        }
        // Keep the most confident box of any group that mostly covers the same spot.
        candidates.sort { $0.score > $1.score }
        var boxes: [CGRect] = []
        for c in candidates {
            let a = c.rect.width * c.rect.height
            let clash = boxes.contains { b in
                let inter = c.rect.intersection(b)
                if inter.isNull { return false }
                let ia = inter.width * inter.height
                return ia / (a + b.width * b.height - ia) > nmsOverlap
            }
            if !clash { boxes.append(c.rect) }
        }
        if boxes.isEmpty { return [] }            // it ran and found no panels: a splash page
        return OrderModel.shared.order(boxes, rtl: rtl) ?? order(boxes, rtl: rtl)
    }

    /// One number from the model's answer, whatever type Core ML hands back.
    private static func readValue(_ a: MLMultiArray, _ offset: Int) -> Float {
        switch a.dataType {
        case .float32: return a.dataPointer.bindMemory(to: Float.self, capacity: offset + 1)[offset]
        case .double: return Float(a.dataPointer.bindMemory(to: Double.self, capacity: offset + 1)[offset])
        default: return a[offset].floatValue
        }
    }

    /// Backup reading order (only if the ordering model is missing): cut the page into bands wherever a clean horizontal line exists, otherwise into
    /// columns, and repeat. That keeps a tall panel beside a stack of small ones in the order a person reads them.
    static func order(_ boxes: [CGRect], rtl: Bool) -> [CGRect] {
        if boxes.count <= 1 { return boxes }
        let tol: CGFloat = 0.01
        // Horizontal cut: a y that no box crosses.
        let byTop = boxes.sorted { $0.minY < $1.minY }
        var reach = byTop[0].maxY
        for i in 1..<byTop.count {
            if byTop[i].minY >= reach - tol {
                let cut = (reach + byTop[i].minY) / 2
                let top = boxes.filter { $0.midY < cut }
                let bottom = boxes.filter { $0.midY >= cut }
                if !top.isEmpty && !bottom.isEmpty { return order(top, rtl: rtl) + order(bottom, rtl: rtl) }
            }
            reach = max(reach, byTop[i].maxY)
        }
        // Vertical cut: an x that no box crosses.
        let byLeft = boxes.sorted { $0.minX < $1.minX }
        var right = byLeft[0].maxX
        for i in 1..<byLeft.count {
            if byLeft[i].minX >= right - tol {
                let cut = (right + byLeft[i].minX) / 2
                let left = boxes.filter { $0.midX < cut }
                let rest = boxes.filter { $0.midX >= cut }
                if !left.isEmpty && !rest.isEmpty {
                    return rtl ? order(rest, rtl: rtl) + order(left, rtl: rtl) : order(left, rtl: rtl) + order(rest, rtl: rtl)
                }
            }
            right = max(right, byLeft[i].maxX)
        }
        // Overlapping layout with no clean cut: top to bottom, then along the row.
        return boxes.sorted {
            let a = Int(($0.minY / 0.04).rounded()), b = Int(($1.minY / 0.04).rounded())
            return a != b ? a < b : (rtl ? $0.minX > $1.minX : $0.minX < $1.minX)
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/OrderModel.swift =====
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics

/// Reading order for comic panels, from the Inkwell project's pairwise model (MIT). For every pair of panels it
/// estimates how likely one is to be read before the other, from where they sit and how big they are; a panel's
/// score is the total over all the others, and panels are read from the highest score down. The model is a set of
/// 100 small decision trees, exported to InkwellOrder.json and evaluated here.
final class OrderModel {
    static let shared = OrderModel()
    /// Where to find InkwellOrder.json when it is not in the app bundle (the build machine's command-line check).
    static var resourceFolder: URL?

    private struct Trees: Decodable {
        let roots: [Int]
        let feature: [Int]
        let value: [Double]
        let yes: [Int]
        let no: [Int]
    }
    private let trees: Trees?

    private init() {
        if let url = OrderModel.resourceFolder?.appendingPathComponent("InkwellOrder.json") ?? Bundle.main.url(forResource: "InkwellOrder", withExtension: "json"),
           let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url) {
            trees = try? JSONDecoder().decode(Trees.self, from: data)
        } else {
            trees = nil
        }
        if trees == nil { Console.log("Panel order model is missing from the app") }
    }

    /// Chance that panel `a` is read before panel `b` (features as the model was trained on them).
    private func before(_ f: [Double]) -> Double {
        guard let t = trees else { return 0.5 }
        var total = 0.0
        for root in t.roots {
            var k = root
            while t.feature[k] >= 0 { k = f[t.feature[k]] <= t.value[k] ? t.yes[k] : t.no[k] }
            total += t.value[k]
        }
        return 1 / (1 + exp(-total))
    }

    /// Panels in reading order, or nil if the model is not available. Right-to-left pages are mirrored first,
    /// because the model learned left-to-right reading.
    func order(_ boxes: [CGRect], rtl: Bool) -> [CGRect]? {
        guard trees != nil else { return nil }
        if boxes.count < 2 { return boxes }
        let p = boxes.map { rtl ? CGRect(x: 1 - $0.maxX, y: $0.minY, width: $0.width, height: $0.height) : $0 }
        let heights = p.map { Double($0.height) }.sorted()
        let median = heights.count % 2 == 1 ? heights[heights.count / 2]
                                            : (heights[heights.count / 2 - 1] + heights[heights.count / 2]) / 2
        func overlap(_ a0: CGFloat, _ a1: CGFloat, _ b0: CGFloat, _ b1: CGFloat, _ shorter: CGFloat) -> Double {
            return Double(max(0, min(a1, b1) - max(a0, b0)) / max(shorter, 1e-6))
        }
        func features(_ a: CGRect, _ b: CGRect) -> [Double] {
            let dx = Double(b.midX - a.midX)
            let vOverlap = overlap(a.minY, a.maxY, b.minY, b.maxY, min(a.height, b.height))
            let hOverlap = overlap(a.minX, a.maxX, b.minX, b.maxX, min(a.width, b.width))
            return [dx, Double(b.midY - a.midY), vOverlap, hOverlap,
                    Double(a.height / b.height), Double(a.width / b.width),
                    a.midX < 0.5 ? 1 : 0, b.midX < 0.5 ? 1 : 0, a.midY < 0.5 ? 1 : 0, b.midY < 0.5 ? 1 : 0,
                    Double(a.height) > 1.4 * median ? 1 : 0, Double(b.height) > 1.4 * median ? 1 : 0,
                    Double(boxes.count) / 20, (vOverlap >= 0.3 && dx > 0) ? 1 : 0]
        }
        var score = [Double](repeating: 0, count: boxes.count)
        for i in 0..<boxes.count {
            for j in 0..<boxes.count where j != i { score[i] += before(features(p[i], p[j])) }
        }
        return boxes.indices.sorted { score[$0] != score[$1] ? score[$0] > score[$1] : $0 < $1 }.map { boxes[$0] }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/PDFComicSource.swift =====
import Foundation
import PDFKit
import UIKit

/// PDF comics. Pages are drawn on demand at the size we ask for.
final class PDFComicSource: ComicSource {
    private let document: PDFDocument
    private let lock = NSLock()

    var pageCount: Int { return document.pageCount }

    init(url: URL) throws {
        guard let doc = PDFDocument(url: url), doc.pageCount > 0 else {
            throw ComicError.unreadable
        }
        self.document = doc
    }

    func metadataXML() -> Data? { return nil }

    func pageData(at index: Int) throws -> Data {
        throw ComicError.unsupported("PDF pages are drawn directly.")
    }

    func pageImage(at index: Int, maxPixel: CGFloat) -> CGImage? {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        guard let page = document.page(at: index) else { return nil }
        let box = page.bounds(for: .mediaBox)
        var w = box.width
        var h = box.height
        if page.rotation % 180 != 0 {
            swap(&w, &h)
        }
        if w < 1 || h < 1 { return nil }
        let scale = maxPixel / max(w, h)
        let size = CGSize(width: (w * scale).rounded(), height: (h * scale).rounded())
        let image = page.thumbnail(of: size, for: .mediaBox)
        return image.cgImage
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/PanelCache.swift =====
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics

/// Remembers detected panels so a page is only analysed once.
final class PanelCache {
    private struct Stored: Codable {
        var version: Int
        var pages: [String: [[Double]]]
        var rtl: Bool
    }

    private var stored: Stored
    private let url: URL
    private var dirty = false
    private let lock = NSLock()

    static func fileURL(for id: UUID) -> URL {
        return Paths.panelCache.appendingPathComponent(id.uuidString + "-d3.json")
    }

    init(comicID: UUID, rtl: Bool) {
        url = PanelCache.fileURL(for: comicID)
        if let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url),
           let s = try? JSONDecoder().decode(Stored.self, from: data),
           s.version == PanelDetector.version, s.rtl == rtl {
            stored = s
        } else {
            stored = Stored(version: PanelDetector.version, pages: [:], rtl: rtl)
        }
    }

    func panels(for page: Int) -> [CGRect]? {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        guard let raw = stored.pages[String(page)] else { return nil }
        return raw.compactMap { a in
            a.count == 4 ? CGRect(x: a[0], y: a[1], width: a[2], height: a[3]) : nil
        }
    }

    func store(_ rects: [CGRect], for page: Int) {
        lock.lock()
        stored.pages[String(page)] = rects.map { [Double($0.origin.x), Double($0.origin.y), Double($0.size.width), Double($0.size.height)] }
        dirty = true
        lock.unlock()
    }

    func flush() {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        if !dirty { return }
        if let data = try? JSONEncoder().encode(stored) {
            try? data.write(to: url, options: .atomic)
        }
        dirty = false
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/PanelDetector.swift =====
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics

/// Finds the panels on a comic page. Panels are found by a neural network (see `MLPanelDetector`); there is
/// no rule-based backup. A page where nothing is found (a splash page, say) is shown whole.
enum PanelDetector {
    /// Bump when detection changes so panels saved from an older version are found again.
    static let version = 10

    /// Panels in reading order as 0...1 rectangles with a top-left origin. A page with no panels comes back as the
    /// whole page. Nil means the model could not run this time, so the answer should not be saved.
    static func detect(image: CGImage, rtl: Bool) -> [CGRect]? {
        guard let found = MLPanelDetector.detect(image: image, rtl: rtl) else { return nil }
        return found.isEmpty ? [CGRect(x: 0, y: 0, width: 1, height: 1)] : found
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/RarComicSource.swift =====
#if canImport(Unrar)
import Foundation
import Unrar

/// CBR: a RAR full of page images.
final class RarComicSource: ComicSource {
    private let archive: Archive
    private let pages: [Entry]
    private let infoEntry: Entry?
    private let lock = NSLock()

    var pageCount: Int { return pages.count }

    init(url: URL) throws {
        let a: Archive
        let all: [Entry]
        do {
            a = try Archive(fileURL: url)
            all = try a.entries()
        } catch {
            throw ComicError.unreadable
        }
        var images: [Entry] = []
        var info: Entry?
        for e in all {
            if e.directory { continue }
            if ArchiveNames.isComicInfo(e.fileName) {
                info = e
            } else if ArchiveNames.isPage(e.fileName) {
                images.append(e)
            }
        }
        images.sort { ArchiveNames.sortKey($0.fileName, $1.fileName) }
        if images.isEmpty { throw ComicError.noPages }
        self.archive = a
        self.pages = images
        self.infoEntry = info
    }

    func metadataXML() -> Data? {
        guard let e = infoEntry else { return nil }
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return try? archive.extract(e)
    }

    func pageData(at index: Int) throws -> Data {
        guard index >= 0 && index < pages.count else { throw ComicError.noPages }
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return try archive.extract(pages[index])
    }
}
#endif

===== PanelSnap/Services/ReaderModel.swift =====
import Foundation
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// Drives the reader: loads pages, finds panels and decides where the "camera" points.
@MainActor
final class ReaderModel: ObservableObject {

    enum Target {
        case first
        case last
        case overview
        case index(Int)
    }

    @Published private(set) var pageIndex: Int = 0
    @Published private(set) var panelIndex: Int = 0          // -1 = whole page
    @Published private(set) var image: UIImage?
    @Published private(set) var panels: [CGRect] = []
    @Published private(set) var isLoading = true
    @Published private(set) var loadError: String?
    @Published var reachedEnd = false

    let entry: ComicEntry
    let rtl: Bool
    let pageCount: Int
    private(set) var identity: ComicIdentity

    private let source: ComicSource
    private let panelCache: PanelCache
    private var loadToken = 0
    private var imageCache: [Int: UIImage] = [:]
    private var panelMemo: [Int: [CGRect]] = [:]
    private var prefetching = Set<Int>()        // pages already being prepared in the background
    private var snap: Bool
    private var pageFirst: Bool
    private var observers: [NSObjectProtocol] = []

    init(entry: ComicEntry, source: ComicSource, identity: ComicIdentity, snap: Bool, pageFirst: Bool) {
        self.entry = entry
        self.source = source
        self.identity = identity
        self.pageCount = source.pageCount
        self.rtl = entry.isManga || identity.rtl
        self.panelCache = PanelCache(comicID: entry.id, rtl: entry.isManga || identity.rtl)
        self.snap = snap
        self.pageFirst = pageFirst

        var startPage = entry.lastPage
        var startPanel = entry.lastPanel
        if entry.finished || startPage >= source.pageCount {
            startPage = 0
            startPanel = 0
        }
        self.pageIndex = max(0, min(startPage, source.pageCount - 1))
        self.panelIndex = startPanel

        // Save detected panels if iOS is about to close the app, and shed memory when it asks us to.
        let cache = panelCache
        observers.append(NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(forName: UIApplication.willResignActiveNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { _ in
            cache.flush()
        })
        observers.append(NotificationCenter.default.addObserver(forName: UIApplication.didReceiveMemoryWarningNotification, object: nil, queue: .main) { [weak self] _ in
            Task { @MainActor in self?.dropFarPages(keeping: self?.pageIndex ?? 0, window: 0) }
        })
    }

    deinit {
        for o in observers { NotificationCenter.default.removeObserver(o) }
    }

    /// Forget decoded pages that are more than `window` pages away from `page`.
    private func dropFarPages(keeping page: Int, window: Int) {
        for key in Array(imageCache.keys) where abs(key - page) > window {
            imageCache.removeValue(forKey: key)
            panelMemo.removeValue(forKey: key)
        }
    }

    func updateSettings(snap: Bool, pageFirst: Bool) {
        let turnedOn = snap && !self.snap
        self.snap = snap
        self.pageFirst = pageFirst
        if !snap { panelIndex = -1 }
        if turnedOn {
            // Pages read in regular mode were never scanned: forget them so they get scanned now.
            panelMemo.removeAll()
            load(page: pageIndex, target: .overview)
        }
    }

    // MARK: Loading

    func start() {
        // A comic you have never opened starts on the whole first page; otherwise resume where you stopped.
        load(page: pageIndex, target: entry.hasStarted ? .index(panelIndex) : .first)
    }

    func finish() {
        panelCache.flush()
    }

    private func load(page: Int, target: Target) {
        loadToken += 1
        let token = loadToken
        let maxPixel: CGFloat = 2600
        let src = source
        let cache = panelCache
        let isRTL = rtl
        let scan = snap            // regular viewer mode never scans for panels

        if let cached = imageCache[page], let memo = panelMemo[page] {
            apply(page: page, image: cached, panels: memo, target: target)
            prefetch(around: page)
            return
        }
        isLoading = true
        loadError = nil

        Task.detached(priority: .userInitiated) { [weak self] in
            guard let cg = src.pageImage(at: page, maxPixel: maxPixel) else {
                await MainActor.run {
                    guard let self = self, token == self.loadToken else { return }
                    self.isLoading = false
                    self.loadError = "Page \(page + 1) couldn't be read."
                }
                return
            }
            var found = cache.panels(for: page)
            if found == nil && scan {
                if let detected = PanelDetector.detect(image: cg, rtl: isRTL) {
                    cache.store(detected, for: page)
                    found = detected
                }
            }
            let rects = found ?? []
            let ui = UIImage(cgImage: cg)
            await MainActor.run {
                guard let self = self else { return }
                self.imageCache[page] = ui
                self.panelMemo[page] = rects
                if token == self.loadToken {
                    self.apply(page: page, image: ui, panels: rects, target: target)
                    self.prefetch(around: page)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func apply(page: Int, image: UIImage, panels: [CGRect], target: Target) {
        // Covers, credits and splash pages have nothing to snap to: show them whole instead of guessing.
        let usable = panels.count >= 2 ? panels : []
        pageIndex = page
        self.image = image
        self.panels = usable
        isLoading = false
        loadError = nil
        if !snap || usable.isEmpty {
            panelIndex = -1
            return
        }
        switch target {
        case .first:
            panelIndex = pageFirst ? -1 : 0
        case .last:
            panelIndex = usable.count - 1
        case .overview:
            panelIndex = -1
        case .index(let i):
            panelIndex = max(-1, min(i, usable.count - 1))
        }
    }

    private func prefetch(around page: Int) {
        // Keep only the current page and its two neighbours in memory (each decoded page is tens of MB).
        dropFarPages(keeping: page, window: 1)
        for next in [page + 1, page - 1] where next >= 0 && next < pageCount {
            if imageCache[next] != nil || prefetching.contains(next) { continue }
            prefetching.insert(next)
            let src = source
            let cache = panelCache
            let isRTL = rtl
            let scan = snap            // regular viewer mode never scans for panels
            Task.detached(priority: .utility) { [weak self] in
                guard let cg = src.pageImage(at: next, maxPixel: 2600) else {
                    await MainActor.run { _ = self?.prefetching.remove(next) }
                    return
                }
                var found = cache.panels(for: next)
                if found == nil, scan, let detected = PanelDetector.detect(image: cg, rtl: isRTL) {
                    cache.store(detected, for: next)
                    found = detected
                }
                let rects = found ?? []
                let ui = UIImage(cgImage: cg)
                await MainActor.run {
                    guard let self = self else { return }
                    self.prefetching.remove(next)
                    if abs(next - self.pageIndex) <= 1 {
                        self.imageCache[next] = ui
                        self.panelMemo[next] = rects
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Navigation

    func goForward() {
        if isLoading { return }
        if panelIndex < 0 {
            if snap && !panels.isEmpty {
                panelIndex = 0
            } else {
                nextPage()
            }
        } else if panelIndex + 1 < panels.count {
            panelIndex += 1
        } else {
            nextPage()
        }
    }

    func goBack() {
        if isLoading { return }
        if panelIndex > 0 {
            panelIndex -= 1
        } else if panelIndex == 0 && pageFirst {
            panelIndex = -1
        } else {
            previousPage()
        }
    }

    /// Swipe: turn the page (like the Kindle / Amazon comics reader). The new page opens whole.
    func turnPage(forward: Bool) {
        if isLoading { return }
        if forward {
            nextPage()
        } else if pageIndex > 0 {
            load(page: pageIndex - 1, target: .first)
        }
    }

    func nextPage() {
        if pageIndex + 1 < pageCount {
            load(page: pageIndex + 1, target: .first)
        } else {
            reachedEnd = true
        }
    }

    func previousPage() {
        if pageIndex > 0 {
            load(page: pageIndex - 1, target: snap ? .last : .overview)
        }
    }

    func jump(toPage page: Int) {
        let p = max(0, min(page, pageCount - 1))
        if p == pageIndex { return }
        load(page: p, target: .first)
    }

    func toggleOverview() {
        if panels.isEmpty { return }
        if panelIndex < 0 {
            panelIndex = 0
        } else {
            panelIndex = -1
        }
    }

    /// Tap on a specific panel: fly straight to it.
    func focus(panel index: Int) {
        if isLoading || index < 0 || index >= panels.count { return }
        panelIndex = index
    }

    func showOverview() {
        if panelIndex >= 0 { panelIndex = -1 }
    }

    func focusPanelNearest(to point: CGPoint) {
        // Used after a pinch-out from the page overview: jump to the panel under the centre.
        if panels.isEmpty { return }
        var best = 0
        var bestDistance = CGFloat.greatestFiniteMagnitude
        for (i, r) in panels.enumerated() {
            let dx = r.midX - point.x
            let dy = r.midY - point.y
            let d = dx * dx + dy * dy
            if d < bestDistance {
                bestDistance = d
                best = i
            }
        }
        panelIndex = best
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/SegmentedDownloader.swift =====
import Foundation

/// Downloads one file over several connections at once (like a download manager). Sites that throttle every
/// connection separately hand out the file several times faster this way. Each connection has its own URLSession so
/// they are real, separate connections even on HTTP/2.
final class SegmentedDownloader {
    struct RangeUnsupported: Error {}

    private struct Chunk { var start: Int64; var end: Int64; var done: Int64 = 0; var tries = 0
        var length: Int64 { return end - start + 1 } }

    private let url: URL
    private let headers: [String: String]
    private let total: Int64
    private let dest: URL
    private let connections: Int

    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var chunks: [Chunk] = []
    private var queue: [Int] = []
    private var finishedChunks = 0
    private var received: Int64 = 0
    private var handle: FileHandle?
    private var workers: [Worker] = []
    private var ended = false
    private var lastReport = Date.distantPast
    private var active = 0                       // connections currently working
    private var scaler: DispatchSourceTimer?
    private var scaleBytes: Int64 = 0
    private var scaleSpeed: Double = 0
    private var ceiling = 16                     // never more connections than this

    /// Called a few times a second with the bytes received so far.
    var onProgress: ((Int64) -> Void)?
    /// Called once: nil on success.
    var onFinish: ((Error?) -> Void)?

    init(url: URL, headers: [String: String], total: Int64, dest: URL, connections: Int = 8) {
        self.url = url
        self.headers = headers
        self.total = total
        self.dest = dest
        self.connections = connections
    }

    func start() {
        // Small pieces: the last ones to finish are short, so there is no long slow tail.
        let size = max(Int64(512_000), min(Int64(2_000_000), total / 64))
        var offset: Int64 = 0
        while offset < total {
            let end = min(offset + size - 1, total - 1)
            chunks.append(Chunk(start: offset, end: end))
            queue.append(chunks.count - 1)
            offset = end + 1
        }
        do {
            try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: dest.deletingLastPathComponent(), withIntermediateDirectories: true)
            FileManager.default.createFile(atPath: dest.path, contents: nil)
            handle = try FileHandle(forWritingTo: dest)
            try handle?.truncate(atOffset: UInt64(total))
        } catch {
            finish(error)
            return
        }
        for _ in 0..<min(connections, chunks.count) { addWorker() }
        startScaling()
    }

    private func addWorker() {
        let w = Worker(owner: self)
        lock.lock()
        workers.append(w)
        active += 1
        lock.unlock()
        w.next()
    }

    /// Some sites hand out far more speed to more connections, others stop caring after a few. Every few seconds,
    /// if the last batch of new connections raised the total speed, add more (up to a limit); if not, stop adding.
    private func startScaling() {
        let timer = DispatchSource.makeTimerSource(queue: DispatchQueue.global(qos: .utility))
        timer.schedule(deadline: .now() + 4, repeating: 4)
        scaleBytes = 0
        timer.setEventHandler { [weak self] in
            guard let self = self else { return }
            self.lock.lock()
            let done = self.ended || self.queue.isEmpty
            let now = self.received
            let current = self.active
            self.lock.unlock()
            if done { self.scaler?.cancel(); self.scaler = nil; return }
            let speed = Double(now - self.scaleBytes) / 4
            self.scaleBytes = now
            let gained = self.scaleSpeed == 0 || speed > self.scaleSpeed * 1.25
            self.scaleSpeed = speed
            if gained && current < self.ceiling {
                for _ in 0..<2 { self.addWorker() }
            } else if !gained {
                self.scaler?.cancel(); self.scaler = nil      // more connections stopped helping
            }
        }
        scaler = timer
        timer.resume()
    }

    func cancel() {
        lock.lock()
        let wasEnded = ended
        ended = true
        lock.unlock()
        if wasEnded { return }
        scaler?.cancel(); scaler = nil
        workers.forEach { $0.stop() }
        try? handle?.close()
        handle = nil
        try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: dest)
    }

    // MARK: Shared state (always called with care for the lock)

    fileprivate func take() -> Int? {
        lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
        if ended || queue.isEmpty { return nil }
        return queue.removeFirst()
    }

    fileprivate func request(for index: Int) -> URLRequest {
        lock.lock()
        let c = chunks[index]
        lock.unlock()
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.cachePolicy = .reloadIgnoringLocalCacheData
        req.timeoutInterval = 30
        for (k, v) in headers { req.setValue(v, forHTTPHeaderField: k) }
        req.setValue("bytes=\(c.start + c.done)-\(c.end)", forHTTPHeaderField: "Range")
        return req
    }

    fileprivate func write(_ data: Data, chunk index: Int) {
        lock.lock()
        if ended { lock.unlock(); return }
        let at = chunks[index].start + chunks[index].done
        do {
            try handle?.seek(toOffset: UInt64(at))
            try handle?.write(contentsOf: data)
        } catch {
            lock.unlock()
            finish(error)
            return
        }
        chunks[index].done += Int64(data.count)
        chunks[index].tries = 0                       // it is moving, so earlier hiccups don't count
        received += Int64(data.count)
        let now = Date()
        let report = now.timeIntervalSince(lastReport) > 0.25
        if report { lastReport = now }
        let bytes = received
        lock.unlock()
        if report { onProgress?(bytes) }
    }

    /// A connection ended. Returns true if it should carry on with the next chunk.
    fileprivate func chunkEnded(_ index: Int, error: Error?) -> Bool {
        lock.lock()
        if ended { lock.unlock(); return false }
        if chunks[index].done >= chunks[index].length {
            finishedChunks += 1
            let all = finishedChunks == chunks.count
            lock.unlock()
            if all { finish(nil) }
            return !all
        }
        // Stopped early. A connection beyond the first few that gets refused is simply retired: the site has had
        // enough, and the rest of the file carries on over the others.
        if active > 4, error != nil {
            active -= 1
            ceiling = max(4, active)                  // this is as many as the site will take
            queue.insert(index, at: 0)
            scaler?.cancel(); scaler = nil
            lock.unlock()
            return false
        }
        // Put the rest in the queue and try again.
        chunks[index].tries += 1
        let tries = chunks[index].tries
        if tries <= 6 { queue.insert(index, at: 0) }
        lock.unlock()
        if tries > 6 {
            let failure: Error = error ?? URLError(.networkConnectionLost)
            finish(failure)
            return false
        }
        return true
    }

    fileprivate func finish(_ error: Error?) {
        lock.lock()
        if ended { lock.unlock(); return }
        ended = true
        lock.unlock()
        scaler?.cancel(); scaler = nil
        workers.forEach { $0.stop() }
        try? handle?.close()
        handle = nil
        if error != nil { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: dest) }
        else { onProgress?(total) }
        onFinish?(error)
    }

    // MARK: One connection

    private final class Worker: NSObject, URLSessionDataDelegate {
        private weak var owner: SegmentedDownloader?
        private var session: URLSession!
        private var chunk = -1
        private var stopped = false
        private var task: URLSessionDataTask?
        private var got: Int64 = 0                 // bytes this connection has received
        private var lastGot: Int64 = 0
        private var slowTicks = 0
        private var stalls: [Int: Int] = [:]       // how many times each piece was restarted for being slow
        private var watchdog: DispatchSourceTimer?

        init(owner: SegmentedDownloader) {
            self.owner = owner
            super.init()
            let cfg = URLSessionConfiguration.default
            cfg.httpMaximumConnectionsPerHost = 1
            cfg.requestCachePolicy = .reloadIgnoringLocalCacheData
            cfg.urlCache = nil
            session = URLSession(configuration: cfg, delegate: self, delegateQueue: nil)
            // A connection that has crawled for a few seconds is dropped and the piece restarts on a fresh one,
            // which is usually fast again. This is what rescues the slow end of a download.
            let t = DispatchSource.makeTimerSource(queue: .global())
            t.schedule(deadline: .now() + 2, repeating: 2)
            t.setEventHandler { [weak self] in self?.checkSpeed() }
            t.resume()
            watchdog = t
        }

        private func checkSpeed() {
            guard !stopped, let task = task, chunk >= 0 else { return }
            let rate = got - lastGot
            lastGot = got
            if rate < 40_000 {          // under about 20 KB/s
                slowTicks += 1
            } else {
                slowTicks = 0
            }
            if slowTicks >= 2, (stalls[chunk] ?? 0) < 4 {
                slowTicks = 0
                stalls[chunk, default: 0] += 1
                task.cancel()
            }
        }

        func next() {
            guard !stopped, let owner = owner, let index = owner.take() else {
                watchdog?.cancel()
                session.finishTasksAndInvalidate()
                return
            }
            chunk = index
            slowTicks = 0
            lastGot = got
            let t = session.dataTask(with: owner.request(for: index))
            task = t
            t.resume()
        }

        func stop() {
            stopped = true
            watchdog?.cancel()
            session.invalidateAndCancel()
        }

        func urlSession(_ session: URLSession, dataTask: URLSessionDataTask, didReceive response: URLResponse,
                        completionHandler: @escaping (URLSession.ResponseDisposition) -> Void) {
            if (response as? HTTPURLResponse)?.statusCode == 206 {
                completionHandler(.allow)
            } else {
                completionHandler(.cancel)
                owner?.finish(RangeUnsupported())
            }
        }

        func urlSession(_ session: URLSession, dataTask: URLSessionDataTask, didReceive data: Data) {
            got += Int64(data.count)
            owner?.write(data, chunk: chunk)
        }

        func urlSession(_ session: URLSession, task: URLSessionTask, didCompleteWithError error: Error?) {
            guard !stopped, let owner = owner else { return }
            if owner.chunkEnded(chunk, error: error) {
                // a short pause after a failure, none after a success
                if error != nil {
                    DispatchQueue.global().asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.5) { [weak self] in self?.next() }
                } else {
                    next()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Services/SevenZipComicSource.swift =====
#if canImport(SevenZip)
import Foundation
import SevenZip

/// CB7: a 7-Zip archive full of page images.
final class SevenZipComicSource: ComicSource {
    private let archive: Archive
    private let pages: [Entry]
    private let infoEntry: Entry?
    private let lock = NSLock()

    var pageCount: Int { return pages.count }

    init(url: URL) throws {
        let a: Archive
        do {
            a = try Archive(fileURL: url)
        } catch {
            throw ComicError.unreadable
        }
        var images: [Entry] = []
        var info: Entry?
        for e in a.entries {
            if e.directory { continue }
            if ArchiveNames.isComicInfo(e.path) {
                info = e
            } else if ArchiveNames.isPage(e.path) {
                images.append(e)
            }
        }
        images.sort { ArchiveNames.sortKey($0.path, $1.path) }
        if images.isEmpty { throw ComicError.noPages }
        self.archive = a
        self.pages = images
        self.infoEntry = info
    }

    func metadataXML() -> Data? {
        guard let e = infoEntry else { return nil }
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return try? archive.extract(entry: e)
    }

    func pageData(at index: Int) throws -> Data {
        guard index >= 0 && index < pages.count else { throw ComicError.noPages }
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return try archive.extract(entry: pages[index])
    }
}
#endif

===== PanelSnap/Services/ZipComicSource.swift =====
import Foundation
import ZIPFoundation

/// CBZ (and EPUB comics): a ZIP full of page images.
final class ZipComicSource: ComicSource {
    private let archive: Archive
    private let pages: [Entry]
    private let infoEntry: Entry?
    private let lock = NSLock()

    var pageCount: Int { return pages.count }

    init(url: URL) throws {
        let a: Archive
        do {
            a = try Archive(url: url, accessMode: .read)
        } catch {
            throw ComicError.unreadable
        }
        var images: [Entry] = []
        var info: Entry?
        for entry in a {
            if entry.type != .file { continue }
            let path = entry.path
            if ArchiveNames.isComicInfo(path) {
                info = entry
            } else if ArchiveNames.isPage(path) {
                images.append(entry)
            }
        }
        images.sort { ArchiveNames.sortKey($0.path, $1.path) }
        if images.isEmpty { throw ComicError.noPages }
        self.archive = a
        self.pages = images
        self.infoEntry = info
    }

    func metadataXML() -> Data? {
        guard let e = infoEntry else { return nil }
        return try? read(e)
    }

    func pageData(at index: Int) throws -> Data {
        guard index >= 0 && index < pages.count else { throw ComicError.noPages }
        return try read(pages[index])
    }

    private func read(_ entry: Entry) throws -> Data {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        var data = Data()
        _ = try archive.extract(entry, skipCRC32: true) { chunk in
            data.append(chunk)
        }
        return data
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/BrowserView.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import WebKit

struct WebHost: UIViewRepresentable {
    let webView: WKWebView
    func makeUIView(context: Context) -> WKWebView { webView }
    func updateUIView(_ uiView: WKWebView, context: Context) {}
}

/// A small, ad-free browser. Download a comic file anywhere and it lands in the library.
struct BrowserView: View {
    @ObservedObject private var model = BrowserModel.shared
    @ObservedObject private var shield = BrowserShield.shared
    @EnvironmentObject var library: LibraryStore
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @State private var topInset: CGFloat = 0
    @State private var landscape = false
    @State private var text = ""
    @FocusState private var focused: Bool
    @State private var askingHome = false
    @State private var showBookmarks = false
    @State private var homeText = ""
    @State private var swiped: [UUID: CGFloat] = [:]

    /// Keep the address bar clear of the Duo's corner status widget (it sits in the status bar when held upright).
    private var barTrailing: CGFloat { return (topInset > 20 && !landscape) ? 0 : Theme.cameraWidth }
    private var barTop: CGFloat { return topInset > 20 ? (landscape ? 22 : 8) : 30 }

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Theme.bg.ignoresSafeArea()
            FullWidthCentered {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
                    topBar
                    content
                    downloadsStrip
                    bottomBar
                }
            }
        }
        .readSafeTop($topInset)
        .readLandscape($landscape)
        .task {
            model.attach(library)
            await model.prepare()
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $showBookmarks) {
            BookmarksSheet { url in
                showBookmarks = false
                model.go(url)
            }
        }
        .alert("Home Page", isPresented: $askingHome) {
            TextField("website.com", text: $homeText)
                .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                .autocorrectionDisabled()
                .keyboardType(.URL)
            Button("Save") { model.setHome(homeText) }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: {
            Text("The browser opens here, and the Home button brings you back.")
        }
        .onChange(of: model.addressText) { _, new in if !focused { text = new } }
        .onChange(of: focused) { _, now in
            if now { model.beginEditing(); text = model.addressText } else { model.endEditing(); text = model.addressText }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Pieces

    private var topBar: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 10) {
            Button { dismiss() } label: { CircleIcon(systemName: "xmark") }
                .accessibilityLabel("Close Browser")
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                Image(systemName: "shield.lefthalf.filled")
                    .font(.system(size: 14, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundStyle(shield.isReady ? Theme.accent : Theme.secondary)
                TextField("Search or type a web address", text: $text)
                    .focused($focused)
                    .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                    .autocorrectionDisabled()
                    .keyboardType(.webSearch)
                    .submitLabel(.go)
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
                    .onSubmit {
                        model.go(text)
                        focused = false
                    }
                if focused && !text.isEmpty {
                    Button { text = "" } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 14)
            .frame(height: 40)
            .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
        }
        .padding(.leading, 20)
        .padding(.trailing, 20 + barTrailing)
        .padding(.top, barTop)
        .padding(.bottom, 10)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private var content: some View {
        ZStack {
            if let wv = model.webView {
                WebHost(webView: wv)
                    .opacity(model.hasPage ? 1 : 0)
            }
            if !model.hasPage { startPage }
            if model.isLoading && model.hasPage {
                VStack {
                    ProgressView(value: model.progress)
                        .tint(Theme.accent)
                    Spacer()
                }
            }
            if let n = model.notice {
                VStack {
                    Spacer()
                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        Text(n.text)
                            .font(.footnote.weight(.medium))
                            .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        if let url = n.openURL {
                            Button("Open") {
                                model.webView?.load(URLRequest(url: url))
                                model.notice = nil
                            }
                            .font(.footnote.weight(.bold))
                            .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                    .padding(.vertical, 10)
                    .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
                    .overlay(Capsule().stroke(Theme.stroke, lineWidth: 1))
                    .padding(.bottom, 12)
                }
                .transition(.opacity)
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
        .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2), value: model.notice?.id)
    }

    private var startPage: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 14) {
            Spacer()
            ZStack {
                RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 26, style: .continuous)
                    .fill(Theme.card)
                    .frame(width: 96, height: 96)
                Image(systemName: "shield.lefthalf.filled")
                    .font(.system(size: 42))
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            }
            Text(shield.isReady ? "Ad-Free Browsing" : shield.status.isEmpty ? "Getting ready…" : shield.status)
                .font(.system(size: 22, weight: .heavy, design: .rounded))
                .foregroundStyle(.white)
                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
            if !shield.isReady {
                ProgressView().tint(Theme.accent)
                Text("This only happens the first time.")
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            } else {
                Text("Ads, trackers and pop-ups are blocked. Download a comic file from any site and it is added to your library automatically.")
                    .font(.subheadline)
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                    .frame(maxWidth: 420)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 28)
            }
            Spacer()
            Spacer()
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
        .background(Theme.bg)
    }

    private var downloadsStrip: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 8) {
            ForEach(model.downloads.prefix(3)) { item in
                downloadRow(item)
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        .padding(.bottom, model.downloads.isEmpty ? 0 : 8)
        .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2), value: model.downloads.count)
    }

    private func downloadRow(_ item: BrowserDownload) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 12) {
            Image(systemName: icon(for: item.state))
                .font(.system(size: 18, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(tint(for: item.state))
                .frame(width: 26)
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 4) {
                Text(item.name)
                    .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
                    .lineLimit(1)
                    .truncationMode(.middle)
                switch item.state {
                case .downloading:
                    ProgressView(value: item.progress).tint(Theme.accent)
                    Text(detail(item)).font(.caption2).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                case .adding:
                    Text("Adding to your library…").font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                case .done(let s):
                    Text(s).font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                case .failed(let s):
                    Text(s).font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.red)
                }
            }
            Spacer(minLength: 0)
            if case .failed = item.state, item.retryURL != nil {
                Button { model.retryDownload(item.id) } label: {
                    Text("Retry").font(.footnote.weight(.bold)).foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                }
            }
            if case .downloading = item.state {} else if case .adding = item.state {} else {
                Button { model.dismissDownload(item.id) } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "xmark").font(.footnote.weight(.bold)).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(12)
        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16, style: .continuous))
        .offset(x: swiped[item.id] ?? 0)
        .opacity(1 - min(abs(swiped[item.id] ?? 0) / 300, 0.6))
        // Swipe a download sideways to dismiss it (a download that is still coming down is stopped first).
        .gesture(
            DragGesture(minimumDistance: 14)
                .onChanged { v in
                    if abs(v.translation.width) > abs(v.translation.height) { swiped[item.id] = v.translation.width }
                }
                .onEnded { v in
                    if abs(v.translation.width) > 90 || abs(v.predictedEndTranslation.width) > 220 {
                        withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.18)) { swiped[item.id] = v.translation.width < 0 ? -500 : 500 }
                        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.18) {
                            model.dismissDownload(item.id)
                            swiped[item.id] = nil
                        }
                    } else {
                        withAnimation(.spring(duration: 0.25)) { swiped[item.id] = 0 }
                    }
                }
        )
    }

    private func detail(_ d: BrowserDownload) -> String {
        let f = ByteCountFormatter()
        f.countStyle = .file
        var parts: [String] = []
        if d.total > 0 { parts.append("\(f.string(fromByteCount: d.bytes)) of \(f.string(fromByteCount: d.total))") }
        if d.speed > 1000 { parts.append("\(f.string(fromByteCount: Int64(d.speed)))/s") }
        if d.fast { parts.append("fast mode") }
        return parts.joined(separator: " · ")
    }

    private func icon(for s: BrowserDownload.State) -> String {
        switch s {
        case .downloading: return "arrow.down.circle.fill"
        case .adding: return "books.vertical.fill"
        case .done: return "checkmark.circle.fill"
        case .failed: return "exclamationmark.triangle.fill"
        }
    }

    private func tint(for s: BrowserDownload.State) -> Color {
        switch s {
        case .failed: return Theme.red
        default: return Theme.accent
        }
    }

    private var bottomBar: some View {
        HStack {
            barButton("chevron.left", enabled: model.canGoBack) { model.webView?.goBack() }
            Spacer()
            barButton("chevron.right", enabled: model.canGoForward) { model.webView?.goForward() }
            Spacer()
            barButton(model.isLoading ? "xmark" : "arrow.clockwise", enabled: model.hasPage) { model.reloadOrStop() }
            Spacer()
            barButton("house", enabled: true) {
                if model.homeURL.isEmpty { homeText = ""; askingHome = true } else { model.goHome() }
            }
            Spacer()
            Menu {
                if model.hasPage {
                    Button { model.toggleBookmark() } label: {
                        Label(model.currentIsBookmarked ? "Remove Bookmark" : "Add Bookmark",
                              systemImage: model.currentIsBookmarked ? "bookmark.slash" : "bookmark")
                    }
                }
                Button { showBookmarks = true } label: { Label("Bookmarks", systemImage: "book") }
                if model.hasPage {
                    Button { model.setHomeToCurrentPage() } label: { Label("Use This Page as Home", systemImage: "house.fill") }
                }
                Button { homeText = model.homeURL; askingHome = true } label: { Label("Set Home Address…", systemImage: "pencil") }
                if !model.homeURL.isEmpty {
                    Button(role: .destructive) { model.setHome("") } label: { Label("Clear Home", systemImage: "xmark") }
                }
            } label: {
                Image(systemName: "ellipsis")
                    .font(.system(size: 18, weight: .semibold))
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
                    .frame(width: 54, height: 44)
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 24)
        .frame(height: 50)
        .background(Theme.card)
    }

    private func barButton(_ name: String, enabled: Bool, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            Image(systemName: name)
                .font(.system(size: 18, weight: .semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(enabled ? Color.white : Theme.secondary.opacity(0.45))
                .frame(width: 54, height: 44)
        }
        .disabled(!enabled)
    }
}


/// The saved pages list.
struct BookmarksSheet: View {
    @ObservedObject private var model = BrowserModel.shared
    let open: (String) -> Void

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Group {
                if model.bookmarks.isEmpty {
                    VStack(spacing: 10) {
                        Image(systemName: "bookmark").font(.system(size: 34)).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                        Text("No Bookmarks Yet").font(.headline).foregroundStyle(.white)
                        Text("Open a page, then choose Add Bookmark from the ••• menu.")
                            .font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary).multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                    }
                    .padding(30)
                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
                } else {
                    List {
                        ForEach(model.bookmarks) { b in
                            Button { open(b.url) } label: {
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                    Text(b.title).foregroundStyle(.white).lineLimit(1)
                                    Text(b.url).font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary).lineLimit(1)
                                }
                            }
                            .listRowBackground(Theme.card)
                        }
                        .onDelete { model.bookmarks.remove(atOffsets: $0) }
                    }
                    .scrollContentBackground(.hidden)
                }
            }
            .background(Theme.bg)
            .navigationTitle("Bookmarks")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
        }
        .preferredColorScheme(.dark)
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/CompanionView.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// "Companion": everything you need to understand the comic you're reading.
struct CompanionView: View {
    @ObservedObject var model: CompanionModel
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings
    var onClose: () -> Void

    @State private var revealed = false
    @State private var selected: CharacterCard?
    @State private var showFix = false

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 24) {
                    header
                    statusRow

                    if let t = model.data.issueSummary {
                        section("About this issue") { textBlock(t) }
                    }
                    if let t = model.data.issueSynopsis {
                        section("What happens (spoilers)") { spoilerBlock(t) }
                    }
                    if let t = model.data.seriesSummary {
                        section("The series") { textBlock(t) }
                    }
                    if !model.data.characters.isEmpty {
                        section("Who's who") { characterStrip }
                    }
                    if !model.data.teams.isEmpty || !model.data.locations.isEmpty {
                        section("Teams & places") { teamsAndPlaces }
                    }
                    if !model.data.arcs.isEmpty {
                        section("Story arc") {
                            FlowChips(items: model.data.arcs, tint: Theme.accent)
                        }
                    }
                    if !model.data.creators.isEmpty {
                        section("Made by") { creators }
                    }
                    if !model.data.links.isEmpty {
                        section("Read more") { links }
                    }
                    footer
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 18)
                .padding(.vertical, 12)
            }
            .navigationTitle("Companion")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
                    Button("Done") { onClose() }
                        .fontWeight(.semibold)
                }
            }
        }
        .sheet(item: $selected) { card in
            CharacterSheet(model: model, name: card.name)
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $showFix) {
            FixMatchSheet(model: model)
        }
    }

    // MARK: Pieces

    private var header: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 14) {
            Group {
                if let img = CoverCache.shared.image(for: model.coverURL) {
                    Image(uiImage: img)
                        .resizable()
                        .scaledToFill()
                } else {
                    Rectangle().fill(Color.gray.opacity(0.25))
                }
            }
            .frame(width: 84, height: 126)
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous))
            .shadow(color: .black.opacity(0.25), radius: 8, y: 4)

            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                Text(model.data.series ?? model.entry.title)
                    .font(.title3.weight(.bold))
                    .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
                if !issueLine.isEmpty {
                    Text(issueLine).font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                }
                Text(subtitle)
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                if !model.data.genres.isEmpty {
                    FlowChips(items: Array(model.data.genres.prefix(4)), tint: .secondary)
                }
            }
            Spacer(minLength: 0)
        }
    }

    private var issueLine: String {
        var line = ""
        if let n = model.data.issue, !n.isEmpty { line = "Issue #" + n }
        if let t = model.data.title, !t.isEmpty {
            line += (line.isEmpty ? "" : " · ") + t
        }
        return line
    }

    private var subtitle: String {
        var parts: [String] = []
        if let p = model.data.publisher, !p.isEmpty { parts.append(p) }
        if let y = model.data.year { parts.append(String(y)) }
        return parts.joined(separator: " · ")
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private var statusRow: some View {
        if model.phase == .loading {
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                ProgressView()
                Text("Looking Up This Comic…")
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
        } else if let tip = model.tip {
            Label(tip, systemImage: "lightbulb")
                .font(.footnote)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .padding(12)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
        }
    }

    private func section<Content: View>(_ title: String, @ViewBuilder content: () -> Content) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Text(title)
                .font(.headline)
            content()
        }
    }

    private func textBlock(_ t: TextBlock) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            Text(t.text)
                .font(.body)
                .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
            sourceLine(t)
        }
    }

    private func sourceLine(_ t: TextBlock) -> some View {
        Group {
            if let u = t.url, let url = URL(string: u) {
                Link(destination: url) {
                    Label(t.source, systemImage: "arrow.up.right.square")
                        .font(.caption)
                }
            } else {
                Text(t.source)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
        }
    }

    private func spoilerBlock(_ t: TextBlock) -> some View {
        let hidden = settings.spoilerShield && !revealed
        return ZStack {
            textBlock(t)
                .blur(radius: hidden ? 9 : 0)
                .allowsHitTesting(!hidden)
            if hidden {
                Button {
                    withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.25)) { revealed = true }
                } label: {
                    Label("Tap to Reveal Spoilers", systemImage: "eye")
                        .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                        .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                        .padding(.vertical, 9)
                        .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
            }
        }
    }

    private var characterStrip: some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            HStack(spacing: 14) {
                ForEach(model.data.characters) { c in
                    Button {
                        selected = c
                    } label: {
                        VStack(spacing: 6) {
                            Avatar(name: c.name, imageURL: c.imageURL, size: 64)
                            Text(c.name)
                                .font(.caption.weight(.medium))
                                .lineLimit(2)
                                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                                .frame(width: 76)
                        }
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }
            .padding(.vertical, 2)
        }
    }

    private var teamsAndPlaces: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            if !model.data.teams.isEmpty {
                FlowChips(items: model.data.teams, tint: .blue)
            }
            if !model.data.locations.isEmpty {
                FlowChips(items: model.data.locations, tint: .green)
            }
        }
    }

    private var creators: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            ForEach(groupedCreators, id: \.0) { pair in
                HStack(alignment: .top) {
                    Text(pair.0)
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                        .frame(width: 92, alignment: .leading)
                    Text(pair.1.joined(separator: ", "))
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private var groupedCreators: [(String, [String])] {
        var order: [String] = []
        var map: [String: [String]] = [:]
        for c in model.data.creators {
            if map[c.role] == nil {
                order.append(c.role)
                map[c.role] = []
            }
            if !(map[c.role] ?? []).contains(c.name) { map[c.role]?.append(c.name) }
        }
        return order.map { ($0, map[$0] ?? []) }
    }

    private var links: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            ForEach(model.data.links) { l in
                if let url = URL(string: l.url) {
                    Link(destination: url) {
                        HStack {
                            Text(l.title)
                            Spacer()
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.up.right")
                        }
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .padding(12)
                        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private var footer: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            Button {
                showFix = true
            } label: {
                Label("Not the Right Comic?", systemImage: "magnifyingglass")
                    .font(.subheadline)
            }
            Button {
                model.load(force: true)
            } label: {
                Label("Refresh", systemImage: "arrow.clockwise")
                    .font(.subheadline)
            }
            if !model.data.sources.isEmpty {
                Text("Sources: " + model.data.sources.joined(separator: ", "))
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
            Text("Character guide: Superhero API (MIT) and FiveThirtyEight (CC BY 4.0). Comic data: Grand Comics Database (CC BY-SA 4.0). Wikipedia, Open Library and fan-wiki text under their own open licences.")
                .font(.caption2)
                .foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
        }
        .padding(.top, 6)
    }
}

// MARK: - Small building blocks

struct Avatar: View {
    let name: String
    let imageURL: String?
    let size: CGFloat

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Circle().fill(Theme.accent.opacity(0.18))
            Text(initials)
                .font(.system(size: size * 0.34, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            if let s = imageURL, let url = URL(string: s) {
                AsyncImage(url: url) { phase in
                    if let image = phase.image {
                        image.resizable().scaledToFill()
                    } else {
                        Color.clear
                    }
                }
                .clipShape(Circle())
            }
        }
        .frame(width: size, height: size)
    }

    private var initials: String {
        let words = name.split(separator: " ").prefix(2)
        let letters = words.compactMap { $0.first }.map { String($0) }
        return letters.joined().uppercased()
    }
}

/// Wrapping row of little capsules.
struct FlowChips: View {
    let items: [String]
    let tint: Color

    var body: some View {
        FlowLayout(spacing: 8) {
            ForEach(items, id: \.self) { item in
                Text(item)
                    .font(.footnote.weight(.medium))
                    .padding(.horizontal, 10)
                    .padding(.vertical, 5)
                    .background(tint.opacity(0.14), in: Capsule())
                    .foregroundStyle(tint)
            }
        }
    }
}

struct FlowLayout: Layout {
    var spacing: CGFloat = 8

    func sizeThatFits(proposal: ProposedViewSize, subviews: Subviews, cache: inout ()) -> CGSize {
        let maxWidth = proposal.width ?? 320
        var x: CGFloat = 0
        var y: CGFloat = 0
        var rowHeight: CGFloat = 0
        var widest: CGFloat = 0
        for sub in subviews {
            let size = sub.sizeThatFits(.unspecified)
            if x > 0 && x + size.width > maxWidth {
                y += rowHeight + spacing
                x = 0
                rowHeight = 0
            }
            x += size.width + spacing
            rowHeight = max(rowHeight, size.height)
            widest = max(widest, x - spacing)
        }
        return CGSize(width: min(maxWidth, max(widest, 0)), height: y + rowHeight)
    }

    func placeSubviews(in bounds: CGRect, proposal: ProposedViewSize, subviews: Subviews, cache: inout ()) {
        var x = bounds.minX
        var y = bounds.minY
        var rowHeight: CGFloat = 0
        for sub in subviews {
            let size = sub.sizeThatFits(.unspecified)
            if x > bounds.minX && x + size.width > bounds.maxX {
                y += rowHeight + spacing
                x = bounds.minX
                rowHeight = 0
            }
            sub.place(at: CGPoint(x: x, y: y), proposal: ProposedViewSize(size))
            x += size.width + spacing
            rowHeight = max(rowHeight, size.height)
        }
    }
}

struct CharacterSheet: View {
    @ObservedObject var model: CompanionModel
    let name: String
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss

    private var card: CharacterCard? {
        return model.data.characters.first(where: { $0.name == name })
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(spacing: 16) {
                    Avatar(name: name, imageURL: card?.imageURL, size: 110)
                        .padding(.top, 8)
                    Text(name)
                        .font(.title2.weight(.bold))
                        .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                    if let real = card?.role, !real.isEmpty {
                        Text(real)
                            .font(.subheadline)
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                    if let facts = card?.facts, !facts.isEmpty {
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                            ForEach(facts.filter { $0.label != "Real name" }, id: \.self) { f in
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                    Text(f.label.uppercased())
                                        .font(.caption2.weight(.semibold))
                                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                                    Text(f.value)
                                        .font(.subheadline)
                                        .fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(14)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous))
                    }
                    if let blurb = card?.blurb {
                        Text(blurb)
                            .font(.body)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                        if let src = card?.source {
                            Text("Source: " + src)
                                .font(.caption)
                                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                        }
                    } else if card?.facts == nil {
                        HStack(spacing: 10) {
                            ProgressView()
                            Text("Looking Up This Character…")
                                .font(.footnote)
                                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    if let u = card?.url, let url = URL(string: u) {
                        Link(destination: url) {
                            Label("Read More", systemImage: "arrow.up.right.square")
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                    }
                }
                .padding(20)
            }
            .navigationTitle("Character")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
                    Button("Done") { dismiss() }
                }
            }
        }
        .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
        .task {
            await model.loadCharacter(name)
        }
    }
}

struct FixMatchSheet: View {
    @ObservedObject var model: CompanionModel
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section(footer: Text("Type the real series name and issue number, for example “Saga” and “12”.")) {
                    TextField("Series name", text: $model.fixSeries)
                        .autocorrectionDisabled()
                    TextField("Issue number", text: $model.fixIssue)
                        .keyboardType(.numbersAndPunctuation)
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Fix the Match")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Cancel") { dismiss() }
                }
                ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) {
                    Button("Search") {
                        model.relookup(series: model.fixSeries, issue: model.fixIssue)
                        dismiss()
                    }
                    .fontWeight(.semibold)
                }
            }
        }
        .presentationDetents([.medium])
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/Components.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

// MARK: - Buttons

/// The big "confirm" button: comic yellow with black lettering.
struct YellowButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {
    var compact = false

    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        configuration.label
            .font(.system(size: compact ? 15 : 17, weight: .heavy, design: .rounded))
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.onAccent)
            .padding(.horizontal, compact ? 16 : 28)
            .padding(.vertical, compact ? 9 : 14)
            .background(Theme.accent, in: Capsule())
            .overlay(Capsule().strokeBorder(Color.black, lineWidth: 2))
            .background(Capsule().fill(Theme.red).offset(x: 3, y: 3))   // hard comic drop-shadow
            .opacity(configuration.isPressed ? 0.78 : 1)
            .scaleEffect(configuration.isPressed ? 0.97 : 1)
    }
}

/// A comic caption-box title: slanted red label, yellow offset shadow, heavy italic white lettering.
struct ComicTitle: View {
    let text: String

    var body: some View {
        Text(text.uppercased())
            .font(.system(size: 19, weight: .black, design: .rounded))
            .italic()
            .tracking(1.5)
            .foregroundStyle(Color.white)
            .padding(.horizontal, 18)
            .padding(.vertical, 6)
            .background(Theme.red.skew(-0.2))
            .background(Theme.accent.skew(-0.2).offset(x: 3, y: 3))
    }
}

private extension View {
    func skew(_ amount: CGFloat) -> some View {
        return self.transformEffect(CGAffineTransform(a: 1, b: 0, c: amount, d: 1, tx: 0, ty: 0))
    }
}

/// Quiet grey button for secondary actions.
struct GreyButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {
    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        configuration.label
            .font(.system(size: 17, weight: .semibold, design: .rounded))
            .foregroundStyle(Color.white)
            .padding(.horizontal, 24)
            .padding(.vertical, 14)
            .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
            .opacity(configuration.isPressed ? 0.78 : 1)
    }
}

/// Round grey icon button used in headers.
struct CircleIcon: View {
    let systemName: String
    var body: some View {
        Image(systemName: systemName)
            .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
            .foregroundStyle(Color.white)
            .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
            .background(Theme.card2, in: Circle())
    }
}

// MARK: - Layout

/// Lays content out across the WHOLE screen and keeps it centered on the screen itself, even when the system
/// reserves more room on one side (e.g. the iPhone Duo's status area): both sides get the larger inset.
struct FullWidthCentered<Content: View>: View {
    @ViewBuilder var content: () -> Content

    var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            let side = max(geo.safeAreaInsets.leading, geo.safeAreaInsets.trailing)
            content()
                .padding(.horizontal, side)
                .frame(width: geo.size.width, height: geo.size.height)
                // Only the wide (landscape) layout lets content rise level with the status widget. Upright, the
                // system status bar sits there and would cover (and swallow taps for) anything in the corner.
                .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: geo.size.width > geo.size.height ? .top : [])
        }
        .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: .horizontal)
    }
}

extension View {
    /// Reports whether the screen is currently wider than it is tall.
    func readLandscape(_ value: Binding<Bool>) -> some View {
        background(
            GeometryReader { g in
                Color.clear
                    .onAppear { value.wrappedValue = g.size.width > g.size.height }
                    .onChange(of: g.size) { _, new in value.wrappedValue = new.width > new.height }
            }
        )
    }

    /// Reports the top safe-area inset (the Duo's status area) so headers can sit snugly under it.
    func readSafeTop(_ value: Binding<CGFloat>) -> some View {
        background(
            GeometryReader { g in
                Color.clear
                    .onAppear { value.wrappedValue = g.safeAreaInsets.top }
                    .onChange(of: g.safeAreaInsets.top) { _, new in value.wrappedValue = new }
            }
        )
    }
}

// MARK: - File picker

/// Shows the system file browser straight from UIKit (not inside a SwiftUI sheet), so Open/Cancel always work.
/// Identical to the picker that works in iOXcode: asCopy true, so iOS hands us a private copy and no security-scoped
/// access is involved. That is the most reliable setup for sideloaded apps on the newest iOS.
enum FilePicker {
    private static var holder: Delegate?

    static func present(types: [UTType], multiple: Bool, onPick: @escaping ([URL]) -> Void) {
        // Wait a moment so a closing menu or sheet can't block the presentation.
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.4) {
            let vc = UIDocumentPickerViewController(forOpeningContentTypes: types, asCopy: true)
            vc.allowsMultipleSelection = multiple
            let d = Delegate(onPick)
            holder = d
            vc.delegate = d
            if let host = top() {
                Console.log("picker: presenting from \(type(of: host)), multiple=\(multiple)")
                host.present(vc, animated: true) { Console.log("picker: on screen") }
            } else {
                Console.log("picker: ERROR no view controller to present from")
            }
        }
    }

    /// Pick a whole folder (every comic inside gets added). Folders can't be copied by the picker, so this one opens in place.
    static func presentFolder(onPick: @escaping (URL) -> Void) {
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.4) {
            let vc = UIDocumentPickerViewController(forOpeningContentTypes: [.folder], asCopy: false)
            vc.allowsMultipleSelection = false
            let d = Delegate({ urls in if let u = urls.first { onPick(u) } })
            holder = d
            vc.delegate = d
            if let host = top() {
                Console.log("picker: presenting folder picker")
                host.present(vc, animated: true) { Console.log("picker: folder picker on screen") }
            } else {
