                Console.log("picker: ERROR no view controller to present from")
            }
        }
    }

    private static func top() -> UIViewController? {
        let scenes = UIApplication.shared.connectedScenes.compactMap { $0 as? UIWindowScene }
        let windows = scenes.flatMap { $0.windows }
        var vc = windows.first(where: { $0.isKeyWindow })?.rootViewController ?? windows.first?.rootViewController
        while let next = vc?.presentedViewController { vc = next }
        return vc
    }

    private final class Delegate: NSObject, UIDocumentPickerDelegate {
        let onPick: ([URL]) -> Void
        init(_ onPick: @escaping ([URL]) -> Void) { self.onPick = onPick }

        func documentPicker(_ controller: UIDocumentPickerViewController, didPickDocumentsAt urls: [URL]) {
            FilePicker.holder = nil
            Console.log("picker: Open pressed, \(urls.count) file(s): " + urls.map { $0.lastPathComponent }.joined(separator: ", "))
            if !urls.isEmpty { onPick(urls) }
        }

        func documentPickerWasCancelled(_ controller: UIDocumentPickerViewController) {
            FilePicker.holder = nil
            Console.log("picker: cancelled")
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/DiscoverView.swift =====
import SwiftUI

/// New comic releases, week by week, most popular first. Pick one to open its listing, or to search your Home
/// website for it.
enum DiscoverTab: String, CaseIterable, Identifiable {
    case forYou, current, next, previous
    var id: String { return rawValue }
    var label: String {
        switch self {
        case .forYou: return "For You"
        case .current: return "This Week"
        case .next: return "Next Week"
        case .previous: return "Last Week"
        }
    }
    var week: ReleaseWeek? {
        switch self {
        case .forYou: return nil
        case .current: return .current
        case .next: return .next
        case .previous: return .previous
        }
    }
}

struct DiscoverView: View {
    var onFind: (DiscoverComic) -> Void
    var onOpen: (URL) -> Void

    @EnvironmentObject var library: LibraryStore
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @ObservedObject private var model = DiscoverModel.shared
    @State private var topInset: CGFloat = 0
    @State private var landscape = false
    @State private var tab: DiscoverTab = .forYou
    @State private var search = ""
    @State private var publisher: String?
    @State private var sort: DiscoverSort = .popular
    @State private var selected: DiscoverComic?

    private var searching: Bool { return search.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).count >= 2 }
    private var weekList: [DiscoverComic] { return tab.week.flatMap { model.weeks[$0] } ?? [] }

    private var publishers: [String] {
        let counts = Dictionary(grouping: weekList.filter { !$0.publisher.isEmpty }, by: { $0.publisher }).mapValues { $0.count }
        return counts.sorted { $0.value != $1.value ? $0.value > $1.value : $0.key < $1.key }.prefix(12).map { $0.key }
    }

    private var visibleWeek: [DiscoverComic] {
        var list = weekList
        if let p = publisher { list = list.filter { $0.publisher == p } }
        switch sort {
        case .popular: list.sort { $0.pulls != $1.pulls ? $0.pulls > $1.pulls : $0.title.localizedStandardCompare($1.title) == .orderedAscending }
        case .title: list.sort { $0.title.localizedStandardCompare($1.title) == .orderedAscending }
        }
        return list
    }

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Theme.bg.ignoresSafeArea()
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                header
                searchField
                if !searching {
                    Picker("Section", selection: $tab) {
                        ForEach(DiscoverTab.allCases) { Text($0.label).tag($0) }
                    }
                    .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 20)
                    .padding(.bottom, 10)
                    if tab != .forYou, !publishers.isEmpty { chips }
                }
                content
            }
        }
        .readSafeTop($topInset)
        .readLandscape($landscape)
        .task(id: tab) {
            publisher = nil
            if let w = tab.week { await model.load(w) }
            else {
                await model.recommend(libraryTitles: library.comics.map { $0.title })
                await model.load(.current)
                await model.load(.next)
            }
        }
        .task(id: search) {
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 500_000_000)
            if Task.isCancelled { return }
            await model.search(search)
        }
        .sheet(item: $selected) { comic in
            DetailSheet(comic: comic,
                        onFind: { selected = nil; onFind(comic) },
                        onOpen: { url in selected = nil; onOpen(url) })
                .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
        }
    }

    private var header: some View {
        ZStack {
            ComicTitle(text: "Discover")
            HStack {
                Button("Done") { dismiss() }.buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle(compact: true))
                Spacer()
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        .padding(.top, topInset > 20 ? (landscape ? 22 : 8) : 30)
        .padding(.bottom, 12)
        .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: .horizontal)
    }

    private var searchField: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            TextField("Search every comic, old and new", text: $search)
                .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                .autocorrectionDisabled()
            if !search.isEmpty {
                Button { search = "" } label: { Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary) }
                    .accessibilityLabel("Clear search")
            } else if tab != .forYou {
                Menu {
                    Picker("Sort", selection: $sort) { ForEach(DiscoverSort.allCases) { Text($0.label).tag($0) } }
                } label: {
                    Text(sort.label).font(.footnote.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(10)
        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        .padding(.bottom, 10)
    }

    private var chips: some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                chip("All", on: publisher == nil) { publisher = nil }
                ForEach(publishers, id: \.self) { p in chip(p, on: publisher == p) { publisher = p } }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        }
        .padding(.bottom, 8)
    }

    private func chip(_ text: String, on: Bool, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            Text(text).font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(on ? Theme.onAccent : Color.white)
                .padding(.horizontal, 12).padding(.vertical, 7)
                .background(on ? Theme.accent : Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
        }
    }

    // MARK: Content

    @ViewBuilder private var content: some View {
        if searching {
            if model.searching && model.searchResults.isEmpty {
                Spacer(); ProgressView().tint(Theme.accent); Spacer()
            } else if model.searchResults.isEmpty {
                Spacer(); Text("No comics found").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary); Spacer()
            } else {
                list(model.searchResults)
            }
        } else if let w = tab.week {
            weekContent(w)
        } else {
            forYouContent
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder private func weekContent(_ w: ReleaseWeek) -> some View {
        if model.loadingWeeks.contains(w) && model.weeks[w] == nil {
            Spacer(); ProgressView().tint(Theme.accent); Spacer()
        } else if model.failedWeeks.contains(w) {
            failure { Task { await model.load(w, force: true) } }
        } else if visibleWeek.isEmpty {
            Spacer(); Text("Nothing here yet").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary); Spacer()
        } else {
            list(visibleWeek) { await model.load(w, force: true) }
        }
    }

    private var forYouContent: some View {
        ScrollView {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 22) {
                if model.forYou.isEmpty && library.comics.isEmpty {
                    Text("Add a few comics to your library and this page will suggest more like them.")
                        .font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                }
                shelf("You May Also Like", model.forYou)
                shelf("Popular This Week", Array((model.weeks[.current] ?? []).sorted { $0.pulls > $1.pulls }.prefix(20)))
                shelf("Coming Next Week", Array((model.weeks[.next] ?? []).sorted { $0.pulls > $1.pulls }.prefix(20)))
                if model.forYou.isEmpty && model.weeks[.current] == nil && model.weeks[.next] == nil {
                    if model.failedWeeks.isEmpty { ProgressView().tint(Theme.accent).frame(maxWidth: .infinity).padding(.top, 40) }
                    else { failure { Task { await model.load(.current, force: true); await model.load(.next, force: true) } } }
                }
                Text("Data from League of Comic Geeks (leagueofcomicgeeks.com).")
                    .font(.caption2).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 20)
            .padding(.bottom, 24)
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder private func shelf(_ title: String, _ items: [DiscoverComic]) -> some View {
        if !items.isEmpty {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                Text(title).font(.system(size: 18, weight: .heavy, design: .rounded)).foregroundStyle(.white)
                ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                    HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 12) {
                        ForEach(items) { c in
                            Button { selected = c } label: {
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                                    CoverThumb(url: c.cover, width: 104)
                                    Text(c.title).font(.caption.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(.white).lineLimit(2)
                                        .frame(width: 104, alignment: .leading)
                                }
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func failure(_ retry: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 12) {
            Spacer()
            Text("Couldn't load the release list").font(.headline)
            Text("Check your connection and try again.").font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            Button("Try Again", action: retry).buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle(compact: true))
            if let url = URL(string: "https://leagueofcomicgeeks.com/comics/new-comics") {
                Button("Open the list in the browser") { onOpen(url) }.font(.footnote).foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            }
            Spacer()
        }
        .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
        .padding(30)
    }

    private func list(_ items: [DiscoverComic], refresh: (() async -> Void)? = nil) -> some View {
        ScrollView {
            LazyVStack(spacing: 10) {
                ForEach(items) { comic in
                    Button { selected = comic } label: { row(comic) }.buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 20)
            Text("Data from League of Comic Geeks (leagueofcomicgeeks.com).")
                .font(.caption2).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                .padding(.vertical, 18)
        }
        .refreshable { if let r = refresh { await r() } }
    }

    private func row(_ c: DiscoverComic) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 12) {
            CoverThumb(url: c.cover)
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 3) {
                Text(c.title).font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(.white).lineLimit(2)
                if !c.publisher.isEmpty { Text(c.publisher).font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.accent) }
                if c.pulls > 0 { Text("\(c.pulls.formatted()) pulls").font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary) }
            }
            Spacer(minLength: 4)
            if !c.price.isEmpty { Text(c.price).font(.caption.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary) }
            Image(systemName: "chevron.right").font(.caption).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
        }
        .padding(10)
        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14, style: .continuous))
    }
}

/// A cover picture that falls back to a plain tile when there is none.
private struct CoverThumb: View {
    let url: URL?
    var width: CGFloat = 46
    var body: some View {
        AsyncImage(url: url) { phase in
            if let image = phase.image {
                image.resizable().scaledToFill()
            } else {
                ZStack { Theme.card2; Image(systemName: "book.closed").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary) }
            }
        }
        .frame(width: width, height: width * 1.5)
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 6, style: .continuous))
    }
}

private struct DetailSheet: View {
    let comic: DiscoverComic
    var onFind: () -> Void
    var onOpen: (URL) -> Void

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Theme.bg.ignoresSafeArea()
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                    HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 14) {
                        CoverThumb(url: comic.cover, width: 90)
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                            Text(comic.title).font(.title3.weight(.bold)).foregroundStyle(.white)
                            Text(comic.publisher).font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                                if !comic.price.isEmpty { Text(comic.price).font(.footnote.weight(.semibold)).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary) }
                                if comic.pulls > 0 { Text("\(comic.pulls.formatted()) pulls").font(.footnote).foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary) }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if !comic.summary.isEmpty {
                        Text(comic.summary).font(.subheadline).foregroundStyle(Color(white: 0.85))
                    }
                    Button { onFind() } label: {
                        Label("Find on My Home Website", systemImage: "magnifyingglass").frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle(compact: false))
                    if let url = comic.listingURL, url.scheme?.hasPrefix("http") == true {
                        Button { onOpen(url) } label: {
                            Label("Open Listing in Browser", systemImage: "globe").frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        }
                        .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        .padding(.vertical, 12)
                        .background(Theme.card2, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
                    }
                }
                .padding(20)
            }
        }
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/LibraryView.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

struct LibraryView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var library: LibraryStore
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings

    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
    @State private var route: Route?
    @State private var search = ""
    @State private var topInset: CGFloat = 0
    @State private var landscape = false
    @State private var themeStamp = ""                      // changes after Settings closes, to repaint with new colours

    // Organising
    @State private var shelf: String?                       // nil = every comic
    @State private var organizing = false
    @State private var continueID: UUID?                    // which Continue Reading card leads the row
    @State private var selection = Set<UUID>()
    @State private var showRename = false
    @State private var renameID: UUID?
    @State private var renameText = ""
    @State private var showShelfAlert = false
    @State private var shelfRenaming: String?               // nil = creating a new shelf
    @State private var shelfMoveIDs = Set<UUID>()           // comics to move into a shelf that is being created
    @State private var shelfText = ""
    @State private var showDeleteShelf = false
    @State private var shelfToDelete = ""
    @State private var showDeleteComics = false
    @State private var deleteIDs = Set<UUID>()

    /// One presentation slot for everything (reader + settings) so SwiftUI presentations never fight each other.
    enum Route: Identifiable {
        case reader(ComicEntry)
        case settings
        case browser
        case discover
        var id: String {
            switch self {
            case .reader(let e): return "reader-" + e.id.uuidString
            case .settings: return "settings"
            case .browser: return "browser"
            case .discover: return "discover"
            }
        }
    }

    private func sorted(_ list: [ComicEntry], by sort: LibrarySort) -> [ComicEntry] {
        var out = list
        switch sort {
        case .recent: out.sort { ($0.lastOpenedAt ?? $0.addedAt) > ($1.lastOpenedAt ?? $1.addedAt) }
        case .added: out.sort { $0.addedAt > $1.addedAt }
        case .title: out.sort { $0.title.localizedStandardCompare($1.title) == .orderedAscending }
        case .custom: break
        }
        return out
    }

    private var visible: [ComicEntry] {
        var list = library.comics
        if let s = shelf { list = list.filter { $0.shelf == s } }
        let q = search.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        if !q.isEmpty {
            list = list.filter { $0.title.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains(q) }
        }
        return sorted(list, by: settings.librarySort)
    }

    /// Everything you have started and not finished, most recently read first.
    private var continueList: [ComicEntry] {
        let started = library.comics.filter { $0.hasStarted && !$0.finished }
        let ordered = started.sorted { ($0.lastOpenedAt ?? .distantPast) > ($1.lastOpenedAt ?? .distantPast) }
        return Array(ordered.prefix(8))
    }

    private let columns = [GridItem(.adaptive(minimum: 140, maximum: 200), spacing: 16, alignment: .top)]

    /// One picker for everything: choose a single comic or as many as you like.
    private func addComics() {
        let target = shelf
        FilePicker.present(types: [UTType.item], multiple: true) { urls in
            Task { await library.importFiles(urls, shelf: target) }
        }
    }

    private func addFolder() {
        let target = shelf
        FilePicker.presentFolder { url in
            Task { await library.importFolder(url, shelf: target) }
        }
    }

    /// A discreet "+" that offers files or a whole folder.
    private var addMenu: some View {
        Menu {
            Button { addComics() } label: { Label("Files", systemImage: "doc.on.doc") }
            Button { addFolder() } label: { Label("Folder", systemImage: "folder") }
            Button { route = .browser } label: { Label("Browser", systemImage: "globe") }
        } label: {
            CircleIcon(systemName: "plus")
        }
        .accessibilityLabel("Add Comics")
    }

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Theme.bg.ignoresSafeArea()
            // A soft red glow at the top, like the speed-line background of the icon.
            VStack {
                LinearGradient(colors: [Theme.red.opacity(0.34), Theme.red.opacity(0)], startPoint: .top, endPoint: .bottom)
                    .frame(height: 260)
                Spacer()
            }
            .ignoresSafeArea()
            .allowsHitTesting(false)

            FullWidthCentered {
                ZStack {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
                    header
                    if library.comics.isEmpty && !library.isImporting {
                        emptyState
                    } else {
                        ScrollView {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 18) {
                                // This top band sits beside the Duo's corner camera, so it holds the useful stuff.
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                                    if showSearch {
                                        searchField
                                    }
                                    if search.isEmpty && !organizing {
                                        let list = continueList
                                        if list.count == 1 {
                                            continueCard(list[0])
                                                .padding(.trailing, cameraPad)      // stay clear of the Duo's corner camera
                                        } else if list.count > 1 {
                                            continueRow(list)
                                        } else {
                                            statsTile
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, minHeight: wideStatus ? 0 : Theme.cameraHeight - 64, alignment: .top)

                                shelfBar
                                sortRow

                                let items = visible
                                if items.isEmpty {
                                    Text(shelf == nil ? "No comics match your search." : "Nothing on this shelf yet. Press and hold a comic, then choose Move to Shelf, or drag it onto the shelf.")
                                        .font(.subheadline)
                                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                        .padding(.top, 30)
                                } else {
                                    LazyVGrid(columns: columns, spacing: 22) {
                                        ForEach(items) { entry in
                                            cell(entry)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            .frame(maxWidth: 900)
                            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                            .padding(.horizontal, 20)
                            .padding(.top, 0)
                            .padding(.bottom, organizing ? 110 : 40)
                        }
                    }
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)

                // floating bottom bar: the yellow "add" button, or the actions while organising
                if organizing {
                    VStack {
                        Spacer()
                        Group {
                            if organizing { organizeBar }
                        }
                        .shadow(color: .black.opacity(0.6), radius: 14, y: 6)
                        .padding(.bottom, 22)
                    }
                }

                if let status = library.importStatus {
                    VStack {
                        Spacer()
                        HStack(spacing: 10) {
                            ProgressView().tint(Theme.accent)
                            Text(status).font(.subheadline.weight(.medium)).foregroundStyle(.white)
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal, 18)
                        .padding(.vertical, 12)
                        .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
                        .overlay(Capsule().stroke(Theme.stroke, lineWidth: 1))
                        .padding(.bottom, 92)
                    }
                    .transition(.move(edge: .bottom).combined(with: .opacity))
                }

                if let message = library.errorMessage {
                    errorCard(message)
                }
                }
            }
            .id(themeStamp)
        }
        .animation(.easeInOut, value: library.importStatus)
        .readSafeTop($topInset)
        .readLandscape($landscape)
        .onChange(of: route?.id) { _, id in
            if id == nil { themeStamp = settings.themeKey }
        }
        .onAppear {
            Task { await library.scanFolder() }
            // Compile the browser's ad-block lists quietly in the background, so the browser opens instantly.
            Task {
                try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 4_000_000_000)
                await BrowserShield.shared.prepare()
            }
        }
        .onChange(of: scenePhase) { _, phase in
            Console.log("app state: \(phase)")
            if phase == .active {
                Task { await library.scanFolder() }
            } else {
                library.flush()          // don't lose progress if iOS closes the app
            }
        }
        .onChange(of: library.shelves) { _, list in
            if let s = shelf, !list.contains(s) { shelf = nil }
        }
        .alert("Rename Comic", isPresented: $showRename) {
            TextField("Title", text: $renameText)
            Button("Save") {
                if let id = renameID { library.rename(id: id, to: renameText) }
            }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
        }
        .alert(shelfRenaming == nil ? "New Shelf" : "Rename Shelf", isPresented: $showShelfAlert) {
            TextField("Shelf name", text: $shelfText)
            Button(shelfRenaming == nil ? "Create" : "Save") { commitShelfName() }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: {
            if shelfRenaming == nil && !shelfMoveIDs.isEmpty {
                Text("The selected comics will be moved to it.")
            }
        }
        .confirmationDialog("Delete Shelf?", isPresented: $showDeleteShelf, titleVisibility: .visible) {
            Button("Delete Shelf", role: .destructive) {
                library.deleteShelf(shelfToDelete)
                if shelf == shelfToDelete { shelf = nil }
            }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: {
            Text("The comics stay in your library.")
        }
        .confirmationDialog(deleteIDs.count == 1 ? "Delete Comic?" : "Delete \(deleteIDs.count) Comics?",
                            isPresented: $showDeleteComics, titleVisibility: .visible) {
            Button("Delete", role: .destructive) {
                library.delete(ids: deleteIDs)
                selection.subtract(deleteIDs)
                deleteIDs = []
            }
            Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) {}
        } message: {
            Text("The files will be removed from PanelSnap.")
        }
        .task(id: library.comics.count) {
            if settings.showDiscover { await DiscoverModel.shared.prefetch(libraryTitles: library.comics.map { $0.title }) }
        }
        .fullScreenCover(item: $route) { r in
            switch r {
            case .reader(let entry):
                ReaderHost(entry: entry)
            case .settings:
                SettingsView()
            case .browser:
                BrowserView()
            case .discover:
                DiscoverView(onFind: { comic in
                                 BrowserModel.shared.searchHome(comic.searchTerm)
                                 route = .browser
                             },
                             onOpen: { url in
                                 BrowserModel.shared.open(url)
                                 route = .browser
                             })
            }
        }
    }

    // MARK: Organising

    private func cell(_ entry: ComicEntry) -> some View {
        ComicCell(entry: entry, organizing: organizing, selected: selection.contains(entry.id))
            .onTapGesture {
                if organizing {
                    if selection.contains(entry.id) { selection.remove(entry.id) } else { selection.insert(entry.id) }
                } else {
                    route = .reader(entry)
                }
            }
            .contextMenu {
                if !organizing { menu(for: entry) }
            }
            .modifier(ReorderModifier(enabled: true, id: entry.id) { dragged, target in
                reorder(dragged, onto: target)
            })
    }

    private func reorder(_ dragged: UUID, onto target: UUID) {
        if settings.librarySort != .custom {
            // Freeze what is on screen as "my order", then move the comic within it.
            library.applyOrder(sorted(library.comics, by: settings.librarySort).map { $0.id })
            settings.librarySort = .custom
        }
        withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2)) {
            library.moveComic(dragged, onto: target)
        }
    }

    /// A comic dragged onto a shelf chip moves to that shelf (the All chip takes it off any shelf).
    private func dropOnShelf(_ items: [String], _ name: String?) -> Bool {
        let ids = Set(items.compactMap { UUID(uuidString: $0) })
        if ids.isEmpty { return false }
        library.move(ids, toShelf: name)
        return true
    }

    private func commitShelfName() {
        if let old = shelfRenaming {
            library.renameShelf(old, to: shelfText)
            if shelf == old { shelf = library.shelves.first(where: { $0.caseInsensitiveCompare(shelfText.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)) == .orderedSame }) ?? old }
        } else if let name = library.addShelf(shelfText) {
            if !shelfMoveIDs.isEmpty {
                library.move(shelfMoveIDs, toShelf: name)
                shelfMoveIDs = []
            } else {
                shelf = name
            }
        }
    }

    private func newShelf(moving ids: Set<UUID>) {
        shelfRenaming = nil
        shelfMoveIDs = ids
        shelfText = ""
        showShelfAlert = true
    }

    private func endOrganizing() {
        organizing = false
        selection = []
    }

    private func shelfChip(_ title: String, count: Int, selected: Bool, action: @escaping () -> Void) -> some View {
        Button(action: action) {
            HStack(spacing: 6) {
                Text(title).font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold)).lineLimit(1)
                Text("\(count)").font(.caption.weight(.bold)).opacity(0.6)
            }
            .foregroundStyle(selected ? Theme.onAccent : Color.white)
            .padding(.horizontal, 14)
            .padding(.vertical, 8)
            .background(selected ? Theme.accent : Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
    }

    private var shelfBar: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 10) {
            shelfChips
            if settings.showDiscover {
                Button { route = .discover } label: {
                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                        Image(systemName: "sparkles")
                        Text("Discover").font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    }
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                    .padding(.vertical, 8)
                    .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
                }
                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                .accessibilityLabel("Discover new comics")
            }
        }
    }

    private var shelfChips: some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            HStack(spacing: 8) {
                shelfChip("All", count: library.comics.count, selected: shelf == nil) { shelf = nil }
                    .dropDestination(for: String.self) { items, _ in dropOnShelf(items, nil) }
                ForEach(library.shelves, id: \.self) { name in
                    shelfChip(name, count: library.comics.filter { $0.shelf == name }.count, selected: shelf == name) { shelf = name }
                        .dropDestination(for: String.self) { items, _ in dropOnShelf(items, name) }
                        .contextMenu {
                            Button {
                                shelfRenaming = name
                                shelfText = name
                                shelfMoveIDs = []
                                showShelfAlert = true
                            } label: { Label("Rename Shelf", systemImage: "pencil") }
                            Button(role: .destructive) {
                                shelfToDelete = name
                                showDeleteShelf = true
                            } label: { Label("Delete Shelf", systemImage: "trash") }
                        }
                }
                Button { newShelf(moving: []) } label: {
                    Image(systemName: "plus")
                        .font(.system(size: 14, weight: .bold))
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                        .frame(width: 34, height: 34)
                        .background(Theme.card2, in: Circle())
                }
                .accessibilityLabel("New Shelf")
            }
            .padding(.vertical, 2)
        }
    }

    private var sortRow: some View {
        HStack {
            Menu {
                Picker("Sort", selection: $settings.librarySort) {
                    ForEach(LibrarySort.allCases) { sort in
                        Label(sort.label, systemImage: sort.icon).tag(sort)
                    }
                }
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    Image(systemName: "arrow.up.arrow.down")
                    Text(settings.librarySort.label)
                    Image(systemName: "chevron.down").font(.caption2.weight(.bold))
                }
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            }
            Spacer()
            if organizing {
                Button(selection.count == visible.count && !selection.isEmpty ? "Select none" : "Select all") {
                    if selection.count == visible.count { selection = [] } else { selection = Set(visible.map { $0.id }) }
                }
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                .padding(.trailing, 6)
                Button("Done") { endOrganizing() }
                    .buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle(compact: true))
            }
        }
    }

    private func barPill(_ icon: String, _ text: String, tint: Color = .white) -> some View {
        Label(text, systemImage: icon)
            .font(.system(size: 15, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
            .foregroundStyle(tint)
            .padding(.horizontal, 16)
            .padding(.vertical, 12)
            .background(Theme.card2, in: Capsule())
            .overlay(Capsule().stroke(Theme.stroke, lineWidth: 1))
    }

    /// Actions for the comics that are selected while organising.
    private var organizeBar: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 10) {
            Menu {
                shelfItems(for: selection)
            } label: { barPill("folder", "Shelf") }
            Menu {
                Button { library.markRead(ids: selection, true) } label: { Label("Mark as Read", systemImage: "checkmark.circle") }
                Button { library.markRead(ids: selection, false) } label: { Label("Mark as Unread", systemImage: "arrow.uturn.backward") }
            } label: { barPill("checkmark.circle", "Read") }
            Button {
                deleteIDs = selection
                showDeleteComics = true
            } label: { barPill("trash", "Delete", tint: Theme.red) }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
        }
        .opacity(selection.isEmpty ? 0.45 : 1)
        .disabled(selection.isEmpty)
        .overlay(alignment: .top) {
            Text(selection.isEmpty ? "Tap comics to select them" : "\(selection.count) selected")
                .font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                .offset(y: -22)
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func shelfItems(for ids: Set<UUID>) -> some View {
        Button { library.move(ids, toShelf: nil) } label: { Label("No Shelf", systemImage: "tray") }
        ForEach(library.shelves, id: \.self) { name in
            Button { library.move(ids, toShelf: name) } label: { Label(name, systemImage: "folder") }
        }
        Button { newShelf(moving: ids) } label: { Label("New Shelf…", systemImage: "plus") }
    }

    // MARK: Pieces

    private var showSearch: Bool { return library.comics.count > 6 }

    /// On the wide layout the Duo's status widget is a short capsule in the corner (the layout starts below the
    /// system's safe area), so only the header needs to avoid it. On the narrow layout it is a tall box.
    private var wideStatus: Bool { return topInset > 20 }          // the system already reserves the status area
    private var headerTop: CGFloat { return wideStatus ? (landscape ? 22 : 8) : 30 }
    private var cameraPad: CGFloat { return wideStatus ? 0 : Theme.cameraWidth }

                    .accessibilityLabel("Settings")
                addMenu
                Spacer()
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        .padding(.top, headerTop)      // wide screen: the camera widget is a short capsule, so the title can sit level with it
        .padding(.bottom, 14)
    }

        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            Image(systemName: "magnifyingglass").foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)

        .padding(.trailing, cameraPad)      // stay clear of the Duo's corner camera
    }

                .font(.subheadline)

    private func errorCard(_ message: String) -> some View {
