    /// Several books in progress: cards sized to fit exactly (one per page on the narrow layout, two on the wide one),
    /// snapping into place, so nothing is ever sliced off at the edge.
    private func continueRow(_ list: [ComicEntry]) -> some View {
        let perPage = (wideStatus && landscape) ? 2 : 1
        return VStack(spacing: 10) {
            ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                HStack(spacing: 12) {
                    ForEach(list) { entry in
                        continueCard(entry)
                            .containerRelativeFrame(.horizontal, count: perPage, spacing: 12)
                    }
                }
                .scrollTargetLayout()
            }
            .scrollTargetBehavior(.viewAligned)
            .scrollPosition(id: $continueID)
            if list.count > perPage {
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    ForEach(list) { entry in
                        Circle()
                            .fill(entry.id == (continueID ?? list.first?.id) ? Theme.accent : Theme.secondary.opacity(0.35))
                            .frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(.trailing, cameraPad)
    }

    private func continueCard(_ entry: ComicEntry) -> some View {
        Button {
            route = .reader(entry)
        } label: {
            HStack(spacing: 14) {
                CoverView(entry: entry)
                    .frame(width: 58, height: 86)
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 5) {
                    Text("CONTINUE READING")
                        .font(.caption2.weight(.heavy))
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    Text(entry.title)
                        .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        .lineLimit(2)
                        .multilineTextAlignment(.leading)
                    Text("Page \(entry.lastPage + 1) of \(entry.pageCount)")
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                    ProgressView(value: entry.progress)
                        .tint(Theme.red)
                }
                Spacer(minLength: 0)
                Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                    .font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            }
            .padding(12)
            .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 20, style: .continuous))
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
    }

    /// Shown in the top band when nothing is in progress.
    private var statsTile: some View {
        let finished = library.comics.filter { $0.finished }.count
        let reading = library.comics.filter { $0.hasStarted && !$0.finished }.count
        return HStack(spacing: 0) {
            stat(library.comics.count, "Comics")
            stat(reading, "Reading")
            stat(finished, "Finished")
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 18)
        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 20, style: .continuous))
        .padding(.trailing, cameraPad)
    }

    private func stat(_ value: Int, _ label: String) -> some View {
        VStack(spacing: 3) {
            Text("\(value)")
                .font(.system(size: 26, weight: .heavy, design: .rounded))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            Text(label)
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
    }

    @ViewBuilder
    private func menu(for entry: ComicEntry) -> some View {
        Button {
            renameID = entry.id
            renameText = entry.title
            showRename = true
        } label: { Label("Rename", systemImage: "pencil") }
        Menu {
            shelfItems(for: [entry.id])
        } label: { Label("Move to Shelf", systemImage: "folder") }
        Button {
            library.markRead(id: entry.id, !entry.finished)
        } label: {
            Label(entry.finished ? "Mark as unread" : "Mark as read",
                  systemImage: entry.finished ? "arrow.uturn.backward" : "checkmark.circle")
        }
        Button {
            library.setManga(id: entry.id, !entry.isManga)
        } label: {
            Label(entry.isManga ? "Read left-to-right" : "Read right-to-left (manga)",
                  systemImage: "arrow.left.arrow.right")
        }
        Button {
            organizing = true
            selection = [entry.id]
        } label: { Label("Select…", systemImage: "checkmark.circle.badge.plus") }
        Button(role: .destructive) {
            deleteIDs = [entry.id]
            showDeleteComics = true
        } label: { Label("Delete", systemImage: "trash") }
    }
}

/// Lets a cell be picked up with a long press and dropped onto another comic or a shelf.
private struct ReorderModifier: ViewModifier {
    let enabled: Bool
    let id: UUID
    let onDrop: (UUID, UUID) -> Void

    @ViewBuilder
    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        if enabled {
            content
                .draggable(id.uuidString)
                .dropDestination(for: String.self) { items, _ in
                    guard let first = items.first, let dragged = UUID(uuidString: first) else { return false }
                    onDrop(dragged, id)
                    return true
                }
        } else {
            content
        }
    }
}

struct CoverView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var library: LibraryStore
    let entry: ComicEntry
    @State private var image: UIImage?

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Rectangle().fill(Theme.card2)
            if let img = image {
                Image(uiImage: img)
                    .resizable()
                    .scaledToFill()
            } else {
                Image(systemName: "book.closed.fill")
                    .font(.title)
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
            }
        }
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous))
        .shadow(color: .black.opacity(0.22), radius: 7, y: 4)
        .task(id: entry.id) {
            let url = library.coverURL(for: entry)
            if let hit = CoverCache.shared.cached(url) {
                image = hit
            } else {
                image = await CoverCache.shared.load(url)
            }
        }
    }
}

struct ComicCell: View {
    let entry: ComicEntry
    var organizing = false
    var selected = false

    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
            CoverView(entry: entry)
                .aspectRatio(2.0 / 3.0, contentMode: .fit)
                .overlay {
                    if organizing && selected {
                        RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10, style: .continuous).strokeBorder(Theme.accent, lineWidth: 3)
                    }
                }
                .overlay(alignment: .topLeading) {
                    if organizing {
                        Image(systemName: selected ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle")
                            .font(.title3)
                            .foregroundStyle(selected ? Theme.onAccent : Color.white, selected ? Theme.accent : Color.black.opacity(0.35))
                            .padding(6)
                    }
                }
                .overlay(alignment: .topTrailing) {
                    if entry.finished {
                        Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                            .font(.title3)
                            .foregroundStyle(Theme.onAccent, Theme.accent)
                            .padding(6)
                    }
                }
                .overlay(alignment: .bottom) {
                    if entry.hasStarted && !entry.finished {
                        ProgressView(value: entry.progress)
                            .tint(Theme.accent)
                            .padding(8)
                    }
                }
            Text(entry.title)
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(.white)
                .lineLimit(2)
                .multilineTextAlignment(.leading)
            Text("\(entry.format.label) · \(entry.pageCount) pages")
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
        }
        .contentShape(Rectangle())
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/PanelCanvas.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// Shows one comic page and smoothly "flies" the camera to the current panel.
struct PanelCanvas: View {
    let image: UIImage
    let panels: [CGRect]
    let panelIndex: Int
    let size: CGSize
    let fill: Double
    var animated = true          // false while a pinch hands a focused panel over to free zooming

    struct Camera {
        var zoom: CGFloat
        var dx: CGFloat
        var dy: CGFloat
        var width: CGFloat
        var height: CGFloat
    }

    static func camera(image: UIImage, panels: [CGRect], panelIndex: Int, size: CGSize, fill: Double) -> Camera {
        let iw = max(image.size.width, 1)
        let ih = max(image.size.height, 1)
        let fit = min(size.width / iw, size.height / ih)
        let dw = iw * fit
        let dh = ih * fit
        var zoom: CGFloat = 1
        var dx: CGFloat = 0
        var dy: CGFloat = 0
        if panelIndex >= 0 && panelIndex < panels.count {
            let p = panels[panelIndex]
            let pw = max(p.width * dw, 1)
            let ph = max(p.height * dh, 1)
            let wanted = min(size.width / pw, size.height / ph) * CGFloat(fill)
            zoom = min(max(wanted, 1), 7)
            dx = -(p.midX - 0.5) * dw * zoom
            dy = -(p.midY - 0.5) * dh * zoom
        }
        return Camera(zoom: zoom, dx: dx, dy: dy, width: dw, height: dh)
    }

    /// Which panel is under a tap? Converts the screen point to page coordinates (0...1) and hit-tests the panels.
    static func panel(at point: CGPoint, image: UIImage, panels: [CGRect], panelIndex: Int, size: CGSize, fill: Double) -> Int? {
        if panels.isEmpty { return nil }
        let cam = camera(image: image, panels: panels, panelIndex: panelIndex, size: size, fill: fill)
        let vx = (point.x - size.width / 2 - cam.dx) / cam.zoom
        let vy = (point.y - size.height / 2 - cam.dy) / cam.zoom
        let nx = 0.5 + vx / max(cam.width, 1)
        let ny = 0.5 + vy / max(cam.height, 1)
        let p = CGPoint(x: nx, y: ny)
        var best: Int?
        var bestArea = CGFloat.greatestFiniteMagnitude
        for (i, r) in panels.enumerated() where r.insetBy(dx: -0.01, dy: -0.01).contains(p) {
            let area = r.width * r.height
            if area < bestArea {
                bestArea = area
                best = i
            }
        }
        return best
    }

    private func camera() -> Camera {
        return PanelCanvas.camera(image: image, panels: panels, panelIndex: panelIndex, size: size, fill: fill)
    }

    var body: some View {
        let cam = camera()
        return Image(uiImage: image)
            .resizable()
            .interpolation(.high)
            .frame(width: cam.width, height: cam.height)
            .scaleEffect(cam.zoom)
            .offset(x: cam.dx, y: cam.dy)
            .animation(animated ? .spring(response: 0.46, dampingFraction: 0.9) : nil, value: panelIndex)
            .frame(width: size.width, height: size.height)
            .clipped()
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/ReaderView.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// Everything the reader needs once a comic is open.
final class ReaderSession: ObservableObject {
    let model: ReaderModel
    let companion: CompanionModel
    init(model: ReaderModel, companion: CompanionModel) {
        self.model = model
        self.companion = companion
    }
}

/// Opens the comic file (showing a spinner), then shows the reader.
struct ReaderHost: View {
    let entry: ComicEntry
    @EnvironmentObject var library: LibraryStore
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @State private var session: ReaderSession?
    @State private var failure: String?

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Color.black.ignoresSafeArea()
            if let s = session {
                ReaderView(entry: entry, model: s.model, companion: s.companion)
            } else if let message = failure {
                VStack(spacing: 16) {
                    Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                        .font(.system(size: 44))
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    Text(message)
                        .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        .padding(.horizontal, 32)
                    Button("Close") { dismiss() }
                        .buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle())
                }
            } else {
                VStack(spacing: 14) {
                    ProgressView()
                        .tint(.white)
                        .scaleEffect(1.3)
                    Text("Opening…")
                        .font(.footnote)
                        .foregroundStyle(.white.opacity(0.7))
                }
            }
        }
        .task {
            await open()
        }
    }

    private func open() async {
        let url = library.fileURL(for: entry)
        let name = entry.originalName
        let result = await Task.detached(priority: .userInitiated) { () -> Result<(ComicSource, ComicIdentity), Error> in
            do {
                let source = try ComicOpener.open(url: url)
                let identity = IdentityBuilder.make(originalName: name, source: source)
                return .success((source, identity))
            } catch {
                return .failure(error)
            }
        }.value
        switch result {
        case .success(let pair):
            let model = ReaderModel(
                entry: entry,
                source: pair.0,
                identity: pair.1,
                snap: settings.snapToPanels,
                pageFirst: settings.showFullPageFirst
            )
            let companion = CompanionModel(entry: entry, identity: pair.1, settings: settings, coverURL: library.coverURL(for: entry))
            session = ReaderSession(model: model, companion: companion)
        case .failure(let error):
            failure = error.localizedDescription
        }
    }
}

struct ReaderView: View {
    let entry: ComicEntry
    @ObservedObject var model: ReaderModel
    @ObservedObject var companion: CompanionModel

    @EnvironmentObject var library: LibraryStore
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss

    @State private var chromeVisible = true
    @State private var showCompanion = false
    // Manual zoom (pinch or double-tap). 1 = off, so everything behaves exactly as before until you zoom.
    @State private var zoom = ZoomState()          // not observed here: zooming redraws the page only
    @State private var lastTap: (time: Date, point: CGPoint)?
    @State private var pendingTap: Task<Void, Never>?
    @State private var scrub: Double = 0
    @State private var scrubbing = false
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase

    /// Puts the slider back on the page that is really showing (after returning to the app, or an interrupted drag).
    private func syncSlider() {
        scrubbing = false
        scrub = Double(model.pageIndex)
    }
    @State private var hideTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    @State private var started = false

    private let haptic = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .light)

    var body: some View {
        GeometryReader { geo in
            let wide = geo.size.width >= 640
            HStack(spacing: 0) {
                stage(size: stageSize(geo.size, wide: wide))
                if wide && showCompanion {
                    Divider()
                    CompanionView(model: companion, onClose: { toggleCompanion() })
                        .frame(width: min(420, geo.size.width * 0.4))
                        .background(Theme.bg)
                        .transition(.move(edge: .trailing))
                }
            }
            .background(Color.black)
            .sheet(isPresented: Binding(
                get: { showCompanion && !wide },
                set: { newValue in if !newValue { showCompanion = false } }
            )) {
                CompanionView(model: companion, onClose: { showCompanion = false })
                    .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
                    .presentationDragIndicator(.visible)
                    .presentationBackgroundInteraction(.enabled(upThrough: .medium))
            }
        }
        .ignoresSafeArea()
        .statusBarHidden(!chromeVisible)
        .persistentSystemOverlays(chromeVisible ? .automatic : .hidden)
        .onAppear {
            if !started {
                started = true
                model.start()
                companion.load()
            }
            UIApplication.shared.isIdleTimerDisabled = true
            haptic.prepare()
            syncSlider()
            scheduleHide()
        }
        .onDisappear {
            UIApplication.shared.isIdleTimerDisabled = false
            saveProgress()
            model.finish()
        }
        .onChange(of: model.panelIndex) { _, _ in
            haptic.impactOccurred(intensity: 0.5)
            saveProgress()
        }
        .onChange(of: model.pageIndex) { _, newValue in
            if !scrubbing { scrub = Double(newValue) }
            saveProgress()
        }
        .onChange(of: model.pageCount) { _, _ in syncSlider() }
        .onChange(of: chromeVisible) { _, shown in if shown { syncSlider() } }
        .onChange(of: scenePhase) { _, phase in if phase == .active { syncSlider() } }
        .onChange(of: model.pageIndex) { _, _ in resetZoom() }
        .onChange(of: model.panelIndex) { _, _ in if !zoom.converting { resetZoom() } }
        .onChange(of: settings.snapToPanels) { _, _ in
            model.updateSettings(snap: settings.snapToPanels, pageFirst: settings.showFullPageFirst)
        }
        .onChange(of: settings.showFullPageFirst) { _, _ in
            model.updateSettings(snap: settings.snapToPanels, pageFirst: settings.showFullPageFirst)
        }
    }

    private func stageSize(_ total: CGSize, wide: Bool) -> CGSize {
        if wide && showCompanion {
            return CGSize(width: total.width - min(420, total.width * 0.4) - 1, height: total.height)
        }
        return total
    }

    // MARK: Stage

    private func stage(size: CGSize) -> some View {
        ZStack {
            Color.black

            if let img = model.image {
                ZoomedContent(zoom: zoom) {
                    PanelCanvas(
                        image: img,
                        panels: model.panels,
                        panelIndex: model.panelIndex,
                        size: size,
                        fill: 0.96,
                        animated: !zoom.converting
                    )
                    .id(model.pageIndex)
                    .transition(.opacity)
                    .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.3), value: model.pageIndex)
                }
            }

            if model.isLoading && model.image == nil {
                ProgressView().tint(.white).scaleEffect(1.3)
            }

            if let err = model.loadError {
                Text(err)
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .foregroundStyle(.white)
                    .padding(12)
                    .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12, style: .continuous))
            }

            // Gesture layer
            Color.clear
                .contentShape(Rectangle())
                .gesture(
                    SpatialTapGesture().onEnded { value in
                        handleTap(at: value.location, size: size)
                    }
                )
                .simultaneousGesture(
                    DragGesture(minimumDistance: 24)
                        .onEnded { value in
                            // Zoomed in, or just pinched: the drag belongs to the zoom, so it must not turn the page.
                            if zoom.isZoomed || Date().timeIntervalSince(zoom.lastPinch) < 0.45 { return }
                            handleDrag(value.translation)
                        }
                )

            ZoomGestures(zoom: zoom, enabled: !showCompanion && !scrubbing && !model.reachedEnd,
                         panelFocused: model.panelIndex >= 0,
                         onFlick: { handleDrag($0) },
                         onPinchedOut: { model.showOverview() },
                         onLeavePanel: { leavePanel(size: size) },
                         onEdgeSwipe: { swipedLeft in
                             hideChrome()
                             model.turnPage(forward: model.rtl ? !swipedLeft : swipedLeft)
                         })
                .allowsHitTesting(false)

            // Chrome
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                if chromeVisible { topBar(size: size).transition(.move(edge: .top).combined(with: .opacity)) }
                Spacer()
                if chromeVisible { bottomBar.transition(.move(edge: .bottom).combined(with: .opacity)) }
            }
            .animation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.22), value: chromeVisible)

            if model.reachedEnd {
                endCard
            }
        }
        .frame(width: size.width, height: size.height)
        .clipped()
    }

    // MARK: Gestures

    /// Tap rules:
    ///  - the outer edges step to the previous / next panel (and onto the next page),
    ///  - a tap on a panel inside the middle area jumps straight to that panel,
    ///  - anywhere else in the middle shows or hides the controls.
    private func handleTap(at point: CGPoint, size: CGSize) {
        let width = max(size.width, 1)
        let f = point.x / width
        let edge: CGFloat = model.panelIndex < 0 ? 0.095 : 0.17    // whole page: slim edges so panels near them stay tappable
        // The edges stay instant. Only the middle waits a moment, to see whether a second tap is coming.
        if f < edge {
            if model.rtl { forward() } else { back() }
            return
        }
        if f > 1 - edge {
            if model.rtl { back() } else { forward() }
            return
        }
        if let last = lastTap, Date().timeIntervalSince(last.time) < 0.3,
           hypot(point.x - last.point.x, point.y - last.point.y) < 60 {
            pendingTap?.cancel()
            lastTap = nil
            doubleTap(at: point, size: size)
            return
        }
        lastTap = (Date(), point)
        pendingTap?.cancel()
        pendingTap = Task {
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 300_000_000)
            if Task.isCancelled { return }
            lastTap = nil
            middleTap(at: point, size: size)
        }
    }

    private func middleTap(at point: CGPoint, size: CGSize) {
        if let img = model.image {
            // Undo any zoom or nudge the reader has applied, so the tap lands on the panel that is really under the finger.
            let z = max(zoom.zoom, 0.01)
            let base = CGPoint(x: size.width / 2 + (point.x - size.width / 2 - zoom.offset.width) / z,
                               y: size.height / 2 + (point.y - size.height / 2 - zoom.offset.height) / z)
            if let hit = PanelCanvas.panel(at: base, image: img, panels: model.panels,
                                           panelIndex: model.panelIndex, size: size, fill: 0.96) {
                hideChrome()
                if hit != model.panelIndex {
                    resetZoomInstantly()
                    model.focus(panel: hit)
                } else if isZoomed {
                    zoom.reset()          // already on this panel but nudged or zoomed: snap it back
                }
                return
            }
        }
        if isZoomed { return }
        chromeVisible.toggle()
        if chromeVisible { scheduleHide() }
    }

    private func resetZoomInstantly() {
        zoom.set(zoom: 1, offset: .zero)
    }

    // MARK: Manual zoom

    private var isZoomed: Bool { return zoom.isZoomed }

    private func resetZoom() { zoom.reset() }

    /// Pinching while a panel is focused: show the whole page, keeping exactly what is on screen, so the page can be explored.
    private func leavePanel(size: CGSize) {
        guard model.panelIndex >= 0, let img = model.image else { return }
        let cam = PanelCanvas.camera(image: img, panels: model.panels, panelIndex: model.panelIndex, size: size, fill: 0.96)
        let z = zoom.zoom
        let newZoom = z * cam.zoom
        let newOffset = CGSize(width: z * cam.dx + zoom.offset.width, height: z * cam.dy + zoom.offset.height)
        zoom.converting = true
        var t = Transaction()
        t.disablesAnimations = true
        withTransaction(t) {
            model.showOverview()
            zoom.set(zoom: newZoom, offset: zoom.clamp(newOffset, zoom: newZoom, size: size))
        }
        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.3) { zoom.converting = false }
    }

    /// Double-tap: zoom in on the spot you tapped, or zoom back out.
    private func doubleTap(at point: CGPoint, size: CGSize) {
        if isZoomed { resetZoom(); return }
        let z: CGFloat = 2.5
        let c = CGPoint(x: size.width / 2, y: size.height / 2)
        let off = CGSize(width: (point.x - c.x) * (1 - z), height: (point.y - c.y) * (1 - z))
        hideChrome()
        withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.25)) {
            zoom.set(zoom: z, offset: zoom.clamp(off, zoom: z, size: size))
        }
    }

    /// Swipe left/right turns the page, swipe up opens the Companion, swipe down shows the menu (or closes the Companion).
    private func handleDrag(_ t: CGSize) {
        let dx = t.width
        let dy = t.height
        if abs(dx) > 50 && abs(dx) > abs(dy) * 1.3 {
            let swipedLeft = dx < 0
            hideChrome()
            // Swiping left brings the next page (right-to-left comics swipe the other way).
            model.turnPage(forward: model.rtl ? !swipedLeft : swipedLeft)
        } else if dy < -80 && abs(dx) < 70 {
            if !showCompanion { toggleCompanion() }
        } else if dy > 90 && abs(dx) < 70 {
            if showCompanion {
                showCompanion = false
            } else {
                chromeVisible = true          // swipe down = bring up the menu
                scheduleHide()
            }
        }
    }

    private func forward() {
        hideChrome()
        model.goForward()
    }

    private func back() {
        hideChrome()
        model.goBack()
    }

    private func hideChrome() {
        if chromeVisible { chromeVisible = false }
    }

    private func scheduleHide() {
        hideTask?.cancel()
        hideTask = Task {
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 5_000_000_000)
            if Task.isCancelled { return }
            chromeVisible = false
        }
    }

    private func toggleCompanion() {
        withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.25)) {
            showCompanion.toggle()
        }
        if showCompanion { companion.load() }
    }

    private func saveProgress() {
        library.recordProgress(
            id: entry.id,
            page: model.pageIndex,
            panel: model.panelIndex,
            finished: model.reachedEnd
        )
    }

    private func close() {
        saveProgress()
        model.finish()
        dismiss()
    }

    // MARK: Chrome

    private func topBar(size: CGSize) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: 12) {
            Button(action: { close() }) {
                Image(systemName: "chevron.down")
                    .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                    .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                    .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: Circle())
            }
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 1) {
                Text(entry.title)
                    .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    .lineLimit(1)
                Text(positionText)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundStyle(.white.opacity(0.7))
            }
            Spacer(minLength: 0)
            readerMenu
        }
        .foregroundStyle(.white)
        .padding(.leading, 16)
        .padding(.trailing, Theme.cameraWidth + 30)      // keep the menu button clear of the Duo status widget
        .padding(.top, 54)
        .padding(.bottom, 22)
        .background(
            LinearGradient(colors: [Color.black.opacity(0.65), Color.black.opacity(0)], startPoint: .top, endPoint: .bottom)
        )
    }

    /// Everything you might want while reading, in one place.
    private var readerMenu: some View {
        Menu {
            Toggle(isOn: $settings.snapToPanels) { Label("Snap Panel to Panel", systemImage: "rectangle.split.2x2") }
            Toggle(isOn: $settings.showFullPageFirst) { Label("Whole Page First", systemImage: "doc") }
            Divider()
            if !model.panels.isEmpty {
                Button { model.toggleOverview() } label: {
                    Label(model.panelIndex < 0 ? "Zoom to first panel" : "Show whole page", systemImage: "viewfinder")
                }
            }
            Button { toggleCompanion() } label: { Label("Companion", systemImage: "sparkles") }
            Button { model.reachedEnd = true; close() } label: { Label("Mark as Read and Close", systemImage: "checkmark.circle") }
            Divider()
            Button { close() } label: { Label("Back to Library", systemImage: "chevron.down") }
        } label: {
            Image(systemName: "ellipsis")
                .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .bold))
                .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: Circle())
        }
    }

    private var positionText: String {
        var s = "Page \(model.pageIndex + 1) of \(model.pageCount)"
        if model.panelIndex >= 0 && !model.panels.isEmpty {
            s += "  ·  Panel \(model.panelIndex + 1)/\(model.panels.count)"
        }
        return s
    }

    private var bottomBar: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 4) {
            HStack(spacing: 12) {
                Button(action: { model.toggleOverview() }) {
                    Image(systemName: model.panelIndex < 0 ? "viewfinder" : "rectangle.expand.vertical")
                        .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(.white)
                        .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                        .background(.ultraThinMaterial, in: Circle())
                }
                if model.pageCount > 1 {
                    Slider(
                        value: $scrub,
                        in: 0...Double(max(1, model.pageCount - 1)),
                        step: 1,
                        onEditingChanged: { editing in
                            scrubbing = editing
                            if !editing { model.jump(toPage: Int(scrub)) }
                        }
                    )
                    .tint(Theme.accent)
                } else {
                    Spacer()
                }
                Button(action: { toggleCompanion() }) {
                    Image(systemName: "sparkles")
                        .font(.system(size: 16, weight: .semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.onAccent)
                        .frame(width: 40, height: 40)
                        .background(Theme.accent, in: Circle())
                }
            }
            HStack {
                Text("\(Int(scrub) + 1)")
                Spacer()
                Text("\(model.pageCount)")
            }
            .font(.caption2.monospacedDigit())
            .foregroundStyle(.white.opacity(0.65))
            .padding(.horizontal, 62)
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 16)
        .padding(.top, 14)
        .padding(.bottom, 26)
        .background(
            LinearGradient(colors: [Color.black.opacity(0), Color.black.opacity(0.7)], startPoint: .top, endPoint: .bottom)
        )
    }

    private var endCard: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 14) {
            Image(systemName: "checkmark.seal.fill")
                .font(.system(size: 44))
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
            Text("The End")
                .font(.title3.weight(.bold))
            Text(entry.title)
                .font(.subheadline)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .lineLimit(2)
            HStack(spacing: 10) {
                Button("Keep Reading") { model.reachedEnd = false }
                    .buttonStyle(GreyButtonStyle())
                Button("Done") { close() }
                    .buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle())
            }
        }
        .padding(26)
        .background(Theme.card, in: RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 26, style: .continuous))
        .padding(40)
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/SettingsView.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

struct SettingsView: View {
    @EnvironmentObject var settings: AppSettings
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @State private var topInset: CGFloat = 0
    @State private var landscape = false

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                header
                Form {
                    Section(header: Text("Reading"),
                            footer: Text(settings.snapToPanels
                                         ? "Zooms in on one panel at a time. Tap or swipe to move to the next."
                                         : "Shows whole pages. Swipe to turn the page and pinch to zoom. Panels are not detected.")) {
                        Picker("Reading Mode", selection: $settings.snapToPanels) {
                            Text("Panel by Panel").tag(true)
                            Text("Regular Pages").tag(false)
                        }
                        .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                        if settings.snapToPanels {
                            Toggle("Show Whole Page First", isOn: $settings.showFullPageFirst)
                        }
                    }

                    Section(header: Text("Companion"), footer: Text("Character info comes from a guide built into the app. With lookups on, PanelSnap also fetches story summaries from public sources such as Wikipedia. No account or key is needed.")) {
                        Toggle("Look Up Comic Info Online", isOn: $settings.onlineLookup)
                        Toggle("Hide Spoilers", isOn: $settings.spoilerShield)
                    }

                    Section(header: Text("Theme"), footer: Text("Choose a preset, or pick your own two colors.")) {
                        presetRow
                        ColorPicker("Main Color", selection: colorBinding(\.accentHex), supportsOpacity: false)
                        ColorPicker("Second Color", selection: colorBinding(\.highlightHex), supportsOpacity: false)
                        Button("Reset to Comic Colors") {
                            settings.accentHex = ThemePreset.comic.accent
                            settings.highlightHex = ThemePreset.comic.highlight
                        }
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.secondary)
                    }

                    Section(header: Text("Discover"), footer: Text("A browsable comic catalogue from League of Comic Geeks: search, new releases, and suggestions based on your library, with a button to search your Home website for any of them. Its button sits at the end of the shelf row on the library.")) {
                        Toggle("Show Discover", isOn: $settings.showDiscover)
                    }

                    Section(header: Text("Library"), footer: Text("Your comics are stored in Files › On My iPhone › PanelSnap › Comics.")) {
                        Button { Paths.showInFiles() } label: {
                            Label("Show Comics Folder in Files", systemImage: "folder")
                        }
                    }
                }
                .scrollContentBackground(.hidden)
            }
            .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: landscape ? .top : [])       // wide: start at the very top, level with the status widget
            .background(Theme.bg.ignoresSafeArea())
            .toolbar(.hidden, for: .navigationBar)
        }
        .readSafeTop($topInset)
        .readLandscape($landscape)
    }

    private func colorBinding(_ keyPath: ReferenceWritableKeyPath<AppSettings, String>) -> Binding<Color> {
        return Binding(
            get: { Color(hex: settings[keyPath: keyPath]) },
            set: { settings[keyPath: keyPath] = $0.hexString }
        )
    }

    private var presetRow: some View {
        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
            HStack(spacing: 16) {
                ForEach(ThemePreset.all) { preset in
                    let selected = settings.accentHex == preset.accent && settings.highlightHex == preset.highlight
                    Button {
                        settings.accentHex = preset.accent
                        settings.highlightHex = preset.highlight
                    } label: {
                        VStack(spacing: 6) {
                            Circle()
                                .fill(LinearGradient(stops: [
                                    .init(color: Color(hex: preset.accent), location: 0.5),
                                    .init(color: Color(hex: preset.highlight), location: 0.5)
                                ], startPoint: .topLeading, endPoint: .bottomTrailing))
                                .frame(width: 46, height: 46)
                                .overlay(Circle().strokeBorder(Color.white, lineWidth: selected ? 3 : 0))
                            Text(preset.name)
                                .font(.caption2.weight(.semibold))
                                .foregroundStyle(selected ? Color.white : Theme.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }
            .padding(.vertical, 4)
        }
    }

    /// Our own header, so iOS can't wrap the button in a glass bubble and clip it.
    private var header: some View {
        ZStack {
            ComicTitle(text: "Settings")
            HStack {
                Button("Done") { dismiss() }
                    .buttonStyle(YellowButtonStyle(compact: true))
                Spacer()
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 20)
        .padding(.top, topInset > 20 ? (landscape ? 22 : 8) : 30)
        .padding(.bottom, 12)
        // Span the whole screen, so the title sits in the true centre (the Duo's status-widget inset would
        // otherwise push it left of centre).
        .ignoresSafeArea(.container, edges: .horizontal)
    }
}

===== PanelSnap/Views/ZoomLayer.swift =====
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// The reader's manual zoom (pinch and double-tap). It lives in its own object so that zooming only redraws the page,
/// not the whole reader, which is what keeps it smooth.
final class ZoomState: ObservableObject {
    private(set) var zoom: CGFloat = 1
    private(set) var offset: CGSize = .zero
    var lastPinch = Date.distantPast
    var converting = false          // true while switching from a focused panel to the whole page, so the zoom is not reset

    var isZoomed: Bool { return zoom > 1.02 }

    /// Changes zoom and offset together, so the page is redrawn once per frame instead of twice.
    func set(zoom z: CGFloat, offset o: CGSize) {
        objectWillChange.send()
        zoom = z
        offset = o
    }

    func set(offset o: CGSize) { set(zoom: zoom, offset: o) }

    func reset() {
        withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.22)) { set(zoom: 1, offset: .zero) }
    }

    /// How far the page may be moved either way before its edge shows.
    func limits(zoom z: CGFloat, size: CGSize) -> CGSize {
        return CGSize(width: max(z - 1, 0) * size.width / 2, height: max(z - 1, 0) * size.height / 2)
    }

    /// Keeps a zoomed page from being dragged completely out of view.
    func clamp(_ o: CGSize, zoom z: CGFloat, size: CGSize) -> CGSize {
        let m = limits(zoom: z, size: size)
        return CGSize(width: min(max(o.width, -m.width), m.width), height: min(max(o.height, -m.height), m.height))
    }
}

/// Applies the zoom to the page.
struct ZoomedContent<Content: View>: View {
    @ObservedObject var zoom: ZoomState
    @ViewBuilder var content: () -> Content

    var body: some View {
        content()
            .scaleEffect(zoom.zoom)
            .offset(zoom.offset)
    }
}

/// Pinch-to-zoom around the point between your two fingers, with the page following your fingers as they move,
/// plus one-finger dragging while zoomed in (with a little glide when you let go). The recognisers sit on the window,
/// so the reader's own taps and swipes keep working exactly as they did.
struct ZoomGestures: UIViewRepresentable {
    let zoom: ZoomState
    var enabled: Bool
    var panelFocused: Bool                 // on a snapped panel: one finger nudges it, a quick flick still swipes
    var onFlick: (CGSize) -> Void          // a quick flick on a snapped panel: the same as a swipe
    var onPinchedOut: () -> Void           // pinching in on a page that is not zoomed: show the whole page
    var onLeavePanel: () -> Void           // pinching on a focused panel: switch to free zooming on the whole page
    var onEdgeSwipe: (Bool) -> Void        // zoomed in and pushed past the side of the page: true = swiped left

    func makeCoordinator() -> Coordinator { Coordinator(zoom: zoom) }

    func makeUIView(context: Context) -> HookView {
        let v = HookView()
        v.isUserInteractionEnabled = false
        v.coordinator = context.coordinator
        return v
    }

    func updateUIView(_ uiView: HookView, context: Context) {
        context.coordinator.enabled = enabled
        context.coordinator.panelFocused = panelFocused
        context.coordinator.onFlick = onFlick
        context.coordinator.onPinchedOut = onPinchedOut
        context.coordinator.onLeavePanel = onLeavePanel
        context.coordinator.onEdgeSwipe = onEdgeSwipe
    }

    final class HookView: UIView {
        weak var coordinator: Coordinator?
        override func didMoveToWindow() {
            super.didMoveToWindow()
            if let window = window { coordinator?.attach(to: window, stage: self) } else { coordinator?.detach() }
        }
    }

    final class Coordinator: NSObject, UIGestureRecognizerDelegate {
        let zoom: ZoomState
        var enabled = true
        var panelFocused = false
        var onFlick: (CGSize) -> Void = { _ in }
        var onPinchedOut: () -> Void = {}
        var onLeavePanel: () -> Void = {}
        var onEdgeSwipe: (Bool) -> Void = { _ in }
        private var startedAtLeftEdge = false, startedAtRightEdge = false
        private var left = false
        private var baseScale: CGFloat = 1       // the recogniser's scale when the current two-finger hold began
        private var touches = 2
        private weak var stage: UIView?
        private weak var window: UIWindow?
        private lazy var pinch = UIPinchGestureRecognizer(target: self, action: #selector(pinched(_:)))
        private lazy var pan = UIPanGestureRecognizer(target: self, action: #selector(panned(_:)))
        private var start: (zoom: CGFloat, offset: CGSize, point: CGPoint)?
        private var panBase: CGSize = .zero
        private var panTouches = 1               // fingers on the pan last frame; a change means the drag must start afresh
        private var pinchSeen = false            // a pinch ran during this drag, so it is not a swipe
        private var link: CADisplayLink?
        private var glideV = CGPoint.zero        // points per second
        private var glideStamp: CFTimeInterval = 0
        private var glideSize = CGSize.zero

        init(zoom: ZoomState) { self.zoom = zoom }

        func attach(to window: UIWindow, stage: UIView) {
            detach()
            self.window = window
            self.stage = stage
            pan.maximumNumberOfTouches = 2
            for r in [pinch, pan] as [UIGestureRecognizer] {
                r.delegate = self
                r.cancelsTouchesInView = false
                r.delaysTouchesBegan = false
                r.delaysTouchesEnded = false
                window.addGestureRecognizer(r)
            }
        }

        func detach() {
            stopGlide()
            window?.removeGestureRecognizer(pinch)
            window?.removeGestureRecognizer(pan)
            window = nil
        }

        // MARK: Delegate

        func gestureRecognizerShouldBegin(_ g: UIGestureRecognizer) -> Bool {
            guard enabled, let stage = stage, stage.window != nil else { return false }
            if !stage.bounds.contains(g.location(in: stage)) { return false }
            if g === pan { return zoom.isZoomed || panelFocused }   // one finger moves the page when zoomed in, or nudges a snapped panel
            return true
        }

        func gestureRecognizer(_ g: UIGestureRecognizer, shouldRecognizeSimultaneouslyWith other: UIGestureRecognizer) -> Bool {
            return true
        }

        func gestureRecognizer(_ g: UIGestureRecognizer, shouldReceive touch: UITouch) -> Bool {
            if touch.phase == .began { stopGlide() }    // putting a finger down catches a gliding page where it is
            return !(touch.view is UIControl)           // leave sliders and buttons alone
        }

        // MARK: Pinch

        @objc private func pinched(_ g: UIPinchGestureRecognizer) {
            guard let stage = stage else { return }
            let size = stage.bounds.size
            let c = CGPoint(x: size.width / 2, y: size.height / 2)
            switch g.state {
            case .began:
                stopGlide()
                left = false
                baseScale = 1
                touches = g.numberOfTouches
                start = (zoom.zoom, zoom.offset, g.location(in: stage))
                zoom.lastPinch = Date()
            case .changed:
                guard var s = start else { return }
                // With fewer than two fingers down, iOS reports the position of the one left, which would yank the page
                // toward it. Ignore those frames, and start afresh from the current state when the second finger returns.
                if g.numberOfTouches < 2 { touches = g.numberOfTouches; return }
                if touches != g.numberOfTouches {
                    touches = g.numberOfTouches
                    s = (zoom.zoom, zoom.offset, g.location(in: stage))
                    start = s
                    baseScale = g.scale
                    return
                }
                if !left && abs(g.scale / baseScale - 1) > 0.015 {
                    // Pinching on a focused panel: go to the whole page at the same apparent zoom, so you can roam it freely.
                    left = true
                    onLeavePanel()
                    s = (zoom.zoom, zoom.offset, s.point)
                    start = s
                }
                // The spot of the page that was between your fingers when you started stays between them,
                // wherever they move, so the page zooms and follows you at the same time.
                let z = min(max(s.zoom * g.scale / baseScale, 0.8), 12)
                let now = g.location(in: stage)
                let ratio = z / s.zoom
                let off = CGSize(width: (now.x - c.x) - ratio * (s.point.x - c.x - s.offset.width),
                                 height: (now.y - c.y) - ratio * (s.point.y - c.y - s.offset.height))
                zoom.set(zoom: z, offset: zoom.clamp(off, zoom: z, size: size))
                zoom.lastPinch = Date()
            case .ended, .cancelled, .failed:
                guard let s = start else { return }
                start = nil
                zoom.lastPinch = Date()
                if zoom.zoom < 1.05 || (left && g.scale < 0.7) {
                    let showWholePage = s.zoom <= 1.02 && g.scale < 0.85
                    zoom.reset()
                    if showWholePage { onPinchedOut() }
                } else {
                    withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.2)) {
                        zoom.set(offset: zoom.clamp(zoom.offset, zoom: zoom.zoom, size: size))
                    }
                }
            default:
                break
            }
        }

        // MARK: Gliding (momentum after a drag, and the spring back from an edge)

        private func stopGlide() {
            link?.invalidate()
            link = nil
        }

        private func startGlide(velocity: CGPoint, size: CGSize) {
            stopGlide()
            glideV = velocity
            glideSize = size
            glideStamp = 0
            let l = CADisplayLink(target: self, selector: #selector(glideStep(_:)))
            l.add(to: .main, forMode: .common)
            link = l
        }

        /// One frame of momentum. It moves the real offset (not a pending animation), so a touch can catch it exactly,
        /// and a page dragged past its edge eases back while the rest of the movement carries on.
        @objc private func glideStep(_ l: CADisplayLink) {
            guard zoom.isZoomed else { stopGlide(); return }
            let dt = glideStamp == 0 ? l.duration : min(l.timestamp - glideStamp, 1.0 / 20)
            glideStamp = l.timestamp
            let m = zoom.limits(zoom: zoom.zoom, size: glideSize)
            let x = Coordinator.advance(zoom.offset.width, limit: m.width, v: &glideV.x, dt: dt)
            let y = Coordinator.advance(zoom.offset.height, limit: m.height, v: &glideV.y, dt: dt)
            zoom.set(offset: CGSize(width: x.pos, height: y.pos))
            if !x.moving && !y.moving { stopGlide() }
        }

        private static func advance(_ p: CGFloat, limit: CGFloat, v: inout CGFloat, dt: Double) -> (pos: CGFloat, moving: Bool) {
            if p < -limit || p > limit {                       // past the edge: ease back to it
                let target = p < 0 ? -limit : limit
                v = 0
                let np = p + (target - p) * CGFloat(1 - exp(-dt * 16))
                return abs(target - np) < 0.5 ? (target, false) : (np, true)
            }
            v *= CGFloat(pow(0.998, dt * 1000))                 // the same deceleration as a scroll view
            var np = p + v * CGFloat(dt)
            if np < -limit || np > limit {                      // reached the edge: stop there
                np = min(max(np, -limit), limit)
                v = 0
            }
            return (np, abs(v) > 20)
        }

        // MARK: Pan (zoomed in)

        @objc private func panned(_ g: UIPanGestureRecognizer) {
            guard let stage = stage else { return }
            if pinch.state == .began || pinch.state == .changed { pinchSeen = true; return }
            let size = stage.bounds.size
            if !zoom.isZoomed && !converted {
                nudge(g, size: size)
                return
            }
            switch g.state {
            case .began:
                rebase(g, size: size)
            case .changed:
                // After a pinch, or when a finger is added or lifted, carry on from where the page is now
                // rather than from where the drag first began (which would make the page jump).
                if pinchSeen || g.numberOfTouches != panTouches { rebase(g, size: size); return }
                let t = g.translation(in: stage)
                let raw = CGSize(width: panBase.width + t.x, height: panBase.height + t.y)
                let held = zoom.clamp(raw, zoom: zoom.zoom, size: size)
                // Past the edge the page stretches a little and resists, so you can feel that the edge was reached.
                zoom.set(offset: CGSize(width: held.width + (raw.width - held.width) * 0.3,
                                        height: held.height + (raw.height - held.height) * 0.3))
            case .ended:
                converted = false
                let v = g.velocity(in: stage)
                let t = g.translation(in: stage)
                let raw = CGSize(width: panBase.width + t.x, height: panBase.height + t.y)
                let held = zoom.clamp(raw, zoom: zoom.zoom, size: size)
                let pushed = raw.width - held.width          // > 0: dragged right past the left edge; < 0: past the right edge
                let horizontal = abs(t.x) > abs(t.y) * 1.3
                let toNext = startedAtRightEdge && pushed < -40 || startedAtRightEdge && pushed < -8 && v.x < -700
                let toPrevious = startedAtLeftEdge && pushed > 40 || startedAtLeftEdge && pushed > 8 && v.x > 700
                if !pinchSeen && horizontal && (toNext || toPrevious) {
                    withAnimation(.easeOut(duration: 0.2)) { zoom.set(offset: held) }
                    onEdgeSwipe(toNext)
                } else {
                    startGlide(velocity: v, size: size)
                }
            case .cancelled:
                converted = false
                startGlide(velocity: .zero, size: size)
            default:
                break
            }
        }

        /// Starts (or restarts) a drag from the page's current position.
        private func rebase(_ g: UIPanGestureRecognizer, size: CGSize) {
            stopGlide()
            pinchSeen = false
            panTouches = g.numberOfTouches
            g.setTranslation(.zero, in: stage)
            panBase = zoom.offset
            // Only a drag that starts against a side of the page can carry on into a swipe to the next one.
            let maxX = zoom.limits(zoom: zoom.zoom, size: size).width
            startedAtLeftEdge = zoom.offset.width >= maxX - 2
            startedAtRightEdge = zoom.offset.width <= -maxX + 2
        }

        // MARK: Nudging a snapped panel

        private var nudgeBase: CGSize = .zero
        private var converted = false     // this drag has turned into free panning of the whole page

        /// On a snapped panel a quick flick is a swipe. A slower drag lets go of the panel and moves around the
        /// whole page, at the same zoom, so you can look anywhere (the same hand-over a pinch makes).
        private func nudge(_ g: UIPanGestureRecognizer, size: CGSize) {
            switch g.state {
            case .began:
                converted = false
                pinchSeen = false
                g.setTranslation(.zero, in: stage)
            case .changed:
                let t = g.translation(in: stage), v = g.velocity(in: stage)
                zoom.lastPinch = Date()             // keeps the reader's own swipe from also firing
                if hypot(t.x, t.y) > 6 && hypot(v.x, v.y) < 450 {
                    converted = true
                    onLeavePanel()
                    rebase(g, size: size)
                }
            case .ended:
                let v = g.velocity(in: stage), t = g.translation(in: stage)
                zoom.lastPinch = Date()
                let ax = abs(v.x), ay = abs(v.y)
                if !pinchSeen && max(ax, ay) > 700 && max(ax, ay) > 1.3 * min(ax, ay) {
                    onFlick(CGSize(width: t.x, height: t.y))
                }
            default:
                break
            }
        }
    }
}

===== Scripts/PanelCheck/Stubs.swift =====
// Stand-ins for the app pieces the detector touches, so it can run as a command-line check.
import Foundation
enum Console { static func log(_ s: String) { print(s) } }
enum Paths { static var caches: URL { return FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory } }

===== Scripts/PanelCheck/main.swift =====
// CI check: runs the app's own panel detector (model, gutter splitting, reading order) on a test page and
// compares the panels with the expected ones. Usage: check test-page.png expected.json
import Foundation
import CoreGraphics
import ImageIO

let args = CommandLine.arguments
guard let src = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL(URL(fileURLWithPath: args[1]) as CFURL, nil),
      let image = CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(src, 0, nil) else {
    print("::error::Could not read the test page."); exit(1)
}
let expected = try! JSONDecoder().decode([[Double]].self, from: Data(contentsOf: URL(fileURLWithPath: args[2])))
guard let found = MLPanelDetector.detect(image: image, rtl: false) else {
    print("::error::The panel detector could not run."); exit(1)
}
for r in found { print(String(format: "  panel x %.3f-%.3f  y %.3f-%.3f", r.minX, r.maxX, r.minY, r.maxY)) }
var ok = found.count == expected.count
if ok {
    for (r, e) in zip(found, expected) {
        let d = [abs(r.minX - e[0]), abs(r.minY - e[1]), abs(r.maxX - e[2]), abs(r.maxY - e[3])].max()!
        if d > 0.03 { ok = false }
    }
}
if !ok {
    print("::error::Panel detector found \(found.count) panels, expected \(expected.count) (or they are in the wrong place or order).")
    exit(1)
}
print("Panel detector check passed: \(found.count) panels in the right order.")

===== Scripts/build-ipa.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Builds an UNSIGNED PanelSnap.ipa. Tries the full build first (CBZ + CBR + CB7 + PDF + EPUB).
# If the optional RAR/7z libraries ever fail to compile, it automatically falls back to a
# "lite" build (CBZ + PDF + EPUB) so you ALWAYS get an IPA.
set -uo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

SUMMARY="${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-/dev/null}"

build() {
  local spec="$1" log="$2"
  rm -rf build PanelSnap.xcodeproj
  echo "::group::XcodeGen ($spec)"
  xcodegen generate --spec "$spec" || { echo "::endgroup::"; return 1; }
  echo "::endgroup::"
  echo "::group::xcodebuild ($spec)"
  xcodebuild \
    -project PanelSnap.xcodeproj \
    -scheme PanelSnap \
    -configuration Release \
    -sdk iphoneos \
    -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' \
    -derivedDataPath build \
    CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="" \
    build 2>&1 | tee "$log"
  local status=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
  echo "::endgroup::"
  return $status
}

report_errors() {
  local log="$1"
  {
    echo "## Build failed"
    echo ""
    echo "Copy the lines below and paste them to Claude. They are the first errors:"
    echo ""
    echo '```'
    grep -E "error:|error: |fatal error|Resolve Package|could not resolve" "$log" | sort -u | head -40
    echo '```'
  } >> "$SUMMARY"
}

MODE=full
if ! build project.yml build-full.log; then
  echo "::warning::Full build failed - retrying without RAR/7z support (lite build)"
  MODE=lite
  if ! build project.lite.yml build-lite.log; then
    report_errors build-lite.log
    echo "mode=failed" >> "${GITHUB_OUTPUT:-/dev/null}"
    exit 1
  fi
fi

APP="$(find build/Build/Products/Release-iphoneos -maxdepth 1 -name '*.app' | head -1)"
if [ -z "$APP" ]; then
  echo "Could not find the built .app" >&2
  exit 1
fi

# iPhone Duo (iOS 27) lays out apps linked against an older SDK with a blank strip on the right.
# Stamping the binary as linked against SDK 27.1 turns on the native full-screen layout (same trick as iOXcode).
BIN="$APP/PanelSnap"
MINOS=$(xcrun vtool -show-build "$BIN" 2>/dev/null | awk '/minos/{print $2; exit}')
if xcrun vtool -set-build-version ios "${MINOS:-17.0}" 27.1 -replace -output "$BIN.new" "$BIN"; then
  mv "$BIN.new" "$BIN" && chmod +x "$BIN"
  echo "Stamped SDK 27.1:"; xcrun vtool -show-build "$BIN" | grep -E "platform|minos|sdk" || true
else
  echo "::warning::Could not stamp the SDK version (layout may show a strip on iPhone Duo)"
fi

# Also stamp the Info.plist the same way (iOXcode does both): the Duo layout check looks at the SDK the app claims.
PL="$APP/Info.plist"
for kv in "DTPlatformVersion:27.1" "DTSDKName:iphoneos27.1"; do
  k="${kv%%:*}"; v="${kv#*:}"
  /usr/libexec/PlistBuddy -c "Set :$k $v" "$PL" 2>/dev/null || /usr/libexec/PlistBuddy -c "Add :$k string $v" "$PL" || true
done
/usr/libexec/PlistBuddy -c "Print :DTSDKName" "$PL" || true
/usr/libexec/PlistBuddy -c "Print :UIFileSharingEnabled" "$PL" || true
/usr/libexec/PlistBuddy -c "Print :UILaunchScreen" "$PL" || true

rm -rf Payload PanelSnap.ipa
mkdir Payload
cp -R "$APP" Payload/
zip -qry PanelSnap.ipa Payload
ls -lh PanelSnap.ipa

echo "mode=$MODE" >> "${GITHUB_OUTPUT:-/dev/null}"

===== Scripts/build_adblock.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Builds the browser's ad-block lists (WebKit content-blocker JSON) from EasyList, which is published on GitHub
under the GPLv3 / CC BY-SA 3.0. Output: PanelSnap/Resources/shield-*.json. Re-run any time to refresh the lists:

    python3 Scripts/build_adblock.py

Only conservative rules are converted (domain blocks, plain URL fragments and simple element hiding), so a bad
rule can never take a page or a download down with it.
"""
import json, os, re, sys, urllib.request

BASE = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/easylist/easylist/master/"
SOURCES = [
    "easylist/easylist_adservers.txt",
    "easylist/easylist_thirdparty.txt",
    "easylist/easylist_general_block.txt",
    "easylist/easylist_general_hide.txt",
    "easyprivacy/easyprivacy_trackingservers.txt",
    "easyprivacy/easyprivacy_trackingservers_international.txt",
    "easyprivacy/easyprivacy_general.txt",
    "easyprivacy/easyprivacy_thirdparty.txt",
]
OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "..", "PanelSnap", "Resources")
PER_LIST = 9000

TYPE = {"script": "script", "image": "image", "stylesheet": "style-sheet", "xmlhttprequest": "raw",
        "subdocument": "document", "media": "media", "font": "font", "popup": "popup", "ping": "ping", "other": "other",
        "object": "media"}

def fetch(path):
    with urllib.request.urlopen(BASE + path, timeout=60) as r:
        return r.read().decode("utf-8", "ignore").splitlines()

DOMAIN = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?(\.[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?)+$")

def esc(s):
    return re.sub(r"([.+?(){}\[\]\\^$|*])", r"\\\1", s)

def convert_net(line):
    """One EasyList network rule -> a content-blocker rule, or None if it is not safe/simple."""
    if line.startswith("@@") or "$" in line and line.count("$") > 1:
        return None
    opts = []
    if "$" in line:
        line, o = line.rsplit("$", 1)
        opts = o.split(",")
    third = False
    types = []
    for o in opts:
        if o == "third-party":
            third = True
        elif o in TYPE:
            types.append(TYPE[o])
        else:
            return None                   # negations, domain=, csp, redirect ... are skipped on purpose
    if not line or line.startswith("/") and line.endswith("/"):
        return None                       # regex rules
    m = re.match(r"^\|\|([^/^*|?:]+)(\^|/|$)(.*)$", line)
    if m:
        dom, sep, rest = m.groups()
        dom = dom.lower()
        if not DOMAIN.match(dom):
            return None
        pat = r"^[^:]+://+([^:/]+\.)?" + esc(dom)
        if sep == "/":
            tail = "/" + rest
            if re.search(r"[*^|]", tail) or len(tail) < 4:
                return None
            pat += r"(:[0-9]+)?" + esc(tail)
        elif rest:
            return None
        else:
            pat += r"[/:]"
    else:
        frag = line.lstrip("|")
        if len(frag) < 6 or re.search(r"[*^|]", frag) or not re.match(r"^[\x21-\x7e]+$", frag):
            return None
        pat = esc(frag)
    trig = {"url-filter": pat}
    if third:
        trig["load-type"] = ["third-party"]
    if types:
        trig["resource-type"] = sorted(set(types))
    return {"trigger": trig, "action": {"type": "block"}}

HIDE = re.compile(r"^[#.][A-Za-z0-9_-]+(\[[A-Za-z-]+([\^$*]?=\"[A-Za-z0-9_ ./:-]*\")?\])?$")

def convert_hide(line):
    if not line.startswith("##"):
        return None
    sel = line[2:]
    return sel if HIDE.match(sel) else None

def main():
    net, seen, hide = [], set(), []
    for src in SOURCES:
        try:
            lines = fetch(src)
        except Exception as e:
            print("skip", src, e, file=sys.stderr)
            continue
        n0, h0 = len(net), len(hide)
        for ln in lines:
            ln = ln.strip()
            if not ln or ln.startswith("!") or ln.startswith("["):
                continue
            if "##" in ln or "#@#" in ln or "#?#" in ln or "#$#" in ln:
                s = convert_hide(ln)
                if s and s not in seen:
                    seen.add(s)
                    hide.append(s)
                continue
            r = convert_net(ln)
            if r:
                key = json.dumps(r, sort_keys=True)
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key)
                    net.append(r)
        print(f"{src}: +{len(net)-n0} blocks, +{len(hide)-h0} hides")

    # Hand-written rules that matter most for comic download sites: pop-ups and pop-unders.
    extra = [
        {"trigger": {"url-filter": ".*", "resource-type": ["popup"]}, "action": {"type": "block"}},
        *[{"trigger": {"url-filter": pat, "resource-type": ["script", "document", "popup"], "load-type": ["third-party"]},
           "action": {"type": "block"}} for pat in (r"[/.-]popunders?[/.-]", r"[/.-]popups?[/.-]", r"[/.-]clickunder[/.-]")],
        *[{"trigger": {"url-filter": pat, "resource-type": ["script"], "load-type": ["third-party"]},
           "action": {"type": "block"}} for pat in (r"/adserver", r"/ads/[^/]*\.js", r"/banners?/[^/]*\.js")],
    ]
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    for f in os.listdir(OUT):
        if f.startswith("shield-"):
            os.remove(os.path.join(OUT, f))
    allnet = extra + net
    for i in range(0, len(allnet), PER_LIST):
        chunk = allnet[i:i + PER_LIST]
        with open(os.path.join(OUT, f"shield-net-{i // PER_LIST + 1}.json"), "w") as f:
            json.dump(chunk, f, separators=(",", ":"))
    rules = []
    for i in range(0, len(hide), 25):
        rules.append({"trigger": {"url-filter": ".*"},
                      "action": {"type": "css-display-none", "selector": ", ".join(hide[i:i + 25])}})
    with open(os.path.join(OUT, "shield-hide.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(rules, f, separators=(",", ":"))
    print("network rules:", len(allnet), " element-hiding selectors:", len(hide))

main()

===== Scripts/build_glossary.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Builds PanelSnap/Resources/Characters.json: the built-in "who's who" the Companion uses with no internet.

Sources (both open, no API keys):
  * akabab/superhero-api  (MIT)         - bios, aliases, affiliations, relatives, portraits
  * FiveThirtyEight comic-characters (CC BY 4.0) - Marvel/DC wikia character lists with appearance counts

Usage: build_glossary.py <superhero-api all.json> <marvel-wikia-data.csv> <dc-wikia-data.csv> <out.json>
"""
import csv, json, re, sys

sh_path, marvel_csv, dc_csv, out_path = sys.argv[1:5]

def norm(s):
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", s.lower())

def trim_list(text, limit):
    text = (text or "").strip()
    if text in ("", "-", "None", "null"):
        return ""
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    if len(text) <= limit:
        return text
    cut = text[:limit]
    m = max(cut.rfind(","), cut.rfind(";"))
    return (cut[:m] if m > limit // 2 else cut).rstrip(" ,;") + "…"

PUBLISHERS = {"Marvel Comics": "Marvel", "DC Comics": "DC", "Dark Horse Comics": "Dark Horse",
              "Image Comics": "Image", "IDW Publishing": "IDW", "Wildstorm": "WildStorm",
              "Icon Comics": "Icon", "Shueisha": "Shueisha"}

entries = {}   # key: normalized hero name

def parse_fte(path, pub, host):
    rows = []
    with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            name = r["name"].replace("\\", "").strip()
            try:
                ap = int(r["APPEARANCES"]) if r["APPEARANCES"] else 0
            except ValueError:
                ap = 0
            m = re.match(r"^(.*?)\s*\((.*)\)\s*$", name)
            hero, real = (m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip()) if m else (name, "")
            if real.lower().startswith("earth-") or "earth-" in real.lower():
                real = ""
            slug = r["urlslug"].replace("\\/", "/").replace("\\", "")
            if not slug.startswith("/wiki/"):
                slug = "/wiki" + slug
            year = int(r["Year"]) if r.get("Year") and r["Year"].isdigit() else 0
            rows.append(dict(hero=hero, real=real, ap=ap, slug=slug, year=year, first=r["FIRST APPEARANCE"], pub=pub, host=host))
    rows.sort(key=lambda x: -x["ap"])
    return rows

fte_rows = parse_fte(marvel_csv, "Marvel", "marvel.fandom.com") + parse_fte(dc_csv, "DC", "dc.fandom.com")

# keep the well-known ones (enough appearances), at most one per hero name
for r in sorted(fte_rows, key=lambda x: -x["ap"]):
    if r["ap"] < 45:
        continue
    k = norm(r["hero"])
    if not k or k in entries:
        continue
    entries[k] = {
        "n": r["hero"], "r": r["real"], "p": r["pub"], "ap": r["ap"], "y": r["year"],
        "w": "https://%s%s" % (r["host"], r["slug"]),
    }

# superhero-api: richer bios, merged by hero name or real name
sh = json.load(open(sh_path, encoding="utf-8"))
by_real = {norm(e["r"]): k for k, e in entries.items() if e["r"]}
for x in sh:
    bio, conn, work, img = x["biography"], x["connections"], x["work"], x["images"]
    pub = PUBLISHERS.get(bio.get("publisher") or "", None)
    k = norm(x["name"])
    full = bio.get("fullName") or ""
    fk = norm(full) if full not in ("", "-") else ""
    target = None
    if k in entries:
        target = k
    elif fk and fk in by_real:
        target = by_real[fk]
    elif fk and fk in entries:
        target = fk                       # the wikia lists the hero under the civilian name
    if target is None:
        if pub is None:
            continue                      # TV/film/other universes: not comics
        target = k
        entries[target] = {"n": x["name"], "r": "" if not fk else full, "p": pub, "ap": 0, "y": 0, "w": ""}
    e = entries[target]
    if norm(e["n"]) == fk and norm(x["name"]) != fk:
        e["r"] = e["n"]                   # e.g. "Wanda Maximoff" is really Scarlet Witch
        e["n"] = x["name"]
    if not e.get("r") and fk:
        e["r"] = full
    aliases = [a for a in bio.get("aliases", []) if a and a != "-" and norm(a) != norm(e["n"])][:5]
    if aliases:
        e["al"] = aliases
    fa = (bio.get("firstAppearance") or "").strip()
    if fa and fa != "-":
        e["fa"] = fa
    g = trim_list(conn.get("groupAffiliation"), 150)
    if g: e["g"] = g
    rel = trim_list(conn.get("relatives"), 170)
    if rel: e["rel"] = rel
    occ = trim_list(work.get("occupation"), 80)
    if occ: e["occ"] = occ
    e["img"] = img.get("md", "")

out = sorted(entries.values(), key=lambda e: (-e.get("ap", 0), e["n"]))
json.dump(out, open(out_path, "w", encoding="utf-8"), ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"))
print(len(out), "characters ->", out_path)

===== Scripts/ci-check.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Compiles the app for the iOS Simulator (full build, then the lite one if the RAR/7z packages fail) and writes
# a short report to report/ that anyone (including regular Claude, via GitHub) can read.
set -uo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
mkdir -p report

build() {
  local spec="$1" log="$2"
  rm -rf build-sim PanelSnap.xcodeproj
  xcodegen generate --spec "$spec" || return 1
  xcodebuild \
    -project PanelSnap.xcodeproj -scheme PanelSnap -configuration Debug \
    -sdk iphonesimulator -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' \
    -derivedDataPath build-sim \
    CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="" \
    build 2>&1 | tee "$log"
  return "${PIPESTATUS[0]}"
}

MODE=full RESULT=passed
if ! build project.yml report/build-full.log; then
  MODE=lite
  if ! build project.lite.yml report/build-lite.log; then RESULT=FAILED; fi
fi

LOG=report/build-$MODE.log
grep -E "error:|fatal error|could not resolve" "$LOG" | sort -u | head -60 > report/errors.txt || true
grep -E "warning:" "$LOG" | sed 's#^.*/PanelSnap/#PanelSnap/#' | sort -u | head -60 > report/warnings.txt || true
# keep the committed log small: only the tail
for f in report/build-*.log; do [ -f "$f" ] && tail -n 400 "$f" > "$f.tmp" && mv "$f.tmp" "$f"; done

{
  echo "# Simulator build: $RESULT"
  echo ""
  echo "- Branch: \`${GITHUB_HEAD_REF:-${GITHUB_REF_NAME:-local}}\`"
  echo "- Commit: \`${GITHUB_SHA:-unknown}\`"
  echo "- Build type: $MODE (lite = without the RAR/7z libraries)"
  echo "- Errors: $(grep -c . report/errors.txt)   Warnings: $(grep -c . report/warnings.txt)"
  echo ""
  if [ -s report/errors.txt ]; then echo "## Errors"; echo '```'; cat report/errors.txt; echo '```'; fi
  if [ -s report/warnings.txt ]; then echo "## Warnings"; echo '```'; cat report/warnings.txt; echo '```'; fi
} > report/status.md

cat report/status.md >> "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-/dev/null}"
[ "$RESULT" = passed ]

===== Scripts/make-source-bundle.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Writes the app's source as a few plain-text files, so a chat that can only read GitHub files can see it all at once.
#   report/source/reader.txt : the reader and its gestures (the part that changes most)
#   report/source/all.txt    : every Swift file, the project spec, scripts and docs
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
OUT=report/source
mkdir -p "$OUT"

bundle() {
  local dest="$1"; shift
  : > "$dest"
  for f in "$@"; do
    [ -f "$f" ] || continue
    printf '\n===== %s =====\n' "$f" >> "$dest"
    cat "$f" >> "$dest"
  done
}

bundle "$OUT/reader.txt" ARCHITECTURE.md \
  PanelSnap/Views/ReaderView.swift PanelSnap/Views/ZoomLayer.swift PanelSnap/Views/PanelCanvas.swift \
  PanelSnap/Services/ReaderModel.swift PanelSnap/Services/PanelDetector.swift PanelSnap/Services/PanelCache.swift

# shellcheck disable=SC2046
bundle "$OUT/all.txt" ARCHITECTURE.md README.md project.yml project.lite.yml \
  $(git ls-files 'PanelSnap/*.swift' 'Scripts/*.sh' 'Scripts/*.py' 'Scripts/PanelCheck/*.swift' '.github/workflows/*.yml')
ls -l "$OUT"

===== Scripts/make_icon.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regenerates the PanelSnap app icon: red manga speed lines, one tilted comic book page made of abstract, brightly coloured panels.
Needs Pillow: pip install pillow"""
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import math, os

OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "PanelSnap", "Resources", "Assets.xcassets", "AppIcon.appiconset", "AppIcon.png")
S = 2048
INK = (14, 10, 16)
RED = (226, 28, 36)
SOFT_RED = (206, 20, 30)      # speed lines: only a hint darker than the background
YELLOW = (255, 208, 20)
CYAN, PINK, LIME, VIOLET = (0, 190, 255), (255, 40, 150), (120, 220, 40), (110, 70, 255)   # one bright colour per panel

img = Image.new("RGB", (S, S), RED)
d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
cx, cy = S // 2, S // 2

n = 48
for i in range(n):
    a0 = 2 * math.pi * i / n
    a1 = a0 + math.pi / n
    R = S * 1.2
    d.polygon([(cx, cy), (cx + R * math.cos(a0), cy + R * math.sin(a0)), (cx + R * math.cos(a1), cy + R * math.sin(a1))], fill=SOFT_RED)

layer = Image.new("RGBA", (S, S), (0, 0, 0, 0))
ld = ImageDraw.Draw(layer)
# A tall comic-book page with its panel layout, each panel a different bright colour.
BW, BH = 800, 1120
box = (cx - BW // 2, cy - BH // 2, cx + BW // 2, cy + BH // 2)
off = 44
ld.rounded_rectangle((box[0] + off, box[1] + off, box[2] + off, box[3] + off), radius=36, fill=INK)     # shadow
ld.rounded_rectangle(box, radius=36, fill=YELLOW)
m, gap = 104, 36
x0, y0, x1, y1 = box[0] + m, box[1] + m, box[2] - m, box[3] - m
h1 = int((y1 - y0) * 0.42)
h2 = int((y1 - y0) * 0.26)
midx = (x0 + x1) // 2
ld.rectangle((x0, y0, x1, y0 + h1), fill=CYAN, outline=INK, width=26)                                                   # wide panel
ld.rectangle((x0, y0 + h1 + gap, midx - gap // 2, y0 + h1 + gap + h2), fill=PINK, outline=INK, width=26)                # two small
ld.rectangle((midx + gap // 2, y0 + h1 + gap, x1, y0 + h1 + gap + h2), fill=LIME, outline=INK, width=26)
ld.rectangle((x0, y0 + h1 + h2 + 2 * gap, x1, y1), fill=VIOLET, outline=INK, width=26)                                    # wide bottom

layer = layer.rotate(-7, resample=Image.BICUBIC, center=(cx, cy))
img.paste(layer, (0, 0), layer)

img.resize((1024, 1024), Image.LANCZOS).save(OUT)
print("wrote", OUT)
